var array<float> FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS = array.from(1.236, 1.382, 1.500, 1.618, 2.000)

f_price(float value) =>
    na(value) ? "-" : str.tostring(value, format.mintick)


f_htf_state_caption(int rawState) =>
    rawState == 9 ? "-" : rawState > 0 ? "صاعد" : rawState < 0 ? "هابط" : "محايد"

f_market_tick(float value) =>
    syminfo.prefix == "TADAWUL" and (syminfo.type == "stock" or syminfo.type == "fund" or syminfo.type == "dr") ? value < 25 ? 0.01 : value < 50 ? 0.02 : value < 100 ? 0.05 : value < 250 ? 0.10 : value < 500 ? 0.20 : 0.50 : syminfo.mintick

f_floor_to_tick(float value) =>
    float first = na(value) ? na : math.floor(value / f_market_tick(value) + 1e-10) * f_market_tick(value)
    na(first) ? na : math.floor(value / f_market_tick(first) + 1e-10) * f_market_tick(first)

f_ceil_to_tick(float value) =>
    float first = na(value) ? na : math.ceil(value / f_market_tick(value) - 1e-10) * f_market_tick(value)
    na(first) ? na : math.ceil(value / f_market_tick(first) - 1e-10) * f_market_tick(first)

f_next_fib_extension_level(bool isLong, float anchorLevel, float swingDistance, float entryPrice, float requiredDistance, float maximumDistance) =>
    float selectedLevel = na
    for ratioIndex = 0 to array.size(FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS) - 1
        float extensionRatio = array.get(FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS, ratioIndex)
        float extensionOffset = swingDistance * math.max(extensionRatio - 1.0, 0.0)
        float extensionLevel = isLong ? anchorLevel + extensionOffset : anchorLevel - extensionOffset
        float distanceFromEntry = isLong ? extensionLevel - entryPrice : entryPrice - extensionLevel
        if na(selectedLevel) and (distanceFromEntry >= requiredDistance and distanceFromEntry <= maximumDistance)
            selectedLevel := extensionLevel
    selectedLevel

f_build_fib_extension_targets(bool isLong, float anchorLevel, float swingDistance, float entryPrice, float minimumDistance1, float minimumDistance2, float minimumDistance3, float maximumDistance1, float maximumDistance2, float maximumDistance3, float minimumGap) =>
    float selectedLevel1 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, minimumDistance1, maximumDistance1)
    float selectedDistance1 = na(selectedLevel1) ? minimumDistance1 : isLong ? selectedLevel1 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel1
    float boundedDistance1 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance1, minimumDistance1), maximumDistance1)
    float requiredDistance2 = math.max(minimumDistance2, boundedDistance1 + minimumGap)
    float selectedLevel2 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, requiredDistance2, maximumDistance2)
    float selectedDistance2 = na(selectedLevel2) ? requiredDistance2 : isLong ? selectedLevel2 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel2
    float boundedDistance2 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance2, requiredDistance2), maximumDistance2)
    float requiredDistance3 = math.max(minimumDistance3, boundedDistance2 + minimumGap)
    float selectedLevel3 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, requiredDistance3, maximumDistance3)
    float selectedDistance3 = na(selectedLevel3) ? requiredDistance3 : isLong ? selectedLevel3 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel3
    float boundedDistance3 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance3, requiredDistance3), maximumDistance3)
    [(isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance1 : entryPrice - boundedDistance1), (isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance2 : entryPrice - boundedDistance2), (isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance3 : entryPrice - boundedDistance3), selectedLevel1, selectedLevel2, selectedLevel3]


f_zone_origin(bool enabled, bool demandSide, int lookbackBars, float maximumBaseBodyRatio) =>
    bool originReady = false
    int farthestOffset = na
    int baseCandleCount = 0
    float proximalLine = na
    float distalLine = na
    if enabled
        for offset = 1 to lookbackBars
            float originCandleRange = high[offset] - low[offset]
            bool candleReady = not na(originCandleRange) and originCandleRange > syminfo.mintick * 0.50
            float candleBodyRatio = candleReady ? math.abs(close[offset] - open[offset]) / originCandleRange : 1.0
            bool baseCandle = candleReady and candleBodyRatio <= maximumBaseBodyRatio
            bool oppositeCandle = candleReady and (demandSide ? close[offset] < open[offset] : close[offset] > open[offset])
            bool firstOriginCandle = not originReady and (baseCandle or oppositeCandle)
            bool adjacentOlderBase = originReady and offset == farthestOffset + 1 and baseCandle and baseCandleCount < 6
            if firstOriginCandle or adjacentOlderBase
                originReady := true
                farthestOffset := offset
                baseCandleCount += baseCandle ? 1 : 0
                float bodyHigh = math.max(open[offset], close[offset])
                float bodyLow = math.min(open[offset], close[offset])
                proximalLine := na(proximalLine) ? (demandSide ? bodyHigh : bodyLow) : demandSide ? math.max(proximalLine, bodyHigh) : math.min(proximalLine, bodyLow)
                distalLine := na(distalLine) ? (demandSide ? low[offset] : high[offset]) : demandSide ? math.min(distalLine, low[offset]) : math.max(distalLine, high[offset])
            else if originReady
                break
    int incomingOffset = not na(farthestOffset) ? farthestOffset + 1 : na
    bool incomingAvailable = not na(incomingOffset) and incomingOffset <= lookbackBars
    bool incomingDown = incomingAvailable and close[incomingOffset] < open[incomingOffset]
    bool incomingUp = incomingAvailable and close[incomingOffset] > open[incomingOffset]
    bool incomingDirectional = incomingDown or incomingUp
    int patternCode = not incomingDirectional ? 0 : demandSide ? (incomingDown ? 1 : 2) : (incomingUp ? -1 : -2)
    [originReady, farthestOffset, proximalLine, distalLine, baseCandleCount, patternCode]

f_zone_geometry_valid(float topLevel, float bottomLevel, float atrValue) =>
    float zoneWidth = topLevel - bottomLevel
    float zoneMidpoint = (topLevel + bottomLevel) * 0.50
    float atrWidthLimit = math.max(atrValue * (timeframe.isintraday ? 1.50 : 2.00), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float percentWidthLimit = math.max(zoneMidpoint * (timeframe.isintraday ? 0.04 : 0.08), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    not na(topLevel) and not na(bottomLevel) and not na(atrValue) and bottomLevel > 0 and zoneWidth >= syminfo.mintick and zoneWidth <= atrWidthLimit and zoneWidth <= percentWidthLimit

f_stop_atr_limits(float seconds) =>
    float minimumMultiple = seconds <= 5 * 60 ? 0.60 : seconds <= 30 * 60 ? 0.70 : seconds <= 60 * 60 ? 0.80 : seconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 0.90 : 1.10
    float maximumMultiple = seconds <= 5 * 60 ? 2.50 : seconds <= 30 * 60 ? 3.00 : seconds <= 60 * 60 ? 3.50 : seconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 4.00 : 5.00
    [minimumMultiple, maximumMultiple]


f_clear_line(line objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        line.delete(objectId)
    line clearedObject = na
    clearedObject

f_clear_label(label objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        label.delete(objectId)
    label clearedObject = na
    clearedObject

f_clear_label_array(array<label> ids) =>
    while array.size(ids) > 0
        label id = array.pop(ids)
        if not na(id)
            label.delete(id)
    true

f_safe_past_bar(int requestedBar) =>
    math.max(bar_index - 4999, math.max(0, nz(requestedBar, bar_index)))

f_safe_future_bar(int requestedBar, int firstBar) =>
    math.min(bar_index + 499, math.max(firstBar + 1, nz(requestedBar, bar_index + 1)))


f_zone_state_valid(float topLevel, float bottomLevel, float creationAtr, float creationClose) =>
    float mid = (topLevel + bottomLevel) * 0.50
    float atr0 = math.max(nz(creationAtr, 0.0), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float limit = math.max(atr0 * (timeframe.isintraday ? 4.0 : 6.0), nz(creationClose, mid) * (timeframe.isintraday ? 0.08 : 0.12))
    f_zone_geometry_valid(topLevel, bottomLevel, atr0) and not na(creationClose) and creationClose > 0 and math.abs(mid - creationClose) <= limit

f_new_plan_line(float level, color lineColor, int transparency, string lineStyle) =>
    line.new(bar_index, level, bar_index + 1, level, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(lineColor, transparency), style = lineStyle, width = lineStyle == line.style_solid ? 2 : 1, force_overlay = true)

f_replace_plan_line(line currentId, bool enabled, float level, color lineColor, int transparency, string lineStyle) =>
    f_clear_line(currentId)
    enabled ? f_new_plan_line(level, lineColor, transparency, lineStyle) : na

f_nearest_price_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float candidate1, float candidate2, float candidate3, float candidate4, float candidate5, float candidate6, float candidate7, float candidate8, float candidate9, float candidate10) =>
    array<float> candidateLevels = array.from(candidate1, candidate2, candidate3, candidate4, candidate5, candidate6, candidate7, candidate8, candidate9, candidate10)
    float nearestLevel = na
    for candidateIndex = 0 to array.size(candidateLevels) - 1
        float candidateLevel = array.get(candidateLevels, candidateIndex)
        bool candidateValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
        bool candidateNearer = candidateValid and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove and candidateLevel < nearestLevel) or (not searchAbove and candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
        if candidateNearer
            nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel

f_target_level_array(float c0, float c1, float c2, float c3, float c4, float c5, float c6, float c7, float c8, float c9, float c10) =>
    array<float> levels = array.from(c0, c1, c2, c3, c4, c5, c6, c7, c8, c9, c10)
    levels

f_ranked_array_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, array<float> levels) =>
    float selected = na
    int selectedRank = 100
    int count = array.size(levels)
    if count > 0
        for index = 0 to count - 1
            float level = array.get(levels, index)
            int rank = index < 4 ? 1 : index < 5 ? 2 : index < 7 ? 5 : 6
            float distance = not na(level) ? math.abs(level - currentPrice) : na
            bool valid = not na(level) and (searchAbove ? level >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : level <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or maximumDistance <= 0 or distance <= maximumDistance)
            bool nearer = valid and (na(selected) or (searchAbove ? level < selected : level > selected))
            if valid and (rank < selectedRank or rank == selectedRank and nearer)
                selected := level
                selectedRank := rank
    selected

f_nearest_stored_pivot(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, int maximumAgeMilliseconds) =>
    float nearestLevel = na
    int count = math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), array.size(pivotTimes))
    if count > 0
        for index = 0 to count - 1
            float candidateLevel = array.get(prices, index)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, index)
            int candidateTime = array.get(pivotTimes, index)
            float candidateDistance = not na(candidateLevel) ? math.abs(candidateLevel - currentPrice) : na
            bool positionValid = not na(candidateBar) and candidateBar >= 0 and candidateBar <= bar_index
            bool ageValid = positionValid and not na(candidateTime) and time >= candidateTime and (maximumAgeMilliseconds <= 0 or time - candidateTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds)
            bool sideValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
            bool distanceValid = sideValid and (na(maximumDistance) or maximumDistance <= 0 or candidateDistance <= maximumDistance)
            if ageValid and distanceValid and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nearestLevel : candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
                nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel

// مستوى تاريخي صالح للكسر: محور قديم ومؤكد ولم يسبق إغلاق السعر خلفه.
// يمنع هذا اعتبار كل Pivot محلي أو إعادة كسر مستوى مستهلك حدثًا تاريخيًا جديدًا.
f_nearest_unbroken_historical_pivot(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, array<bool> brokenStates, int minimumAgeBars, int maximumAgeMilliseconds) =>
    float nearestLevel = na
    int count = math.min(math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), array.size(pivotTimes)), array.size(brokenStates))
    if count > 0
        for index = 0 to count - 1
            float candidateLevel = array.get(prices, index)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, index)
            int candidateTime = array.get(pivotTimes, index)
            bool candidateBroken = array.get(brokenStates, index)
            int candidateAgeBars = not na(candidateBar) ? bar_index - candidateBar : 0
            bool ageValid = not na(candidateBar) and candidateBar >= 0 and candidateBar <= bar_index and not na(candidateTime) and candidateAgeBars >= minimumAgeBars and time >= candidateTime and (maximumAgeMilliseconds <= 0 or time - candidateTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds)
            bool sideValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
            if ageValid and sideValid and not candidateBroken and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nearestLevel : candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
                nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel


f_confirmed_level_break(bool bullishSide, float levelNow, float levelPrevious, float bufferNow, bool exceptionalBar) =>
    float priorLevel = nz(levelPrevious, levelNow)
    float priorBuffer = nz(bufferNow[1], bufferNow)
    bool currentBeyond = not na(levelNow) and (bullishSide ? close > levelNow + bufferNow : close < levelNow - bufferNow)
    bool previousBeyond = not na(priorLevel) and (bullishSide ? close[1] > priorLevel + priorBuffer : close[1] < priorLevel - priorBuffer)
    barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and currentBeyond and (previousBeyond or exceptionalBar)

// One-bar pulse for signal creation. Persistent break state is kept above for
// invalidation/expiry checks, while this function prevents repeated opportunities.
f_level_break_event(bool bullishSide, float levelNow, float levelPrevious, float bufferNow, bool exceptionalBar) =>
    float priorLevel = nz(levelPrevious, levelNow)
    float priorPriorLevel = nz(levelPrevious[1], priorLevel)
    float priorBuffer = nz(bufferNow[1], bufferNow)
    float priorPriorBuffer = nz(bufferNow[2], priorBuffer)
    bool currentBeyond = not na(levelNow) and (bullishSide ? close > levelNow + bufferNow : close < levelNow - bufferNow)
    bool previousBeyond = not na(priorLevel) and (bullishSide ? close[1] > priorLevel + priorBuffer : close[1] < priorLevel - priorBuffer)
    bool priorPriorBeyond = not na(priorPriorLevel) and (bullishSide ? close[2] > priorPriorLevel + priorPriorBuffer : close[2] < priorPriorLevel - priorPriorBuffer)
    bool secondClosePulse = currentBeyond and previousBeyond and not priorPriorBeyond and not exceptionalBar[1]
    bool exceptionalFirstClosePulse = currentBeyond and exceptionalBar and not previousBeyond
    barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and (secondClosePulse or exceptionalFirstClosePulse)

f_level_break_attempt(bool bullishSide, float levelNow, float levelPrevious, float bufferNow) =>
    float priorLevel = nz(levelPrevious, levelNow)
    float priorBuffer = nz(bufferNow[1], bufferNow)
    bool currentBeyond = not na(levelNow) and (bullishSide ? close > levelNow + bufferNow : close < levelNow - bufferNow)
    bool previousBeyond = not na(priorLevel) and (bullishSide ? close[1] > priorLevel + priorBuffer : close[1] < priorLevel - priorBuffer)
    barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and currentBeyond and not previousBeyond

var array<float> pendingBreakLevels = array.new_float(18, na)
var array<float> pendingBreakEdges = array.new_float(18, na)
var array<float> pendingBreakBuffers = array.new_float(18, na)
var array<int> pendingBreakBars = array.new_int(18, na)

f_clear_pending_break(int slot) =>
    array.set(pendingBreakLevels, slot, na)
    array.set(pendingBreakEdges, slot, na)
    array.set(pendingBreakBuffers, slot, na)
    array.set(pendingBreakBars, slot, na)

f_static_break_state(int slot, bool bullishSide, float candidateLevel, float candidateEdge, float bufferNow, bool exceptionalBar) =>
    float pendingLevel = array.get(pendingBreakLevels, slot)
    float pendingEdge = array.get(pendingBreakEdges, slot)
    float pendingBuffer = array.get(pendingBreakBuffers, slot)
    int pendingBar = array.get(pendingBreakBars, slot)
    // Freeze the threshold together with the level. ATR or tick changes between
    // the first and second close must not relax or tighten the same attempt.
    float candidateBuffer = not na(bufferNow) ? math.max(bufferNow, 0.0) : na
    float confirmationBuffer = not na(pendingBuffer) ? math.max(pendingBuffer, 0.0) : candidateBuffer
    bool candidateReady = not na(candidateLevel) and not na(candidateBuffer)
    bool currentBeyondCandidate = candidateReady and (bullishSide ? close > candidateLevel + candidateBuffer : close < candidateLevel - candidateBuffer)
    bool previousInsideCandidate = candidateReady and (bullishSide ? close[1] <= candidateLevel + candidateBuffer : close[1] >= candidateLevel - candidateBuffer)
    bool firstAttempt = barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and currentBeyondCandidate and previousInsideCandidate
    bool pendingReady = not na(pendingLevel) and not na(pendingBar) and not na(confirmationBuffer)
    bool currentBeyondPending = pendingReady and (bullishSide ? close > pendingLevel + confirmationBuffer : close < pendingLevel - confirmationBuffer)
    bool previousBeyondPending = pendingReady and (bullishSide ? close[1] > pendingLevel + confirmationBuffer : close[1] < pendingLevel - confirmationBuffer)
    bool secondClose = barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and pendingReady and bar_index == pendingBar + 1 and currentBeyondPending and previousBeyondPending
    bool exceptionalClose = firstAttempt and exceptionalBar
    bool confirmed = secondClose or exceptionalClose
    float confirmedLevel = confirmed ? (secondClose ? pendingLevel : candidateLevel) : na
    float confirmedEdge = confirmed ? (secondClose ? nz(pendingEdge, pendingLevel) : nz(candidateEdge, candidateLevel)) : na
    bool pendingDone = confirmed or pendingReady and bar_index >= pendingBar + 1 and not secondClose
    if pendingDone
        f_clear_pending_break(slot)
    if firstAttempt and not confirmed
        array.set(pendingBreakLevels, slot, candidateLevel)
        array.set(pendingBreakEdges, slot, nz(candidateEdge, candidateLevel))
        array.set(pendingBreakBuffers, slot, candidateBuffer)
        array.set(pendingBreakBars, slot, bar_index)
    float confirmedBuffer = confirmed ? (secondClose ? confirmationBuffer : candidateBuffer) : na
    [firstAttempt, confirmed, confirmedLevel, confirmedEdge, exceptionalClose ? 1 : secondClose ? 2 : 0, confirmedBuffer]

if firstAnalysisSessionBar
    for pendingSlot = 0 to 17
        f_clear_pending_break(pendingSlot)

f_price_action_breakouts(bool enabled, int rangeBars, float rangeAtrWidth, float buffer, float atrInputValue, bool volumeOkay, bool volumeIsAvailable, float relativeVolumeValue, float closeLocationValue, bool priorDown, bool priorUp, bool bullBreakQuality, bool bearBreakQuality, bool bullExceptionalBar, bool bearExceptionalBar) =>
    bool insideBar = high < high[1] and low > low[1]
    bool bullInsideSetup = enabled and insideBar and priorDown
    bool bearInsideSetup = enabled and insideBar and priorUp
    float fastRelativeVolume = ta.ema(relativeVolumeValue, 3)
    float slowRelativeVolume = ta.ema(relativeVolumeValue, 8)
    bool relativeVolumeRising = volumeIsAvailable and not na(fastRelativeVolume) and not na(slowRelativeVolume) and fastRelativeVolume > slowRelativeVolume and fastRelativeVolume > fastRelativeVolume[1]
    bool volumeAfterDecline = relativeVolumeRising and priorDown and closeLocationValue >= 0.50
    bool volumeAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult = relativeVolumeRising and priorUp and closeLocationValue <= 0.50
    float rangeHigh = ta.highest(high[1], rangeBars)
    float rangeLow = ta.lowest(low[1], rangeBars)
    float rangeWidth = rangeHigh - rangeLow
    bool rangeReady = enabled and not na(rangeHigh) and not na(rangeLow) and rangeWidth > syminfo.mintick and rangeWidth <= nz(atrInputValue[1], atrInputValue) * rangeAtrWidth
    float bullInsideLevel = bullInsideSetup[1] ? high[2] : na
    float bullInsideEdge = bullInsideSetup[1] ? low[2] : na
    float bearInsideLevel = bearInsideSetup[1] ? low[2] : na
    float bearInsideEdge = bearInsideSetup[1] ? high[2] : na
    [_, bullInsideBreakRaw, bullInsideConfirmedLevel, bullInsideConfirmedEdge, _, _] = f_static_break_state(8, true, bullInsideLevel, bullInsideEdge, buffer, bullExceptionalBar)
    [_, bearInsideBreakRaw, bearInsideConfirmedLevel, bearInsideConfirmedEdge, _, _] = f_static_break_state(9, false, bearInsideLevel, bearInsideEdge, buffer, bearExceptionalBar)
    [_, bullRangeBreakRaw, bullRangeConfirmedLevel, bullRangeConfirmedEdge, _, _] = f_static_break_state(12, true, enabled and rangeReady ? rangeHigh : na, rangeLow, buffer, bullExceptionalBar)
    [_, bearRangeBreakRaw, bearRangeConfirmedLevel, bearRangeConfirmedEdge, _, _] = f_static_break_state(13, false, enabled and rangeReady ? rangeLow : na, rangeHigh, buffer, bearExceptionalBar)
    [insideBar, bullInsideSetup, bearInsideSetup, volumeAfterDecline, volumeAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, rangeHigh, rangeLow, rangeReady, (enabled and bullInsideBreakRaw), (enabled and bearInsideBreakRaw), (enabled and bullRangeBreakRaw), (enabled and bearRangeBreakRaw), (enabled and bullInsideBreakRaw and bullBreakQuality and volumeOkay), (enabled and bearInsideBreakRaw and bearBreakQuality and volumeOkay), (enabled and bullRangeBreakRaw and bullBreakQuality and volumeOkay), (enabled and bearRangeBreakRaw and bearBreakQuality and volumeOkay), bullInsideConfirmedLevel, bullInsideConfirmedEdge, bearInsideConfirmedLevel, bearInsideConfirmedEdge, bullRangeConfirmedLevel, bullRangeConfirmedEdge, bearRangeConfirmedLevel, bearRangeConfirmedEdge]


f_exceptional_direct_break_pass(bool longSide, bool active, bool directBreak, bool qualified, bool participationPass, float bodyRatioValue, float closeLocationValue, int ownAxes, int ownCount, int oppositeCount, int higherScore, bool reversalBreak) =>
    bool closeQuality = longSide ? closeLocationValue >= 0.75 : closeLocationValue <= 0.25
    bool higherAligned = longSide ? higherScore >= 1 or reversalBreak and higherScore >= 0 : higherScore <= -1 or reversalBreak and higherScore <= 0
    active and directBreak and qualified and participationPass and bodyRatioValue >= 0.60 and closeQuality and ownAxes >= 3 and ownCount >= oppositeCount + 3 and higherAligned


f_dealing_range_context(bool enabled, bool retestMode, bool swingCompatible, float lastHigh, float lastLow, int lastHighBar, int lastLowBar, float currentClose) =>
    bool rangeReady = enabled and swingCompatible and not na(lastHigh) and not na(lastLow) and not na(lastHighBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastHigh > lastLow
    bool bullishLeg = rangeReady and lastLowBar < lastHighBar
    bool bearishLeg = rangeReady and lastHighBar < lastLowBar
    float rangeSize = rangeReady ? lastHigh - lastLow : na
    float midpoint = rangeReady ? (lastHigh + lastLow) * 0.50 : na
    bool insideRange = rangeReady and currentClose >= lastLow and currentClose <= lastHigh
    bool evidenceReady = retestMode and insideRange and (bullishLeg or bearishLeg)
    int signal = not evidenceReady ? 0 : bullishLeg and currentClose <= midpoint ? 1 : bearishLeg and currentClose >= midpoint ? -1 : 0
    string stateText = (not (enabled) ? "سياق العلاوة/الخصم معطل" : not (rangeReady) ? "العلاوة/الخصم غير جاهزة" : not (insideRange) ? "السعر خارج النطاق؛ OTE غير مطبق" : (bullishLeg) ? (((bullishLeg and insideRange and currentClose >= (bullishLeg ? lastHigh - rangeSize * 0.79 : na) and currentClose <= (bullishLeg ? lastHigh - rangeSize * 0.62 : na))) ? "ICT: خصم داخل OTE 62%-79% للصعود" : (currentClose) <= (midpoint) ? "ICT: خصم للصعود لكن خارج OTE" : "ICT: علاوة؛ ليست منطقة ارتداد صاعد مفضلة") : (bearishLeg) ? (((bearishLeg and insideRange and currentClose >= (bearishLeg ? lastLow + rangeSize * 0.62 : na) and currentClose <= (bearishLeg ? lastLow + rangeSize * 0.79 : na))) ? "ICT: علاوة داخل OTE 62%-79% للهبوط" : (currentClose) >= (midpoint) ? "ICT: علاوة للهبوط لكن خارج OTE" : "ICT: خصم؛ ليست منطقة ارتداد هابط مفضلة") : "نطاق تعامل انتقالي")
    [evidenceReady, signal, stateText]

f_fib_retracement_context(bool enabled, bool swingCompatible, float lastHigh, float lastLow, int lastHighBar, int lastLowBar) =>
    bool ready = enabled and swingCompatible and not na(lastHigh) and not na(lastLow) and not na(lastHighBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastHigh > lastLow and lastHighBar != lastLowBar
    bool bullLeg = ready and lastLowBar < lastHighBar
    bool bearLeg = ready and lastHighBar < lastLowBar
    float legRange = ready ? lastHigh - lastLow : na
    [bullLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.382 : bearLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.382 : na, bullLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.500 : bearLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.500 : na, bullLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.618 : bearLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.618 : na, bullLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.786 : bearLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.786 : na]

f_true_level_bounce(bool bullishSide, float level, float tolerance, float atrValue, float closeQuality, float bodyQuality) =>
    float safeTolerance = math.max(tolerance, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
    float maximumOvershoot = math.max(atrValue * 0.55, safeTolerance * 2.5)
    bool touched = not na(level) and (bullishSide ? low <= level + safeTolerance and low >= level - maximumOvershoot : high >= level - safeTolerance and high <= level + maximumOvershoot)
    bool reclaimed = bullishSide ? close > level + safeTolerance * 0.10 and close > open and closeQuality >= 0.56 : close < level - safeTolerance * 0.10 and close < open and closeQuality <= 0.44
    barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and touched and reclaimed and bodyQuality >= 0.24


f_channel_signal(bool upperFakeout, bool lowerFakeout, bool channelIsReadySignal, bool breakoutUp, bool breakoutDown, int bias, float currentClose, float channelMiddle, bool trendIsReadySignal, bool supportBroken, bool resistanceBroken, float channelBase) =>
    upperFakeout ? -1 : lowerFakeout ? 1 : channelIsReadySignal ? (breakoutUp ? 1 : breakoutDown ? -1 : bias == 1 and currentClose >= channelMiddle ? 1 : bias == -1 and currentClose <= channelMiddle ? -1 : 0) : trendIsReadySignal ? (supportBroken ? -1 : resistanceBroken ? 1 : bias == 1 and currentClose >= channelBase ? 1 : bias == -1 and currentClose <= channelBase ? -1 : 0) : 0

f_new_chart_marker(int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float atrOffset) =>
    int safeMarkerBar = f_safe_past_bar(markerBar)
    float safeMarkerLevel = na(markerLevel) ? close : markerLevel
    float safeMarkerAtr = na(markerAtr) ? syminfo.mintick * 10.0 : markerAtr
    // تبقى العلامة خارج جسم الشمعة بهامش ATR واضح، مع خلفية أخف حتى لا تحجب السعر.
    float markerOffset = math.max(safeMarkerAtr * math.max(atrOffset, 0.30), syminfo.mintick * 8.0)
    label.new(safeMarkerBar, below ? safeMarkerLevel - markerOffset : safeMarkerLevel + markerOffset, markerText, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = below ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down, color = color.new(markerColor, 38), textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny, force_overlay = true)

f_replace_chart_marker(label currentId, int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float atrOffset) =>
    f_clear_label(currentId)
    f_new_chart_marker(markerBar, markerLevel, markerText, below, markerColor, markerAtr, atrOffset)

f_close_plan_line(line lineId, int endBar, color baseColor, int transparency, int lineWidth) =>
    if not na(lineId)
        line.set_extend(lineId, extend.none)
        line.set_x2(lineId, endBar)
        line.set_color(lineId, color.new(baseColor, transparency))
        line.set_width(lineId, lineWidth)

f_project(float firstPrice, int firstBar, float secondPrice, int secondBar, int targetBar) =>
    bool validPoints = not na(firstPrice) and not na(secondPrice) and not na(firstBar) and not na(secondBar) and secondBar != firstBar
    validPoints ? secondPrice + (secondPrice - firstPrice) / float(secondBar - firstBar) * float(targetBar - secondBar) : na

// A valid channel breakout has a classical measured objective equal to the
// perpendicular channel width. Keep only a fresh, still-unreached objective and
// expire it after a bounded number of chart bars.
f_update_channel_objectives(float currentBullTarget, int currentBullBar, float currentBearTarget, int currentBearBar, bool qualifiedBreakUp, bool qualifiedBreakDown, bool upperFakeout, bool lowerFakeout, bool geometryChanged, float upperBoundary, float lowerBoundary, float measuredWidth, int maximumAge) =>
    float nextBullTarget = currentBullTarget
    int nextBullBar = currentBullBar
    float nextBearTarget = currentBearTarget
    int nextBearBar = currentBearBar
    bool bullExpired = not na(nextBullBar) and bar_index - nextBullBar > maximumAge
    bool bearExpired = not na(nextBearBar) and bar_index - nextBearBar > maximumAge
    if geometryChanged
        nextBullTarget := na
        nextBullBar := na
        nextBearTarget := na
        nextBearBar := na
    if bullExpired or upperFakeout or qualifiedBreakDown or (not na(nextBullTarget) and high >= nextBullTarget)
        nextBullTarget := na
        nextBullBar := na
    if bearExpired or lowerFakeout or qualifiedBreakUp or (not na(nextBearTarget) and low <= nextBearTarget)
        nextBearTarget := na
        nextBearBar := na
    float bullCandidate = not na(upperBoundary) and not na(measuredWidth) ? upperBoundary + measuredWidth : na
    float bearCandidate = not na(lowerBoundary) and not na(measuredWidth) ? lowerBoundary - measuredWidth : na
    if qualifiedBreakUp and not na(bullCandidate) and bullCandidate > high + syminfo.mintick
        nextBullTarget := bullCandidate
        nextBullBar := bar_index
    if qualifiedBreakDown and not na(bearCandidate) and bearCandidate < low - syminfo.mintick and bearCandidate >= syminfo.mintick
        nextBearTarget := bearCandidate
        nextBearBar := bar_index
    [nextBullTarget, nextBullBar, nextBearTarget, nextBearBar]

// Pine resolves user-defined functions in declaration order. Keep this helper
// above the principal channel engine because that engine compares anchor IDs.
f_int_changed(int previousValue, int currentValue) =>
    na(previousValue) != na(currentValue) or (not na(previousValue) and not na(currentValue) and previousValue != currentValue)

// Maintain a second, slower set of confirmed pivots for the principal trend.
// returns only the latest valid principal trend and one parallel boundary, so
// the chart never accumulates a forest of historical lines.
f_update_major_channel(float pivotHighCandidate, float pivotLowCandidate, int candidateBar, int pivotStrength, int maximumValidationBars, float atrSeries, float breakAtrPart, float zoneAtrPart, float maximumProjectionAtr, float maximumSlopeAtr, float minimumWidthConsistency, int dowDirection, float closeBreakBuffer, bool bullishExceptionalBar, bool bearishExceptionalBar) =>
    var float previousHigh = na
    var float lastHigh = na
    var int previousHighBar = na
    var int lastHighBar = na
    var float previousLow = na
    var float lastLow = na
    var int previousLowBar = na
    var int lastLowBar = na
    // Principal trend anchors are frozen at the first confirmed HH+HL / LL+LH sequence.
    // Newer pivots form the secondary trend elsewhere instead of silently rolling the main line forward.
    var int principalBias = 0
    var float principalFirst = na
    var float principalSecond = na
    var int principalFirstBar = na
    var int principalSecondBar = na
    var float principalWidth = na
    var int lastInvalidatedBullSecondAnchorBar = na
    var int lastInvalidatedBearSecondAnchorBar = na

    bool newHigh = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(pivotHighCandidate)
    bool newLow = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(pivotLowCandidate)
    if newHigh
        previousHigh := lastHigh
        previousHighBar := lastHighBar
        lastHigh := pivotHighCandidate
        lastHighBar := candidateBar
    if newLow
        previousLow := lastLow
        previousLowBar := lastLowBar
        lastLow := pivotLowCandidate
        lastLowBar := candidateBar

    int minimumAnchorBars = pivotStrength + 1
    bool highsReady = not na(previousHigh) and not na(lastHigh) and not na(previousHighBar) and not na(lastHighBar) and lastHighBar - previousHighBar >= minimumAnchorBars
    bool lowsReady = not na(previousLow) and not na(lastLow) and not na(previousLowBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastLowBar - previousLowBar >= minimumAnchorBars
    bool bullishSequence = highsReady and lowsReady and lastHigh > previousHigh + syminfo.mintick and lastLow > previousLow + syminfo.mintick
    bool bearishSequence = highsReady and lowsReady and lastHigh < previousHigh - syminfo.mintick and lastLow < previousLow - syminfo.mintick

    // Validate only when arming a fresh principal trend. This preserves the beginning
    // of the trend until a close-confirmed break instead of replacing it with the latest pair.
    if principalBias == 0 and bullishSequence and dowDirection != -1 and lastLowBar != lastInvalidatedBullSecondAnchorBar
        bool validPair = true
        int validationSpan = bar_index - previousLowBar
        if validationSpan > maximumValidationBars
            validPair := false
        else
            for historyOffset = 0 to validationSpan
                int historicalBar = bar_index - historyOffset
                float lineAtHistory = f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, historicalBar)
                float historicalBuffer = nz(atrSeries[historyOffset], atrSeries) * math.max(breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart * 0.50)
                if close[historyOffset] < lineAtHistory - historicalBuffer
                    validPair := false
                    break
        if validPair
            principalBias := 1
            principalFirst := previousLow
            principalFirstBar := previousLowBar
            principalSecond := lastLow
            principalSecondBar := lastLowBar
            principalWidth := na
    if principalBias == 0 and bearishSequence and dowDirection != 1 and lastHighBar != lastInvalidatedBearSecondAnchorBar
        bool validPair = true
        int validationSpan = bar_index - previousHighBar
        if validationSpan > maximumValidationBars
            validPair := false
        else
            for historyOffset = 0 to validationSpan
                int historicalBar = bar_index - historyOffset
                float lineAtHistory = f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, historicalBar)
                float historicalBuffer = nz(atrSeries[historyOffset], atrSeries) * math.max(breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart * 0.50)
                if close[historyOffset] > lineAtHistory + historicalBuffer
                    validPair := false
                    break
        if validPair
            principalBias := -1
            principalFirst := previousHigh
            principalFirstBar := previousHighBar
            principalSecond := lastHigh
            principalSecondBar := lastHighBar
            principalWidth := na

    bool principalAnchorsReady = principalBias != 0 and not na(principalFirst) and not na(principalSecond) and not na(principalFirstBar) and not na(principalSecondBar) and principalSecondBar > principalFirstBar
    float principalSlope = principalAnchorsReady ? (principalSecond - principalFirst) / float(principalSecondBar - principalFirstBar) : na
    float baseNow = principalAnchorsReady ? f_project(principalFirst, principalFirstBar, principalSecond, principalSecondBar, bar_index) : na
    bool slopeRelevant = principalAnchorsReady and math.abs(principalSlope) <= atrSeries * maximumSlopeAtr
    bool majorTrendReadyCalc = principalBias != 0 and slopeRelevant

    // Principal channel uses opposite-side pivots that belong to the principal trend itself.
    // Pivots printed before the trend origin cannot freeze an unrelated channel width.
    bool risingWidthPivotsReady = principalBias == 1 and highsReady and previousHighBar >= principalFirstBar and lastHighBar >= principalFirstBar
    bool fallingWidthPivotsReady = principalBias == -1 and lowsReady and previousLowBar >= principalFirstBar and lastLowBar >= principalFirstBar
    float width1 = risingWidthPivotsReady ? previousHigh - f_project(principalFirst, principalFirstBar, principalSecond, principalSecondBar, previousHighBar) : fallingWidthPivotsReady ? f_project(principalFirst, principalFirstBar, principalSecond, principalSecondBar, previousLowBar) - previousLow : na
    float width2 = risingWidthPivotsReady ? lastHigh - f_project(principalFirst, principalFirstBar, principalSecond, principalSecondBar, lastHighBar) : fallingWidthPivotsReady ? f_project(principalFirst, principalFirstBar, principalSecond, principalSecondBar, lastLowBar) - lastLow : na
    float widthMax = not na(width1) and not na(width2) ? math.max(width1, width2) : na
    float widthMin = not na(width1) and not na(width2) ? math.min(width1, width2) : na
    float widthConsistency = not na(widthMax) and widthMax > syminfo.mintick ? widthMin / widthMax : na
    bool widthCandidateValid = not na(widthMin) and widthMin > syminfo.mintick and not na(widthConsistency) and widthConsistency >= minimumWidthConsistency and widthMax <= atrSeries * maximumProjectionAtr * 2.0
    if na(principalWidth) and widthCandidateValid
        principalWidth := widthMax
    bool majorChannelReadyCalc = majorTrendReadyCalc and not na(principalWidth) and principalWidth > syminfo.mintick

    // Confirm main-trend invalidation by closes, not a wick. Opposite corrective
    // secondary trends may coexist until this principal line actually fails.
    bool breakDownRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, baseNow, nz(baseNow[1], baseNow), closeBreakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBar)
    bool breakUpRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, baseNow, nz(baseNow[1], baseNow), closeBreakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBar)
    bool invalidateNow = majorTrendReadyCalc and (principalBias == 1 and breakDownRaw or principalBias == -1 and breakUpRaw)
    if invalidateNow
        if principalBias == 1
            lastInvalidatedBullSecondAnchorBar := principalSecondBar
        else
            lastInvalidatedBearSecondAnchorBar := principalSecondBar
        principalBias := 0
        principalFirst := na
        principalSecond := na
        principalFirstBar := na
        principalSecondBar := na
        principalWidth := na

    int outputBias = invalidateNow ? 0 : principalBias
    bool outputTrendReady = invalidateNow ? false : majorTrendReadyCalc
    bool outputChannelReady = invalidateNow ? false : majorChannelReadyCalc
    float outputBase = invalidateNow ? na : baseNow
    float outputWidth = invalidateNow ? na : principalWidth
    float outputLower = outputChannelReady ? (outputBias == 1 ? outputBase : outputBase - outputWidth) : na
    float outputUpper = outputChannelReady ? (outputBias == 1 ? outputBase + outputWidth : outputBase) : na
    [outputBias, outputTrendReady, outputChannelReady, outputBase, outputLower, outputUpper, outputWidth, principalFirstBar, principalFirst, principalSecondBar, principalSecond]

// compile-size optimization only; calculations and confirmation rules are the
// same as the secondary layer.

f_calculate_major_layer(int pivotStrength, int maximumValidationBars, float atrSeries, float breakAtrPart, float zoneAtrPart, float maximumProjectionAtr, float maximumSlopeAtr, float minimumWidthConsistency, int dowDirection, float closeBreakBuffer, int fakeoutBars, int retestBars, int forwardBars, bool bullishExceptionalBar, bool bearishExceptionalBar) =>
    float rightHigh = ta.highest(high, pivotStrength)
    float leftHigh = ta.highest(high[pivotStrength + 1], pivotStrength)
    float rightLow = ta.lowest(low, pivotStrength)
    float leftLow = ta.lowest(low[pivotStrength + 1], pivotStrength)
    float pivotAtr = nz(atrSeries[pivotStrength], atrSeries)
    float pivotProminence = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, pivotAtr * 0.10)
    bool strictHigh = bar_index >= pivotStrength * 2 and high[pivotStrength] > rightHigh and high[pivotStrength] > leftHigh and high[pivotStrength] - math.max(rightHigh, leftHigh) >= pivotProminence
    bool strictLow = bar_index >= pivotStrength * 2 and low[pivotStrength] < rightLow and low[pivotStrength] < leftLow and math.min(rightLow, leftLow) - low[pivotStrength] >= pivotProminence
    bool ambiguousPivot = strictHigh and strictLow
    bool pivotSessionAllowed = analysisSessionPass[pivotStrength]
    float highCandidate = strictHigh and not ambiguousPivot and pivotSessionAllowed ? high[pivotStrength] : na
    float lowCandidate = strictLow and not ambiguousPivot and pivotSessionAllowed ? low[pivotStrength] : na
    int candidateBar = bar_index - pivotStrength
    [bias, trendReady, channelReady, baseNow, lowerNow, upperNow, width, firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice] = f_update_major_channel(highCandidate, lowCandidate, candidateBar, pivotStrength, maximumValidationBars, atrSeries, breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart, maximumProjectionAtr, maximumSlopeAtr, minimumWidthConsistency, dowDirection, closeBreakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBar, bearishExceptionalBar)
    float middleNow = channelReady ? (lowerNow + upperNow) * 0.50 : na
    bool breakoutUpAttemptRaw = f_level_break_attempt(true, upperNow, nz(upperNow[1], upperNow), closeBreakBuffer)
    bool breakoutDownAttemptRaw = f_level_break_attempt(false, lowerNow, nz(lowerNow[1], lowerNow), closeBreakBuffer)
    bool breakoutUpRaw = f_level_break_event(true, upperNow, nz(upperNow[1], upperNow), closeBreakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBar)
    bool breakoutDownRaw = f_level_break_event(false, lowerNow, nz(lowerNow[1], lowerNow), closeBreakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBar)
    bool breakoutUpAttempt = analysisSessionPass and channelReady and breakoutUpAttemptRaw
    bool breakoutDownAttempt = analysisSessionPass and channelReady and breakoutDownAttemptRaw
    bool breakoutUp = analysisSessionPass and channelReady and breakoutUpRaw
    bool breakoutDown = analysisSessionPass and channelReady and breakoutDownRaw
    bool geometryStable = channelReady and channelReady[1] and bias == bias[1] and not f_int_changed(firstBar[1], firstBar) and not f_int_changed(secondBar[1], secondBar)
    bool breakoutUpEvent = breakoutUp and geometryStable and not breakoutUp[1]
    bool breakoutDownEvent = breakoutDown and geometryStable and not breakoutDown[1]
    // Stateful principal-channel watcher. `ta.barssince()` alone can keep an
    // older opposite breakout alive and let both sides appear eligible. Resolve
    // one watched crossing, then arm the next crossing.
    var int watchedDirection = 0
    var int watchedBar = na
    var int watchedBias = 0
    var int watchedFirstBar = na
    var int watchedSecondBar = na
    int priorWatchedDirection = watchedDirection
    int priorWatchedBar = watchedBar
    int barsSinceWatchedBreak = na(priorWatchedBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - priorWatchedBar
    bool watchedContextInvalid = priorWatchedDirection != 0 and (not channelReady or not ((watchedFirstBar) == firstBar and (watchedSecondBar) == secondBar and (watchedBias) == bias))
    bool upperFakeout = barstate.isconfirmed and channelReady and not watchedContextInvalid and priorWatchedDirection == 1 and barsSinceWatchedBreak > 0 and barsSinceWatchedBreak <= fakeoutBars and close <= upperNow
    bool lowerFakeout = barstate.isconfirmed and channelReady and not watchedContextInvalid and priorWatchedDirection == -1 and barsSinceWatchedBreak > 0 and barsSinceWatchedBreak <= fakeoutBars and close >= lowerNow
    if watchedContextInvalid or (priorWatchedDirection != 0 and barsSinceWatchedBreak > fakeoutBars) or upperFakeout or lowerFakeout
        watchedDirection := 0
        watchedBar := na
        watchedBias := 0
        watchedFirstBar := na
        watchedSecondBar := na
    if breakoutUpAttempt
        watchedDirection := 1
        watchedBar := bar_index
        watchedBias := bias
        watchedFirstBar := firstBar
        watchedSecondBar := secondBar
    else if breakoutDownAttempt
        watchedDirection := -1
        watchedBar := bar_index
        watchedBias := bias
        watchedFirstBar := firstBar
        watchedSecondBar := secondBar
    bool supportBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, baseNow, nz(baseNow[1], baseNow), closeBreakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBar)
    bool resistanceBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, baseNow, nz(baseNow[1], baseNow), closeBreakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBar)
    bool supportBroken = trendReady and bias == 1 and supportBreakRaw
    bool resistanceBroken = trendReady and bias == -1 and resistanceBreakRaw
    // Invalidation is persisted by f_update_major_channel(), which owns the state.
    int signal = f_channel_signal(upperFakeout, lowerFakeout, channelReady, breakoutUp, breakoutDown, bias, close, middleNow, trendReady, supportBroken, resistanceBroken, baseNow)
    var float bullishTarget = na
    var int bullishTargetBar = na
    var float bearishTarget = na
    var int bearishTargetBar = na
    int objectiveMaximumAge = math.max(retestBars, forwardBars * 5)
    [nextBullTarget, nextBullTargetBar, nextBearTarget, nextBearTargetBar] = f_update_channel_objectives(bullishTarget, bullishTargetBar, bearishTarget, bearishTargetBar, breakoutUpEvent, breakoutDownEvent, upperFakeout, lowerFakeout, not geometryStable, upperNow, lowerNow, width, objectiveMaximumAge)
    bullishTarget := nextBullTarget
    bullishTargetBar := nextBullTargetBar
    bearishTarget := nextBearTarget
    bearishTargetBar := nextBearTargetBar
    [bias, trendReady, channelReady, baseNow, lowerNow, upperNow, width, firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice, breakoutUpEvent, breakoutDownEvent, breakoutUp, breakoutDown, upperFakeout, lowerFakeout, signal, bullishTarget, bearishTarget]

// Frozen trend-line retest: the anchors are saved on the break, so later pivots
// cannot move the watched level or repaint the retest.
// Frozen trend-line retest. Save the projected level and slope at the confirmed break,
// so later pivots cannot move the watched line or repaint the retest.
f_trendline_retest_state(bool trendIsReady, int trendBias, float trendBase, int firstAnchorBar, float firstAnchorPrice, int secondAnchorBar, float secondAnchorPrice, float closeBuffer, int retestBars, float atrValue, float closeLocationValue, float bodyRatioValue, bool bullishExceptionalBar, bool bearishExceptionalBar) =>
    bool anchorsReady = not na(firstAnchorBar) and not na(firstAnchorPrice) and not na(secondAnchorBar) and not na(secondAnchorPrice) and secondAnchorBar > firstAnchorBar
    bool stable = anchorsReady and trendIsReady and trendIsReady[1] and trendBias == trendBias[1] and not f_int_changed(firstAnchorBar[1], firstAnchorBar) and not f_int_changed(secondAnchorBar[1], secondAnchorBar)
    bool principalInvalidated = trendIsReady[1] and not trendIsReady and trendBias == 0 and trendBias[1] != 0
    int principalInvalidationDirection = principalInvalidated ? -trendBias[1] : 0
    float priorSlope = principalInvalidated ? (secondAnchorPrice[1] - firstAnchorPrice[1]) / float(secondAnchorBar[1] - firstAnchorBar[1]) : na
    float invalidatedBoundaryNow = principalInvalidated ? secondAnchorPrice[1] + priorSlope * float(bar_index - secondAnchorBar[1]) : na
    bool adaptiveBreakUpRaw = f_level_break_event(true, trendBase, nz(trendBase[1], trendBase), closeBuffer, bullishExceptionalBar)
    bool adaptiveBreakDownRaw = f_level_break_event(false, trendBase, nz(trendBase[1], trendBase), closeBuffer, bearishExceptionalBar)
    bool breakUp = analysisSessionPass and (stable and trendBias == -1 and adaptiveBreakUpRaw or principalInvalidationDirection == 1)
    bool breakDown = analysisSessionPass and (stable and trendBias == 1 and adaptiveBreakDownRaw or principalInvalidationDirection == -1)
    float activeBreakBoundary = principalInvalidated ? invalidatedBoundaryNow : trendBase
    float activeBreakSlope = principalInvalidated ? priorSlope : anchorsReady ? (secondAnchorPrice - firstAnchorPrice) / float(secondAnchorBar - firstAnchorBar) : na
    var int watchDirection = 0
    var int breakBar = na
    var float frozenBoundary = na
    var float frozenSlope = na
    int age = na(breakBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - breakBar
    float boundary = watchDirection != 0 and not na(frozenBoundary) and not na(frozenSlope) ? frozenBoundary + frozenSlope * age : na
    float tolerance = math.max(atrValue * math.max(trendZoneAtrWidth, retestAtrTolerance), syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float overshoot = math.max(tolerance * 2.5, syminfo.mintick * 8.0)
    bool bullRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and watchDirection == 1 and age > 0 and age <= retestBars and low <= boundary + tolerance and low >= boundary - overshoot and close > boundary + closeBuffer * 0.15 and close > open and closeLocationValue >= 0.60 and bodyRatioValue >= 0.35
    bool bearRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and watchDirection == -1 and age > 0 and age <= retestBars and high >= boundary - tolerance and high <= boundary + overshoot and close < boundary - closeBuffer * 0.15 and close < open and closeLocationValue <= 0.40 and bodyRatioValue >= 0.35
    bool invalid = watchDirection != 0 and (age > retestBars or watchDirection == 1 and close < boundary - overshoot or watchDirection == -1 and close > boundary + overshoot)
    if invalid or bullRetest or bearRetest
        watchDirection := 0
        breakBar := na
        frozenBoundary := na
        frozenSlope := na
    if breakUp or breakDown
        watchDirection := breakUp ? 1 : -1
        breakBar := bar_index
        frozenBoundary := activeBreakBoundary
        frozenSlope := activeBreakSlope
    float eventBoundary = breakUp or breakDown ? activeBreakBoundary : boundary
    [bullRetest, bearRetest, eventBoundary, breakUp, breakDown]

f_update_channel_visual(line trendLine, line parallelLine, bool showLayer, bool trendIsReady, bool channelIsReady, int bias, int firstBar, float firstPrice, int secondBar, float secondPrice, float channelWidthValue, color bullishColor, color bearishColor, int trendTransparency, int parallelTransparency, bool principalLayer) =>
    line nextTrendLine = trendLine
    line nextParallelLine = parallelLine
    bool anchorsReady = not na(firstBar) and not na(firstPrice) and not na(secondBar) and not na(secondPrice) and secondBar > firstBar
    float anchorSlope = anchorsReady ? (secondPrice - firstPrice) / float(secondBar - firstBar) : na
    int safeFirstBar = anchorsReady ? f_safe_past_bar(firstBar) : bar_index
    int safeSecondBar = anchorsReady ? f_safe_past_bar(secondBar) : bar_index
    bool visualBarsReady = anchorsReady and safeSecondBar > safeFirstBar
    float safeFirstPrice = visualBarsReady ? firstPrice + anchorSlope * float(safeFirstBar - firstBar) : na
    float safeSecondPrice = visualBarsReady ? secondPrice + anchorSlope * float(safeSecondBar - secondBar) : na
    bool directionReady = bias == 1 ? secondPrice > firstPrice + syminfo.mintick : bias == -1 ? secondPrice < firstPrice - syminfo.mintick : false
    if showLayer and trendIsReady and visualBarsReady and directionReady
        color baseColor = bias == 1 ? bullishColor : bearishColor
        color trendColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? math.max(0, trendTransparency - 12) : math.min(95, trendTransparency + 10))
        color parallelColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? math.max(88, parallelTransparency) : math.max(94, parallelTransparency))
        string trendStyle = principalLayer ? line.style_solid : line.style_dashed
        string parallelStyle = principalLayer ? line.style_dashed : line.style_dotted
        int trendWidth = principalLayer ? (trendTransparency <= 35 ? 3 : 2) : 1
        if na(nextTrendLine)
            nextTrendLine := line.new(safeFirstBar, safeFirstPrice, safeSecondBar, safeSecondPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = trendColor, style = trendStyle, width = trendWidth, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(nextTrendLine, safeFirstBar, safeFirstPrice)
            line.set_xy2(nextTrendLine, safeSecondBar, safeSecondPrice)
            line.set_color(nextTrendLine, trendColor)
        if channelIsReady and not na(channelWidthValue)
            float parallelFirstPrice = bias == 1 ? safeFirstPrice + channelWidthValue : safeFirstPrice - channelWidthValue
            float parallelSecondPrice = bias == 1 ? safeSecondPrice + channelWidthValue : safeSecondPrice - channelWidthValue
            if na(nextParallelLine)
                nextParallelLine := line.new(safeFirstBar, parallelFirstPrice, safeSecondBar, parallelSecondPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = parallelColor, style = parallelStyle, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
            else
                line.set_xy1(nextParallelLine, safeFirstBar, parallelFirstPrice)
                line.set_xy2(nextParallelLine, safeSecondBar, parallelSecondPrice)
                line.set_color(nextParallelLine, parallelColor)
        else
            nextParallelLine := f_clear_line(nextParallelLine)
    else
        nextTrendLine := f_clear_line(nextTrendLine)
        nextParallelLine := f_clear_line(nextParallelLine)
    [nextTrendLine, nextParallelLine]

// Build one recent multi-touch cluster from pivots of the same type. Keeping
// highs and lows in separate arrays prevents a support and a resistance from
// being merged merely because their prices happen to be close.
f_strong_sr_cluster(array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, float tolerance, int minimumTouches, int maximumAgeMilliseconds, int clusterPointLimit, int minimumTouchSeparationBars, bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float excludedLow, float excludedHigh) =>
    float selectedLow = na
    float selectedHigh = na
    float selectedDistance = 1e20
    int selectedTouches = 0
    int selectedNewestTime = 0
    int availablePoints = math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), math.min(array.size(pivotTimes), clusterPointLimit))
    int candidatePoints = math.min(availablePoints, timeframe.isintraday ? 24 : 20)
    if availablePoints > 0 and candidatePoints > 0
        for candidateIndex = 0 to candidatePoints - 1
            float candidatePrice = array.get(prices, candidateIndex)
            int candidateTime = array.get(pivotTimes, candidateIndex)
            if not na(candidatePrice) and not na(candidateTime) and time >= candidateTime and time - candidateTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds
                float clusterLow = na
                float clusterHigh = na
                int clusterTouches = 0
                int clusterNewestTime = 0
                int lastAcceptedBar = na
                for pointIndex = 0 to availablePoints - 1
                    float pointPrice = array.get(prices, pointIndex)
                    int pointBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, pointIndex)
                    int pointTime = array.get(pivotTimes, pointIndex)
                    bool pointValid = not na(pointPrice) and not na(pointTime) and time >= pointTime and time - pointTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds and math.abs(pointPrice - candidatePrice) <= tolerance
                    float prospectiveLow = pointValid ? (na(clusterLow) ? pointPrice : math.min(clusterLow, pointPrice)) : clusterLow
                    float prospectiveHigh = pointValid ? (na(clusterHigh) ? pointPrice : math.max(clusterHigh, pointPrice)) : clusterHigh
                    bool touchSeparated = pointValid and (na(lastAcceptedBar) or math.abs(pointBar - lastAcceptedBar) >= minimumTouchSeparationBars)
                    if touchSeparated and prospectiveHigh - prospectiveLow <= tolerance + syminfo.mintick * 0.50
                        clusterLow := prospectiveLow
                        clusterHigh := prospectiveHigh
                        clusterTouches += 1
                        clusterNewestTime := math.max(clusterNewestTime, pointTime)
                        lastAcceptedBar := pointBar
                if clusterTouches >= minimumTouches
                    // حدود المنطقة هي القمم/القيعان المؤكدة نفسها. عند تطابق
                    // السعرين نضيف tick واحد إلى الجهة الخارجية فقط، فلا تتحرك الحافة المواجهة للسعر.
                    float qualifiedLow = clusterLow
                    float qualifiedHigh = clusterHigh
                    if clusterHigh - clusterLow < syminfo.mintick
                        qualifiedLow := searchAbove ? clusterLow : math.max(syminfo.mintick, clusterLow - syminfo.mintick)
                        qualifiedHigh := searchAbove ? clusterHigh + syminfo.mintick : clusterHigh
                    bool sideValid = searchAbove ? qualifiedHigh >= currentPrice : qualifiedLow <= currentPrice
                    float zoneDistance = currentPrice >= qualifiedLow and currentPrice <= qualifiedHigh ? 0.0 : searchAbove ? math.max(qualifiedLow - currentPrice, 0.0) : math.max(currentPrice - qualifiedHigh, 0.0)
                    bool excluded = not na(excludedLow) and not na(excludedHigh) and qualifiedHigh >= excludedLow - tolerance and qualifiedLow <= excludedHigh + tolerance
                    bool candidateWins = sideValid and not excluded and (zoneDistance < selectedDistance - tolerance * 0.50 or math.abs(zoneDistance - selectedDistance) <= tolerance * 0.50 and (clusterTouches > selectedTouches or clusterTouches == selectedTouches and clusterNewestTime > selectedNewestTime))
                    if candidateWins
                        selectedLow := qualifiedLow
                        selectedHigh := qualifiedHigh
                        selectedDistance := zoneDistance
                        selectedTouches := clusterTouches
                        selectedNewestTime := clusterNewestTime
    bool qualified = selectedTouches >= minimumTouches and not na(selectedLow) and not na(selectedHigh) and selectedHigh > selectedLow
    [qualified ? selectedLow : na, qualified ? selectedHigh : na]


// يثبت بداية الخط/المنطقة على أقدم Pivot مؤكد شارك في المستوى، بدلا من
// بدء الرسم من نافذة حديثة تجعله يبدو عائما بعيدا عن القمم أو القيعان.
f_sr_visual_anchor_bar(array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, float zoneLow, float zoneHigh, int maximumAgeMilliseconds, int fallbackBar) =>
    int anchorBar = na
    int count = math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), array.size(pivotTimes))
    if not na(zoneLow) and not na(zoneHigh) and count > 0
        for i = 0 to count - 1
            float p = array.get(prices, i)
            int b = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, i)
            int t = array.get(pivotTimes, i)
            bool ageValid = not na(t) and time >= t and (maximumAgeMilliseconds <= 0 or time - t <= maximumAgeMilliseconds)
            bool member = not na(p) and p >= zoneLow - syminfo.mintick * 0.5 and p <= zoneHigh + syminfo.mintick * 0.5
            if ageValid and member and not na(b)
                anchorBar := na(anchorBar) ? b : math.min(anchorBar, b)
    f_safe_past_bar(nz(anchorBar, fallbackBar))

f_nearest_sr_zone(bool searchAbove, float maximumDistance, float exclusionTolerance, float candidateLow1, float candidateHigh1, float candidateLow2, float candidateHigh2, float excludedLow, float excludedHigh) =>
    float selectedLow = na
    float selectedHigh = na
    float selectedDistance = 1e20
    for zoneIndex = 0 to 1
        float zoneLow = zoneIndex == 0 ? candidateLow1 : candidateLow2
        float zoneHigh = zoneIndex == 0 ? candidateHigh1 : candidateHigh2
        bool geometryValid = not na(zoneLow) and not na(zoneHigh) and zoneLow > 0 and zoneHigh - zoneLow >= syminfo.mintick
        bool priceInsideZone = geometryValid and close >= zoneLow and close <= zoneHigh
        float zoneDistance = priceInsideZone ? 0.0 : geometryValid ? math.abs((searchAbove ? zoneLow : zoneHigh) - close) : na
        bool sideValid = geometryValid and (priceInsideZone or (searchAbove ? zoneHigh >= close : zoneLow <= close))
        bool distanceValid = sideValid and zoneDistance <= maximumDistance
        bool excluded = distanceValid and not na(excludedLow) and not na(excludedHigh) and zoneHigh >= excludedLow - exclusionTolerance and zoneLow <= excludedHigh + exclusionTolerance
        if distanceValid and not excluded and zoneDistance < selectedDistance
            selectedLow := zoneLow
            selectedHigh := zoneHigh
            selectedDistance := zoneDistance
    [selectedLow, selectedHigh]

f_zones_overlap(float firstLow, float firstHigh, float secondLow, float secondHigh, float tolerance) =>
    not na(firstLow) and not na(firstHigh) and firstHigh > firstLow and not na(secondLow) and not na(secondHigh) and secondHigh > secondLow and math.max(firstLow, secondLow) <= math.min(firstHigh, secondHigh) + tolerance

f_level_inside_sr_zones(float level, float zone1Low, float zone1High, float zone2Low, float zone2High, float tolerance) =>
    bool insideFirst = not na(level) and not na(zone1Low) and not na(zone1High) and level >= zone1Low - tolerance and level <= zone1High + tolerance
    bool insideSecond = not na(level) and not na(zone2Low) and not na(zone2High) and level >= zone2Low - tolerance and level <= zone2High + tolerance
    insideFirst or insideSecond

f_next_sr_expiry(array<int> pivotTimes, int maximumAgeMilliseconds) =>
    int nextExpiryTime = na
    int availableTimes = array.size(pivotTimes)
    if availableTimes > 0
        for pointIndex = 0 to availableTimes - 1
            int pointTime = array.get(pivotTimes, pointIndex)
            int pointExpiryTime = pointTime + maximumAgeMilliseconds + 1
            if pointExpiryTime > time
                nextExpiryTime := na(nextExpiryTime) ? pointExpiryTime : math.min(nextExpiryTime, pointExpiryTime)
    nextExpiryTime

f_update_sr_line(line currentLine, int firstBar, float level, color lineColor, string lineStyle, int lineWidth, int transparency) =>
    // TradingView can remove an old drawing through garbage collection while its
    // series variable still retains the former ID. line.delete() is safe even
    // when that ID was already deleted, whereas line.set_*() on it can stop the script.
    line result = currentLine
    if not na(result)
        line.delete(result)
        result := na
    if not na(level)
        int safeFirstBar = math.min(bar_index, f_safe_past_bar(firstBar))
        int rightBar = math.min(bar_index + 499, math.max(safeFirstBar + 1, bar_index + channelForwardBars))
        result := line.new(safeFirstBar, level, rightBar, level, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(lineColor, transparency), style = lineStyle, width = lineWidth, force_overlay = true)
    result

f_update_zone_visual(line currentTop, line currentBottom, linefill currentFill, bool enabled, int firstBar, int lastBar, float topLevel, float bottomLevel, color zoneColor) =>
    line topLine = currentTop
    line bottomLine = currentBottom
    linefill zoneFill = currentFill
    bool valid = enabled and not na(topLevel) and not na(bottomLevel) and topLevel > bottomLevel and lastBar > firstBar
    if not valid
        if not na(zoneFill)
            linefill.delete(zoneFill)
            zoneFill := na
        topLine := f_clear_line(topLine)
        bottomLine := f_clear_line(bottomLine)
    else
        int safeFirstBar = f_safe_past_bar(firstBar)
        int safeLastBar = f_safe_future_bar(lastBar, safeFirstBar)
        if na(topLine)
            topLine := line.new(safeFirstBar, topLevel, safeLastBar, topLevel, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = color.new(zoneColor, 68), style = line.style_solid, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(topLine, safeFirstBar, topLevel)
            line.set_xy2(topLine, safeLastBar, topLevel)
            line.set_color(topLine, color.new(zoneColor, 68))
        if na(bottomLine)
            bottomLine := line.new(safeFirstBar, bottomLevel, safeLastBar, bottomLevel, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = color.new(zoneColor, 68), style = line.style_solid, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(bottomLine, safeFirstBar, bottomLevel)
            line.set_xy2(bottomLine, safeLastBar, bottomLevel)
            line.set_color(bottomLine, color.new(zoneColor, 68))
        if na(zoneFill)
            zoneFill := linefill.new(topLine, bottomLine, color.new(zoneColor, 92))
        else
            linefill.set_color(zoneFill, color.new(zoneColor, 92))
    [topLine, bottomLine, zoneFill]


f_sync_right_line_label(array<label> ids, int i, bool enabled, float level, string caption, color textColor, float atrValue) =>
    label id = array.get(ids, i)
    if not enabled or na(level)
        id := f_clear_label(id)
    else
        bool centeredZoneCaption = i <= 3
        bool zoneCaption = i <= 5
        int lane = i % 3
        float y = centeredZoneCaption ? level : zoneCaption ? level : level + math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, math.max(nz(atrValue, 0.0), syminfo.mintick * 20.0) * 0.008)
        int x = centeredZoneCaption ? bar_index + math.max(2, int(math.round(channelForwardBars * 0.50))) : zoneCaption ? bar_index + 2 + lane * 2 : bar_index + channelForwardBars + 1 + lane * 2
        string s = zoneCaption ? caption : caption + "  " + str.tostring(level, format.mintick)
        if na(id)
            id := label.new(x, y, s, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = textColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = centeredZoneCaption ? text.align_center : text.align_left, force_overlay = true)
        else
            label.set_xy(id, x, y)
            label.set_text(id, s)
            label.set_textcolor(id, textColor)
    array.set(ids, i, id)

f_closed_htf_context() =>
    int monthMultiple = timeframe.ismonthly ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
    int fastLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 3 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 5 : 8) : timeframe.isweekly ? 13 : 20
    int slowLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 6 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 10 : 21) : timeframe.isweekly ? 34 : 50
    float fast = ta.ema(close, fastLength)
    float slow = ta.ema(close, slowLength)
    float momentum = ta.rsi(close, 14)
    float volatility = ta.atr(14)
    float priorFast = fast[3]
    bool ready = not na(priorFast) and not na(slow[1]) and not na(momentum[1]) and not na(volatility[1]) and volatility[1] > 0
    float separation = ready ? (fast[1] - slow[1]) / volatility[1] : 0.0
    float slope = ready ? (fast[1] - priorFast) / volatility[1] : 0.0
    int score = not ready ? 0 : (close[1] > fast[1] ? 1 : close[1] < fast[1] ? -1 : 0) + (separation >= 0.10 ? 1 : separation <= -0.10 ? -1 : 0) + (slope >= 0.05 ? 1 : slope <= -0.05 ? -1 : 0) + (momentum[1] >= 55 ? 1 : momentum[1] <= 45 ? -1 : 0)
    int trendState = not ready ? 9 : score >= 3 ? 2 : score >= 1 ? 1 : score <= -3 ? -2 : score <= -1 ? -1 : 0
    float pivotHighRaw = ta.pivothigh(high, 2, 2)
    float pivotLowRaw = ta.pivotlow(low, 2, 2)
    float pivotHigh = ta.valuewhen(not na(pivotHighRaw), pivotHighRaw, 0)[1]
    float pivotLow = ta.valuewhen(not na(pivotLowRaw), pivotLowRaw, 0)[1]
    [trendState, pivotHigh, pivotLow]

// تحليل الأسهم يعتمد على سعر وحجم الأصل نفسه فقط.
bool stockSymbolAllowed = syminfo.type == "stock" or syminfo.type == "fund" or syminfo.type == "dr"
bool tadawulStock = syminfo.prefix == "TADAWUL" and stockSymbolAllowed
bool shortTradesAllowed = not (saudiLongOnlyMode and tadawulStock)
int chartSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds())
int maximumSupportedSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds("D"))
bool supportedTimeframe = timeframe.isintraday and chartSeconds >= 60 and chartSeconds < maximumSupportedSeconds
bool standardChartAllowed = chart.is_standard  // الشموع العادية والمفرغة تستخدم OHLC الحقيقي؛ Heikin Ashi والأنواع الصناعية غير معتمدة للتنفيذ.
bool stockChartAllowed = stockSymbolAllowed and supportedTimeframe and standardChartAllowed
// إشارات الشارت مصدرها الفاصل المفتوح فقط. الفواصل الأعلى للرأي المترابط والجدول ولا يرسمان أحداثا على الشموع.
bool chartSignalDisplayAllowed = stockChartAllowed
// Bullish and bearish plans are both executable when their evidence, geometry and risk gates pass.
bool entrySessionPass = not onlyRegularSessionEntries or session.ismarket

// Apply a moderate additional horizon factor instead of multiplying time twice.
// Daily, weekly and monthly profiles receive progressively wider fallback target horizons.
f_timeframe_display_profile() =>
    float horizonFactor = chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 1.00 : chartSeconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 1.10 : 1.25
    bool compactDisplay = chartSeconds <= 30 * 60
    bool fastestDisplay = compactDisplay and chartSeconds <= 5 * 60
    int boxOffset = fastestDisplay ? math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 14) : compactDisplay ? math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 10) : math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 7)
    int trendTransparency = compactDisplay ? 72 : 62
    [horizonFactor, boxOffset, trendTransparency, (compactDisplay ? 91 : 84), (compactDisplay ? 12 : 5), (compactDisplay ? 12 : 5), (compactDisplay ? 25 : 15), (compactDisplay ? 30 : 20), (compactDisplay ? 35 : 25)]

[automaticTargetHorizonFactor, visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, trendLineTransparency, channelLineTransparency, planEntryLineTransparency, planStopLineTransparency, planTarget1LineTransparency, planTarget2LineTransparency, planTarget3LineTransparency] = f_timeframe_display_profile()
float targetHorizonFactor = automaticTargetHorizonFactor
// الفواصل اللحظية تحتاج طبقة عرض أخف من اليومي والأسبوعي. لا تتغير
// الحسابات أو الشروط هنا؛ التكيف بصري فقط حتى لا تغطي العلامات الشموع.
// their nearer offset, while entry/stop/target captions use three times the distance.
int visualExplanationOffsetBars = math.min(12, math.max(6, visualPlanBoxOffsetBars))

f_new_plan_level_label(float level, color textColor) =>
    label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, level, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = textColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left, force_overlay = true)

f_replace_plan_level_label(label currentId, bool enabled, float level, color textColor) =>
    f_clear_label(currentId)
    enabled ? f_new_plan_level_label(level, textColor) : na

if barstate.isfirst and not supportedTimeframe
    runtime.error("استخدم اللحظي من 1د إلى أقل من اليومي.")
if barstate.isfirst and not standardChartAllowed
    runtime.error("استخدم شموعًا سعرية قياسية فقط.")
if barstate.isfirst and not stockSymbolAllowed
    runtime.error("النسخة مخصصة للأسهم والصناديق المدرجة.")
// قاعدة 2+2 هي الحد الأدنى الكلاسيكي للمحور الصحيح. يستطيع المستخدم رفع
// القوة يدويا للحصول على قمم وقيعان هيكلية أبطأ وأقل عددا.
int pivotBars = 2
// محاور الرسم أقوى من محاور بنية 2+2. تتسع تلقائيا على الفواصل السريعة
// لتصفية الضوضاء، وتستخدم قيمة مستقلة لحد اليومي المشترك.
int channelPivotBars = chartSeconds <= 60 ? 5 : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 4 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 4 : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 3 : 4
int majorChannelPivotBars = math.min(20, math.max(channelPivotBars + 2, int(math.round(float(channelPivotBars) * 1.50))))
string higherTimeframe1 = chartSeconds <= 60 ? "5" : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "15" : chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? "60" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "120" : chartSeconds <= 120 * 60 ? "240" : "D"
string higherTimeframe2 = chartSeconds <= 60 ? "60" : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "60" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "240" : chartSeconds <= 120 * 60 ? "D" : "W"
string higherTimeframe3 = chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "D" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "D" : chartSeconds <= 120 * 60 ? "W" : "M"
// كل طلب يقرأ آخر شمعة أعلى مكتملة فقط؛ الفاصل الحالي يبقى مصدر التنفيذ.
[higherTrendRaw1, higherResistance1, higherSupport1] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe1, f_closed_htf_context(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on, calc_bars_count = 320)
[higherTrendRaw2, higherResistance2, higherSupport2] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe2, f_closed_htf_context(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on, calc_bars_count = 320)
[higherTrendRaw3, higherResistance3, higherSupport3] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe3, f_closed_htf_context(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on, calc_bars_count = 320)
float higherTimeframeResistance = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, 0.0, higherResistance1, higherResistance2, higherResistance3, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float higherTimeframeSupport = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, 0.0, higherSupport1, higherSupport2, higherSupport3, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float priorHigherTimeframeResistance = f_nearest_price_level(true, nz(close[1], close), 0.0, higherResistance1[1], higherResistance2[1], higherResistance3[1], na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float priorHigherTimeframeSupport = f_nearest_price_level(false, nz(close[1], close), 0.0, higherSupport1[1], higherSupport2[1], higherSupport3[1], na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
f_higher_context_summary(int raw1, int raw2, int raw3) =>
    int state1 = raw1 == 9 ? 0 : raw1
    int state2 = raw2 == 9 ? 0 : raw2
    int state3 = raw3 == 9 ? 0 : raw3
    int readyContexts = (raw1 != 9 ? 1 : 0) + (raw2 != 9 ? 1 : 0) + (raw3 != 9 ? 1 : 0)
    int requiredContexts = timeframe.isweekly or timeframe.ismonthly ? 1 : 2
    [readyContexts >= requiredContexts, state1 * 3 + state2 * 2 + state3]

[higherContextsSufficient, higherWeightedScore] = f_higher_context_summary(higherTrendRaw1, higherTrendRaw2, higherTrendRaw3)
bool higherTimeframesReady = higherContextsSufficient

// Otherwise an anchor equal to the chart interval resets every bar and
// degenerates into the bar's own typical price. The daily chart uses a monthly anchor.
string automaticVwapAnchorTimeframe = chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? "D" : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? "W" : "M"
string requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe = automaticVwapAnchorTimeframe
int requestedVwapAnchorSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds(requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe))
string effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe = requestedVwapAnchorSeconds > chartSeconds ? requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe : higherTimeframe1
bool selectedVwapAnchor = barstate.isfirst or timeframe.change(effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe)

// =============================================================================
// Core calculations and optional filters
// =============================================================================

// Keep all history-dependent TA calls in one function that executes on every bar.
// This reduces TradingView's main-body bytecode without removing any analysis.
f_core_calculation_context() =>
    float atrValue = ta.atr(atrLength)
    float safeAtr = nz(atrValue, math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick))
    // Independent plan-management implementation of a volatility trail. These
    // series are calculated globally on every bar, but never create an entry.
    float postTargetTrailAtr = ta.atr(postTargetTrailLength)
    // ta.lowest over a fixed window could import a pre-entry extreme and jump the
    // trail unrealistically close to price immediately after T1.
    bool postTargetTrailReady = not na(postTargetTrailAtr)
    float sma24 = ta.sma(close, 24)
    float sma50 = ta.sma(close, 50)
    float sma200 = ta.sma(close, 200)
                int bullishMaVotes = (close > sma24 ? 1 : 0) + (sma24 > sma50 ? 1 : 0) + (sma50 > sma200 ? 1 : 0) + (close > sma200 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishMaVotes = (close < sma24 ? 1 : 0) + (sma24 < sma50 ? 1 : 0) + (sma50 < sma200 ? 1 : 0) + (close < sma200 ? 1 : 0)
    int movingAverageBias = bullishMaVotes >= 3 and bullishMaVotes >= bearishMaVotes + 2 ? 1 : bearishMaVotes >= 3 and bearishMaVotes >= bullishMaVotes + 2 ? -1 : 0
    float rsiValue = ta.rsi(close, rsiLength)
    [plusDi, minusDi, adxValue] = ta.dmi(14, 14)

    // SMA/RSI are merged later into one bounded recovery vote, not separate
    // independent votes, preventing duplicated momentum weighting.
    // The adaptive vote reuses the already validated SMA stack. This avoids a
    // duplicate momentum engine while preserving the same non-opposition gate.


    // Use only completed regular-session bars for RVOL. A fixed 6.5-hour
    // session assumption misaligns Saudi and other exchanges; the closed rolling
    // baseline is less brittle and volume is interpreted by event type below.
    float rollingVolumeAverage = ta.sma(session.ismarket ? volume : na, volumeLength)[1]
    bool regularSessionVolumeBar = session.ismarket
    float volumeAverage = rollingVolumeAverage
    bool volumeAvailable = regularSessionVolumeBar and not na(volume) and not na(volumeAverage) and volumeAverage > 0
    float relativeVolume = volumeAvailable ? volume / volumeAverage : na
    int volumeRegimeLength = math.max(30, volumeLength * 5)
    float volumeRegimeMean = ta.sma(relativeVolume[1], volumeRegimeLength)
    float volumeRegimeDeviation = ta.stdev(relativeVolume[1], volumeRegimeLength)
    float volumeZScore = volumeAvailable and not na(volumeRegimeMean) and volumeRegimeDeviation > 0 ? (relativeVolume - volumeRegimeMean) / volumeRegimeDeviation : na
    bool statisticalVolumeHigh = not na(volumeZScore) and volumeZScore >= 1.0
    bool statisticalVolumeExtreme = not na(volumeZScore) and volumeZScore >= 2.0
    int heatmapVolumeTier = na(volumeZScore) ? 0 : volumeZScore > 4.0 ? 5 : volumeZScore > 2.5 ? 4 : volumeZScore > 1.0 ? 3 : volumeZScore > -0.5 ? 2 : 1
    bool heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher = heatmapVolumeTier >= 3
    bool heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher = heatmapVolumeTier >= 4
    bool heatmapVolumeExtreme = heatmapVolumeTier >= 5
    bool standardVolumeEffort = relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher
    bool volumePass = not useVolumeFilter or (volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort)
    float analysisPreviousClose = ta.valuewhen(analysisSessionPass, close, 1)
    float analysisCloseChange = analysisSessionPass and not na(analysisPreviousClose) ? close - analysisPreviousClose : 0.0
    float analysisObvVolume = analysisSessionPass ? nz(volume, 0.0) : 0.0
    float obvValue = ta.cum(math.sign(analysisCloseChange) * analysisObvVolume)
    float obvAverage = ta.ema(obvValue, 14)
    float obvSlope = obvValue - nz(obvValue[5], obvValue)
    bool obvBullishPressure = obvValue > obvAverage and obvAverage > nz(obvAverage[2], obvAverage) and obvSlope > 0
    bool obvBearishPressure = obvValue < obvAverage and obvAverage < nz(obvAverage[2], obvAverage) and obvSlope < 0
    int obvTrendSignal = not volumeAvailable ? 0 : obvBullishPressure ? 1 : obvBearishPressure ? -1 : 0

    float vwapValue = ta.vwap(hlc3, selectedVwapAnchor)
