    // Structural VWAP is anchored by events calculated later in the script. The
    // entry gate therefore uses its last confirmed value, avoiding a circular same-
    // bar dependency. Until the first structural anchor is ready, periodic VWAP is
    // the explicit fallback.
    float entryVwapValue = vwapValue
    bool vwapLongPass = not useVwapFilter or (not na(entryVwapValue) and close > entryVwapValue)
    bool vwapShortPass = not useVwapFilter or (not na(entryVwapValue) and close < entryVwapValue)

    float candleRange = math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick)
    float bodyRatio = math.abs(close - open) / candleRange
    float averageSpread = ta.sma(candleRange, volumeLength)
    float relativeSpread = not na(averageSpread) and averageSpread > 0 ? candleRange / averageSpread : na
    float closeLocation = (close - low) / candleRange
    bool localVsaHighEffort = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort
    bool vsaWideResult = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread >= 1.20
    bool vsaNarrowResult = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread <= 0.75
    bool bullishVsaEffortResult = localVsaHighEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.65
    bool bearishVsaEffortResult = localVsaHighEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.35
    [atrValue, safeAtr, postTargetTrailAtr, postTargetTrailReady, sma24, sma50, sma200, movingAverageBias, rsiValue, plusDi, minusDi, adxValue, volumeAvailable, relativeVolume, statisticalVolumeHigh, statisticalVolumeExtreme, heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher, heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher, heatmapVolumeExtreme, standardVolumeEffort, volumePass, obvValue, obvTrendSignal, entryVwapValue, vwapLongPass, vwapShortPass, candleRange, bodyRatio, relativeSpread, closeLocation, vsaWideResult, vsaNarrowResult, bullishVsaEffortResult, bearishVsaEffortResult]

[atrValue, safeAtr, postTargetTrailAtr, postTargetTrailReady, sma24, sma50, sma200, movingAverageBias, rsiValue, plusDi, minusDi, adxValue, volumeAvailable, relativeVolume, statisticalVolumeHigh, statisticalVolumeExtreme, heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher, heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher, heatmapVolumeExtreme, standardVolumeEffort, volumePass, obvValue, obvTrendSignal, entryVwapValue, vwapLongPass, vwapShortPass, candleRange, bodyRatio, relativeSpread, closeLocation, vsaWideResult, vsaNarrowResult, bullishVsaEffortResult, bearishVsaEffortResult] = f_core_calculation_context()
bool vsaHighEffort = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort

// =============================================================================
// Dow structure: every strength uses a strict symmetric pivot. The candidate
// must be strictly above/below every candle on both sides; equal highs/lows do
// not become separate pivots. Only the latest confirmed pair is shown.
// =============================================================================
f_confirm_symmetric_pivots() =>
    float pivotHigh = na
    float pivotLow = na
    bool newPivotHigh = false
    bool newPivotLow = false
    float structureRightHigh = ta.highest(high, pivotBars)
    float structureLeftHigh = ta.highest(high[pivotBars + 1], pivotBars)
    float structureRightLow = ta.lowest(low, pivotBars)
    float structureLeftLow = ta.lowest(low[pivotBars + 1], pivotBars)
    bool strictPivotHigh = bar_index >= pivotBars * 2 and high[pivotBars] > structureRightHigh and high[pivotBars] > structureLeftHigh
    bool strictPivotLow = bar_index >= pivotBars * 2 and low[pivotBars] < structureRightLow and low[pivotBars] < structureLeftLow
    bool ambiguousStructurePivot = strictPivotHigh and strictPivotLow
    bool pivotSessionAllowed = analysisSessionPass[pivotBars]
    pivotHigh := strictPivotHigh and not ambiguousStructurePivot and pivotSessionAllowed ? high[pivotBars] : na
    pivotLow := strictPivotLow and not ambiguousStructurePivot and pivotSessionAllowed ? low[pivotBars] : na
    newPivotHigh := stockChartAllowed and not na(pivotHigh) and barstate.isconfirmed
    newPivotLow := stockChartAllowed and not na(pivotLow) and barstate.isconfirmed

    [pivotHigh, pivotLow, newPivotHigh, newPivotLow]

[pivotHigh, pivotLow, newPivotHigh, newPivotLow] = f_confirm_symmetric_pivots()
var float lastSwingHigh = na
var float lastSwingLow = na
var int lastSwingHighBar = na
var int lastSwingLowBar = na
var float lastPivotHighRsi = na
var float lastPivotLowRsi = na
var float lastPivotHighObv = na
var float lastPivotLowObv = na
var string lastHighTag = "-"
var string lastLowTag = "-"
var bool highWasBroken = false
var bool lowWasBroken = false
var bool highWasSwept = false
var bool lowWasSwept = false

var array<float> srHighPivotPrices = array.new<float>()
var array<int> srHighPivotBars = array.new<int>()
var array<int> srHighPivotTimes = array.new<int>()
var array<bool> srHighPivotBroken = array.new<bool>()
var array<float> srLowPivotPrices = array.new<float>()
var array<int> srLowPivotBars = array.new<int>()
var array<int> srLowPivotTimes = array.new<int>()
var array<bool> srLowPivotBroken = array.new<bool>()
var float strongResistanceLow = na
var float strongResistanceHigh = na
var float strongSupportLow = na
var float strongSupportHigh = na
var float secondaryResistanceLow = na
var float secondaryResistanceHigh = na
var float secondarySupportLow = na
var float secondarySupportHigh = na

bool bearishRsiDivergence = false
bool bullishRsiDivergence = false
bool bearishObvDivergence = false
bool bullishObvDivergence = false

float pivotTolerance = nz(atrValue[pivotBars], safeAtr) * equalAtrPart

if newPivotHigh
    int pivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
    float confirmedPivotRsi = rsiValue[pivotBars]
    float confirmedPivotObv = obvValue[pivotBars]
    array.unshift(srHighPivotPrices, pivotHigh)
    array.unshift(srHighPivotBars, pivotBar)
    array.unshift(srHighPivotTimes, time[pivotBars])
    array.unshift(srHighPivotBroken, false)
    while array.size(srHighPivotPrices) > srHistoricalArchiveLimit
        array.pop(srHighPivotPrices)
        array.pop(srHighPivotBars)
        array.pop(srHighPivotTimes)
        array.pop(srHighPivotBroken)
    bearishObvDivergence := volumeAvailable and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastPivotHighObv) and pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotObv < lastPivotHighObv
    bearishRsiDivergence := useRsiDivergence and not na(lastSwingHigh) and pivotHigh >= lastSwingHigh - pivotTolerance and ((not na(lastPivotHighRsi) and confirmedPivotRsi <= lastPivotHighRsi - rsiDivergenceGap) or bearishObvDivergence)
    string highTag = na(lastSwingHigh) ? "H" : pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + pivotTolerance ? "HH" : pivotHigh < lastSwingHigh - pivotTolerance ? "LH" : "EH"
    lastSwingHigh := pivotHigh
    lastSwingHighBar := pivotBar
    lastPivotHighRsi := confirmedPivotRsi
    lastPivotHighObv := confirmedPivotObv
    lastHighTag := highTag
    // A strict symmetric pivot is above every right-side confirmation bar,
    // therefore it cannot already be closed through or swept when confirmed.
    highWasBroken := false
    highWasSwept := false
    f_clear_pending_break(0)


if newPivotLow
    int pivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
    float confirmedPivotRsi = rsiValue[pivotBars]
    float confirmedPivotObv = obvValue[pivotBars]
    array.unshift(srLowPivotPrices, pivotLow)
    array.unshift(srLowPivotBars, pivotBar)
    array.unshift(srLowPivotTimes, time[pivotBars])
    array.unshift(srLowPivotBroken, false)
    while array.size(srLowPivotPrices) > srHistoricalArchiveLimit
        array.pop(srLowPivotPrices)
        array.pop(srLowPivotBars)
        array.pop(srLowPivotTimes)
        array.pop(srLowPivotBroken)
    bullishObvDivergence := volumeAvailable and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastPivotLowObv) and pivotLow < lastSwingLow - pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotObv > lastPivotLowObv
    bullishRsiDivergence := useRsiDivergence and not na(lastSwingLow) and pivotLow <= lastSwingLow + pivotTolerance and ((not na(lastPivotLowRsi) and confirmedPivotRsi >= lastPivotLowRsi + rsiDivergenceGap) or bullishObvDivergence)
    string lowTag = na(lastSwingLow) ? "L" : pivotLow > lastSwingLow + pivotTolerance ? "HL" : pivotLow < lastSwingLow - pivotTolerance ? "LL" : "EL"
    lastSwingLow := pivotLow
    lastSwingLowBar := pivotBar
    lastPivotLowRsi := confirmedPivotRsi
    lastPivotLowObv := confirmedPivotObv
    lastLowTag := lowTag
    lowWasBroken := false
    lowWasSwept := false
    f_clear_pending_break(1)

// OBV divergence participates in the combined pivot-divergence pulse above.
int obvSignal = obvTrendSignal


// Recalculate on a new confirmed pivot or exactly when a stored touch ages out.
// Avoid price-clustering loops on every bar.
var int nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime = na
var float lastHorizontalSrSelectionClose = na
bool horizontalSrAgeRefresh = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and not na(nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime) and time >= nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime
bool horizontalSrRecalculated = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and (newPivotHigh or newPivotLow or horizontalSrAgeRefresh)
float horizontalSrPriceFloor = close * (chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 0.0025 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 0.0040 : 0.0060)
float horizontalSrPriceCap = close * (chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 0.0115 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 0.0150 : 0.0200)
float horizontalSrTolerance = math.min(math.max(safeAtr * srAtrTolerance, math.max(horizontalSrPriceFloor, syminfo.mintick * 8.0)), math.max(horizontalSrPriceCap, syminfo.mintick * 8.0))
bool resistanceSelectionStale = (not na(strongResistanceHigh) and close > strongResistanceHigh + horizontalSrTolerance) or (not na(secondaryResistanceHigh) and close > secondaryResistanceHigh + horizontalSrTolerance)
bool supportSelectionStale = (not na(strongSupportLow) and close < strongSupportLow - horizontalSrTolerance) or (not na(secondarySupportLow) and close < secondarySupportLow - horizontalSrTolerance)
bool horizontalSrPriceRefresh = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and (resistanceSelectionStale or supportSelectionStale)
// The stored geometry never moves. This bounded refresh only reselects which already-confirmed cluster is nearest after price travels materially.
float horizontalSrMovementThreshold = math.max(safeAtr, horizontalSrTolerance * 2.0)
bool horizontalSrMovementRefresh = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and not na(lastHorizontalSrSelectionClose) and math.abs(close - lastHorizontalSrSelectionClose) >= horizontalSrMovementThreshold
horizontalSrRecalculated := horizontalSrRecalculated or horizontalSrPriceRefresh or horizontalSrMovementRefresh
int horizontalSrMinimumTouchSeparationBars = math.max(pivotBars + 1, 3)
if horizontalSrRecalculated
    [resistanceLowCandidate, resistanceHighCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, srStoredPivots, horizontalSrMinimumTouchSeparationBars, true, close, na, na)
    [supportLowCandidate, supportHighCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, srStoredPivots, horizontalSrMinimumTouchSeparationBars, false, close, na, na)
    [secondaryResistanceLowCandidate, secondaryResistanceHighCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, srStoredPivots, horizontalSrMinimumTouchSeparationBars, true, close, resistanceLowCandidate, resistanceHighCandidate)
    [secondarySupportLowCandidate, secondarySupportHighCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, srStoredPivots, horizontalSrMinimumTouchSeparationBars, false, close, supportLowCandidate, supportHighCandidate)
    strongResistanceLow := resistanceLowCandidate
    strongResistanceHigh := resistanceHighCandidate
    strongSupportLow := supportLowCandidate
    strongSupportHigh := supportHighCandidate
    secondaryResistanceLow := secondaryResistanceLowCandidate
    secondaryResistanceHigh := secondaryResistanceHighCandidate
    secondarySupportLow := secondarySupportLowCandidate
    secondarySupportHigh := secondarySupportHighCandidate
    lastHorizontalSrSelectionClose := close
    int nextResistanceExpiryTime = f_next_sr_expiry(srHighPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    int nextSupportExpiryTime = f_next_sr_expiry(srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime := na(nextResistanceExpiryTime) ? nextSupportExpiryTime : na(nextSupportExpiryTime) ? nextResistanceExpiryTime : math.min(nextResistanceExpiryTime, nextSupportExpiryTime)

int dowBias = lastHighTag == "HH" and lastLowTag == "HL" ? 1 : lastHighTag == "LH" and lastLowTag == "LL" ? -1 : 0
int barsSinceBullishDivergence = ta.barssince(bullishRsiDivergence)
int barsSinceBearishDivergence = ta.barssince(bearishRsiDivergence)
bool recentBullishDivergence = not na(barsSinceBullishDivergence) and barsSinceBullishDivergence <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishDivergence = not na(barsSinceBearishDivergence) and barsSinceBearishDivergence <= contextLookback
// =============================================================================
// Confirmed breaks, Wyckoff sweeps, and classical retests
// =============================================================================
// Break quality and horizontal S/R preparation are evaluated on every bar in
// a dedicated scope to keep the main program below TradingView's body limit.
f_break_and_sr_context() =>
    float breakBuffer = safeAtr * breakAtrBuffer
    float breakHoldBuffer = math.max(breakBuffer * 0.50, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
    float breakGapBuffer = math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
    float breakLowerWickShare = math.max(math.min(open, close) - low, 0.0) / candleRange
    float breakUpperWickShare = math.max(high - math.max(open, close), 0.0) / candleRange
    bool breakVolumeHigh = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= 1.50 or statisticalVolumeHigh and relativeVolume >= 1.25)
    bool bullishVolumeImpulse = breakVolumeHigh and candleRange >= safeAtr * 0.95 and bodyRatio >= 0.60 and closeLocation >= 0.78 and breakUpperWickShare <= 0.20
    bool bearishVolumeImpulse = breakVolumeHigh and candleRange >= safeAtr * 0.95 and bodyRatio >= 0.60 and closeLocation <= 0.22 and breakLowerWickShare <= 0.20
    bool bullishGapHold = not na(high[1]) and open > high[1] + breakGapBuffer and close > open and close > high[1] + breakGapBuffer * 0.75 and candleRange >= safeAtr * 0.65 and bodyRatio >= 0.45 and closeLocation >= 0.68 and breakUpperWickShare <= 0.25
    bool bearishGapHold = not na(low[1]) and open < low[1] - breakGapBuffer and close < open and close < low[1] - breakGapBuffer * 0.75 and candleRange >= safeAtr * 0.65 and bodyRatio >= 0.45 and closeLocation <= 0.32 and breakLowerWickShare <= 0.25
    float displacementAtrFloor = volumeAvailable ? (relativeVolume < 0.90 ? 1.45 : 1.30) : 1.30
    bool bullishDisplacement = candleRange >= safeAtr * displacementAtrFloor and bodyRatio >= 0.65 and closeLocation >= 0.80 and breakUpperWickShare <= 0.20
    bool bearishDisplacement = candleRange >= safeAtr * displacementAtrFloor and bodyRatio >= 0.65 and closeLocation <= 0.20 and breakLowerWickShare <= 0.20
    bool bullishExceptionalBreakBar = barstate.isconfirmed and not (breakUpperWickShare >= 0.30 and breakLowerWickShare >= 0.30) and (bullishVolumeImpulse or bullishGapHold or bullishDisplacement)
    bool bearishExceptionalBreakBar = barstate.isconfirmed and not (breakUpperWickShare >= 0.30 and breakLowerWickShare >= 0.30) and (bearishVolumeImpulse or bearishGapHold or bearishDisplacement)


    bool rawStrongResistanceReady = useHorizontalSr and not na(strongResistanceLow) and not na(strongResistanceHigh)
    bool rawStrongSupportReady = useHorizontalSr and not na(strongSupportLow) and not na(strongSupportHigh)
    float srZoneMaximumDistance = safeAtr * 8.0
    float srZoneExclusionTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.15, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    bool primaryHorizontalSrConflict = rawStrongResistanceReady and rawStrongSupportReady and f_zones_overlap(strongResistanceLow, strongResistanceHigh, strongSupportLow, strongSupportHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
    bool strongResistanceReady = rawStrongResistanceReady and not primaryHorizontalSrConflict
    bool strongSupportReady = rawStrongSupportReady and not primaryHorizontalSrConflict
    bool secondaryResistanceReady = useHorizontalSr and not na(secondaryResistanceLow) and not na(secondaryResistanceHigh) and (not rawStrongSupportReady or not f_zones_overlap(secondaryResistanceLow, secondaryResistanceHigh, strongSupportLow, strongSupportHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance))
    bool secondarySupportReady = useHorizontalSr and not na(secondarySupportLow) and not na(secondarySupportHigh) and (not rawStrongResistanceReady or not f_zones_overlap(secondarySupportLow, secondarySupportHigh, strongResistanceLow, strongResistanceHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance))
    bool secondaryHorizontalSrConflict = secondaryResistanceReady and secondarySupportReady and f_zones_overlap(secondaryResistanceLow, secondaryResistanceHigh, secondarySupportLow, secondarySupportHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
    secondaryResistanceReady := secondaryResistanceReady and not secondaryHorizontalSrConflict
    secondarySupportReady := secondarySupportReady and not secondaryHorizontalSrConflict
    [nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh] = f_nearest_sr_zone(true, srZoneMaximumDistance, srZoneExclusionTolerance, strongResistanceReady ? strongResistanceLow : na, strongResistanceReady ? strongResistanceHigh : na, secondaryResistanceReady ? secondaryResistanceLow : na, secondaryResistanceReady ? secondaryResistanceHigh : na, na, na)
    [nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh] = f_nearest_sr_zone(false, srZoneMaximumDistance, srZoneExclusionTolerance, strongSupportReady ? strongSupportLow : na, strongSupportReady ? strongSupportHigh : na, secondarySupportReady ? secondarySupportLow : na, secondarySupportReady ? secondarySupportHigh : na, na, na)
    [nextResistanceZoneLow, nextResistanceZoneHigh] = f_nearest_sr_zone(true, srZoneMaximumDistance, srZoneExclusionTolerance, strongResistanceReady ? strongResistanceLow : na, strongResistanceReady ? strongResistanceHigh : na, secondaryResistanceReady ? secondaryResistanceLow : na, secondaryResistanceReady ? secondaryResistanceHigh : na, nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh)
    [nextSupportZoneLow, nextSupportZoneHigh] = f_nearest_sr_zone(false, srZoneMaximumDistance, srZoneExclusionTolerance, strongSupportReady ? strongSupportLow : na, strongSupportReady ? strongSupportHigh : na, secondarySupportReady ? secondarySupportLow : na, secondarySupportReady ? secondarySupportHigh : na, nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh)
    [breakBuffer, breakHoldBuffer, breakLowerWickShare, breakUpperWickShare, bullishExceptionalBreakBar, bearishExceptionalBreakBar, srZoneMaximumDistance, srZoneExclusionTolerance, strongResistanceReady, strongSupportReady, secondaryResistanceReady, secondarySupportReady, nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh, nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh, nextResistanceZoneLow, nextResistanceZoneHigh, nextSupportZoneLow, nextSupportZoneHigh]

[breakBuffer, breakHoldBuffer, breakLowerWickShare, breakUpperWickShare, bullishExceptionalBreakBar, bearishExceptionalBreakBar, srZoneMaximumDistance, srZoneExclusionTolerance, strongResistanceReady, strongSupportReady, secondaryResistanceReady, secondarySupportReady, nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh, nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh, nextResistanceZoneLow, nextResistanceZoneHigh, nextSupportZoneLow, nextSupportZoneHigh] = f_break_and_sr_context()

// Breaks are tied to the nearest zone that was actually facing price on the
// prior bar. Freeze that exact geometry so a recalculation cannot move the
// retest, stop or role-reversal reference to a different cluster.
float resistanceBreakCandidateLow = nearestResistanceZoneLow[1]
float resistanceBreakCandidateLevel = nearestResistanceZoneHigh[1]
float supportBreakCandidateLevel = nearestSupportZoneLow[1]
float supportBreakCandidateHigh = nearestSupportZoneHigh[1]
[strongResistanceBreakAttempt, strongResistanceBreak, confirmedResistanceBreakLevel, confirmedResistanceBreakLow, _, _] = f_static_break_state(2, true, resistanceBreakCandidateLevel, resistanceBreakCandidateLow, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
[strongSupportBreakAttempt, strongSupportBreak, confirmedSupportBreakLevel, confirmedSupportBreakHigh, _, _] = f_static_break_state(3, false, supportBreakCandidateLevel, supportBreakCandidateHigh, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
// A single major historical pivot is structurally important even when it has
// not accumulated enough touches to become a cluster.
int historicalMinimumAgeBars = math.max(pivotBars * 4, timeframe.isintraday ? 24 : 12)
// لا يمكن اختراق مقاومة بالإغلاق إلا على شمعة ارتفع إغلاقها بأكثر من هامش الكسر،
// ولا يمكن كسر دعم إلا على شمعة هبط إغلاقها بالمقدار نفسه. هذا الشرط يمنع
// مسح مئات المحاور التاريخية على كل شمعة من دون تغيير معنى الحدث.
bool historicalResistanceScanNeeded = barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and (close > close[1] or bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool historicalSupportScanNeeded = barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and (close < close[1] or bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
float priorHistoricalResistance = historicalResistanceScanNeeded ? f_nearest_unbroken_historical_pivot(true, close[1], 0.0, srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, srHighPivotBroken, historicalMinimumAgeBars, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds) : na
float priorHistoricalSupport = historicalSupportScanNeeded ? f_nearest_unbroken_historical_pivot(false, close[1], 0.0, srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, srLowPivotBroken, historicalMinimumAgeBars, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds) : na
// كسر حقيقي فقط: الإغلاق السابق على الجهة الأصلية، والإغلاق الحالي خلف المستوى مع الهامش.
[historicalResistanceAttempt, historicalResistanceBreak, confirmedHistoricalResistance, _, _, confirmedHistoricalResistanceBuffer] = f_static_break_state(4, true, historicalResistanceScanNeeded ? priorHistoricalResistance : na, priorHistoricalResistance, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
[historicalSupportAttempt, historicalSupportBreak, confirmedHistoricalSupport, _, _, confirmedHistoricalSupportBuffer] = f_static_break_state(5, false, historicalSupportScanNeeded ? priorHistoricalSupport : na, priorHistoricalSupport, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)

// لا نحدّث حالة الأرشيف إلا عند وقوع كسر تاريخي فعلي. في حالة الفجوة
// تُستهلك جميع المحاور التي تجاوزها الإغلاق في دورة واحدة.
if historicalResistanceBreak
    int highHistoryCount = math.min(array.size(srHighPivotPrices), array.size(srHighPivotBroken))
    if highHistoryCount > 0
        for historyIndex = 0 to highHistoryCount - 1
            float archivedHighLevel = array.get(srHighPivotPrices, historyIndex)
            // Archive de-duplication follows the already-confirmed event. Do not
            // re-test it with a newly changed ATR buffer after the frozen attempt.
            float archivedHighBuffer = math.max(nz(confirmedHistoricalResistanceBuffer, breakBuffer), syminfo.mintick)
            bool archivedHighBroken = bullishExceptionalBreakBar ? close > archivedHighLevel + archivedHighBuffer : close > archivedHighLevel + archivedHighBuffer and close[1] > archivedHighLevel + archivedHighBuffer
            if not array.get(srHighPivotBroken, historyIndex) and archivedHighBroken
                array.set(srHighPivotBroken, historyIndex, true)
if historicalSupportBreak
    int lowHistoryCount = math.min(array.size(srLowPivotPrices), array.size(srLowPivotBroken))
    if lowHistoryCount > 0
        for historyIndex = 0 to lowHistoryCount - 1
            float archivedLowLevel = array.get(srLowPivotPrices, historyIndex)
            float archivedLowBuffer = math.max(nz(confirmedHistoricalSupportBuffer, breakBuffer), syminfo.mintick)
            bool archivedLowBroken = bearishExceptionalBreakBar ? close < archivedLowLevel - archivedLowBuffer : close < archivedLowLevel - archivedLowBuffer and close[1] < archivedLowLevel - archivedLowBuffer
            if not array.get(srLowPivotBroken, historyIndex) and archivedLowBroken
                array.set(srLowPivotBroken, historyIndex, true)
float horizontalSrRetestTolerance = safeAtr * retestAtrTolerance
var int strongResistanceBreakContextBar = na
var int strongSupportBreakContextBar = na
var float brokenResistanceLow = na
var float brokenResistanceHigh = na
var float brokenSupportLow = na
var float brokenSupportHigh = na
if strongResistanceBreak
    f_clear_pending_break(10)
    strongResistanceBreakContextBar := bar_index
    brokenResistanceLow := confirmedResistanceBreakLow
    brokenResistanceHigh := confirmedResistanceBreakLevel
if strongSupportBreak
    f_clear_pending_break(11)
    strongSupportBreakContextBar := bar_index
    brokenSupportLow := confirmedSupportBreakLevel
    brokenSupportHigh := confirmedSupportBreakHigh
[_, strongResistanceRoleFailed, _, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(10, false, not na(strongResistanceBreakContextBar) ? brokenResistanceLow : na, brokenResistanceHigh, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
[_, strongSupportRoleFailed, _, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(11, true, not na(strongSupportBreakContextBar) ? brokenSupportHigh : na, brokenSupportLow, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
if strongResistanceRoleFailed
    strongResistanceBreakContextBar := na
    brokenResistanceLow := na
    brokenResistanceHigh := na
if strongSupportRoleFailed
    strongSupportBreakContextBar := na
    brokenSupportLow := na
    brokenSupportHigh := na
bool strongResistanceRoleReversed = not na(strongResistanceBreakContextBar)
bool strongSupportRoleReversed = not na(strongSupportBreakContextBar)
bool recentStrongResistanceBreak = strongResistanceRoleReversed and bar_index - strongResistanceBreakContextBar <= contextLookback
bool recentStrongSupportBreak = strongSupportRoleReversed and bar_index - strongSupportBreakContextBar <= contextLookback
bool touchingStrongResistanceCluster = not na(nearestResistanceZoneLow) and high >= nearestResistanceZoneLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance and low <= nearestResistanceZoneHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance
bool touchingStrongSupportCluster = not na(nearestSupportZoneLow) and low <= nearestSupportZoneHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance and high >= nearestSupportZoneLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance
bool touchingBrokenResistanceZone = strongResistanceRoleReversed and high >= brokenResistanceLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance and low <= brokenResistanceHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance
bool touchingBrokenSupportZone = strongSupportRoleReversed and low <= brokenSupportHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance and high >= brokenSupportLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance
bool resistanceClusterActsAsSupport = touchingBrokenResistanceZone and bar_index > strongResistanceBreakContextBar and close >= brokenResistanceHigh - breakBuffer
bool supportClusterActsAsResistance = touchingBrokenSupportZone and bar_index > strongSupportBreakContextBar and close <= brokenSupportLow + breakBuffer
bool nearStrongSupport = touchingStrongSupportCluster and close >= nearestSupportZoneLow - breakBuffer
bool nearStrongResistance = touchingStrongResistanceCluster and close <= nearestResistanceZoneHigh + breakBuffer
bool touchingNextSupportZone = not na(nextSupportZoneLow) and not na(nextSupportZoneHigh) and low <= nextSupportZoneHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance and high >= nextSupportZoneLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance and close >= nextSupportZoneLow - breakBuffer
bool touchingNextResistanceZone = not na(nextResistanceZoneLow) and not na(nextResistanceZoneHigh) and high >= nextResistanceZoneLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance and low <= nextResistanceZoneHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance and close <= nextResistanceZoneHigh + breakBuffer
bool nearAnySupportZone = nearStrongSupport or touchingNextSupportZone
bool nearAnyResistanceZone = nearStrongResistance or touchingNextResistanceZone
bool resistanceBreakHolding = recentStrongResistanceBreak and close >= brokenResistanceHigh - breakBuffer
bool supportBreakHolding = recentStrongSupportBreak and close <= brokenSupportLow + breakBuffer
bool localBullishSrEvent = historicalResistanceBreak or resistanceBreakHolding or resistanceClusterActsAsSupport
bool localBearishSrEvent = historicalSupportBreak or supportBreakHolding or supportClusterActsAsResistance
int localHorizontalSrSignal = localBullishSrEvent and not localBearishSrEvent ? 1 : localBearishSrEvent and not localBullishSrEvent ? -1 : not localBullishSrEvent and not localBearishSrEvent and nearAnySupportZone and not nearAnyResistanceZone ? 1 : not localBullishSrEvent and not localBearishSrEvent and nearAnyResistanceZone and not nearAnySupportZone ? -1 : 0
float higherTimeframeSrTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 5.0)
bool touchingHigherResistance = not na(higherTimeframeResistance) and high >= higherTimeframeResistance - higherTimeframeSrTolerance and low <= higherTimeframeResistance + higherTimeframeSrTolerance
bool touchingHigherSupport = not na(higherTimeframeSupport) and low <= higherTimeframeSupport + higherTimeframeSrTolerance and high >= higherTimeframeSupport - higherTimeframeSrTolerance
[higherResistanceBreakAttempt, higherResistanceBreak, confirmedHigherResistance, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(6, true, priorHigherTimeframeResistance, priorHigherTimeframeResistance, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
[higherSupportBreakAttempt, higherSupportBreak, confirmedHigherSupport, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(7, false, priorHigherTimeframeSupport, priorHigherTimeframeSupport, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
int higherTimeframeSrSignal = higherResistanceBreak and not higherSupportBreak ? 1 : higherSupportBreak and not higherResistanceBreak ? -1 : not higherResistanceBreak and not higherSupportBreak and touchingHigherSupport and touchingHigherResistance ? 0 : touchingHigherSupport and close >= higherTimeframeSupport ? 1 : touchingHigherResistance and close <= higherTimeframeResistance ? -1 : 0
bool horizontalSrTimeframeConflict = localHorizontalSrSignal != 0 and higherTimeframeSrSignal != 0 and localHorizontalSrSignal != higherTimeframeSrSignal
int horizontalSrSignal = horizontalSrTimeframeConflict ? 0 : higherTimeframeSrSignal != 0 ? higherTimeframeSrSignal : localHorizontalSrSignal
// Old clusters that are far from price are context, not current evidence and not
// useful chart drawings. Five ATR keeps nearby structure without cluttering.
float horizontalSrVisualDistance = safeAtr * 5.0
var line strongResistanceVisualLine = na
var line strongSupportVisualLine = na
var line resistanceZone1TopLine = na
var line resistanceZone1BottomLine = na
var linefill resistanceZone1Fill = na

float cleanLevelMinimumDistance = syminfo.mintick * 2.0
float storedResistanceVisual1 = barstate.islast ? f_nearest_stored_pivot(true, close, cleanLevelMinimumDistance, horizontalSrVisualDistance, srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds) : na
float storedSupportVisual1 = barstate.islast ? f_nearest_stored_pivot(false, close, cleanLevelMinimumDistance, horizontalSrVisualDistance, srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds) : na
float fallbackResistanceLevel = barstate.islast ? f_nearest_price_level(true, close, cleanLevelMinimumDistance, higherTimeframeResistance, storedResistanceVisual1, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : na
float fallbackSupportLevel = barstate.islast ? f_nearest_price_level(false, close, cleanLevelMinimumDistance, higherTimeframeSupport, storedSupportVisual1, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : na
bool fallbackResistanceOutsideZones = barstate.islast and not na(fallbackResistanceLevel) and not f_level_inside_sr_zones(fallbackResistanceLevel, nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh, nextResistanceZoneLow, nextResistanceZoneHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
bool fallbackSupportOutsideZones = barstate.islast and not na(fallbackSupportLevel) and not f_level_inside_sr_zones(fallbackSupportLevel, nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh, nextSupportZoneLow, nextSupportZoneHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
int cleanLevelStartBar = bar_index - math.min(timeframe.isintraday ? 48 : timeframe.isdaily ? 24 : timeframe.isweekly ? 12 : 6, bar_index)
int resistanceZone1StartBar = barstate.islast ? f_sr_visual_anchor_bar(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, cleanLevelStartBar) : cleanLevelStartBar
int supportZone1StartBar = barstate.islast ? f_sr_visual_anchor_bar(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, cleanLevelStartBar) : cleanLevelStartBar
int fallbackResistanceStartBar = barstate.islast ? f_sr_visual_anchor_bar(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, fallbackResistanceLevel, fallbackResistanceLevel, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, cleanLevelStartBar) : cleanLevelStartBar
int fallbackSupportStartBar = barstate.islast ? f_sr_visual_anchor_bar(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, fallbackSupportLevel, fallbackSupportLevel, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, cleanLevelStartBar) : cleanLevelStartBar
bool srVisualAllowed = stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr
bool rawResistanceZoneVisible = srVisualAllowed and not na(nearestResistanceZoneLow) and not na(nearestResistanceZoneHigh)
bool rawSupportZoneVisible = srVisualAllowed and not na(nearestSupportZoneLow) and not na(nearestSupportZoneHigh)
bool horizontalSrDisplayConflict = rawResistanceZoneVisible and rawSupportZoneVisible and f_zones_overlap(nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestResistanceZoneHigh, nearestSupportZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
float horizontalConflictLow = horizontalSrDisplayConflict ? math.min(nearestResistanceZoneLow, nearestSupportZoneLow) : na
float horizontalConflictHigh = horizontalSrDisplayConflict ? math.max(nearestResistanceZoneHigh, nearestSupportZoneHigh) : na
bool horizontalConflictActsAsSupport = horizontalSrDisplayConflict and close > horizontalConflictHigh + breakBuffer
bool horizontalConflictActsAsResistance = horizontalSrDisplayConflict and close < horizontalConflictLow - breakBuffer
bool horizontalConflictIsPivot = horizontalSrDisplayConflict and not horizontalConflictActsAsSupport and not horizontalConflictActsAsResistance
bool resistanceZoneDisplayVisible = rawResistanceZoneVisible and not horizontalSrDisplayConflict or horizontalConflictActsAsResistance or horizontalConflictIsPivot
bool supportZoneDisplayVisible = rawSupportZoneVisible and not horizontalSrDisplayConflict or horizontalConflictActsAsSupport
float resistanceZoneDisplayLow = horizontalSrDisplayConflict and (horizontalConflictActsAsResistance or horizontalConflictIsPivot) ? horizontalConflictLow : nearestResistanceZoneLow
float resistanceZoneDisplayHigh = horizontalSrDisplayConflict and (horizontalConflictActsAsResistance or horizontalConflictIsPivot) ? horizontalConflictHigh : nearestResistanceZoneHigh
float supportZoneDisplayLow = horizontalConflictActsAsSupport ? horizontalConflictLow : nearestSupportZoneLow
float supportZoneDisplayHigh = horizontalConflictActsAsSupport ? horizontalConflictHigh : nearestSupportZoneHigh
int resistanceZoneDisplayStartBar = horizontalSrDisplayConflict ? math.min(resistanceZone1StartBar, supportZone1StartBar) : resistanceZone1StartBar
int supportZoneDisplayStartBar = horizontalConflictActsAsSupport ? math.min(resistanceZone1StartBar, supportZone1StartBar) : supportZone1StartBar
color resistanceZoneDisplayColor = horizontalConflictIsPivot ? #94A3B8 : color.white
string resistanceZoneDisplayCaption = horizontalConflictIsPivot ? "منطقة محورية" : "مقاومة"
string supportZoneDisplayCaption = "دعم"
if barstate.islast
    [nextResistanceZone1Top, nextResistanceZone1Bottom, nextResistanceZone1Fill] = f_update_zone_visual(resistanceZone1TopLine, resistanceZone1BottomLine, resistanceZone1Fill, resistanceZoneDisplayVisible, resistanceZoneDisplayStartBar, bar_index + channelForwardBars, resistanceZoneDisplayHigh, resistanceZoneDisplayLow, resistanceZoneDisplayColor)
    resistanceZone1TopLine := nextResistanceZone1Top
    resistanceZone1BottomLine := nextResistanceZone1Bottom
    resistanceZone1Fill := nextResistanceZone1Fill
    [nextSupportZone1Top, nextSupportZone1Bottom, nextSupportZone1Fill] = f_update_zone_visual(supportZone1TopLine, supportZone1BottomLine, supportZone1Fill, supportZoneDisplayVisible, supportZoneDisplayStartBar, bar_index + channelForwardBars, supportZoneDisplayHigh, supportZoneDisplayLow, #F59E0B)
    supportZone1TopLine := nextSupportZone1Top
    supportZone1BottomLine := nextSupportZone1Bottom
    supportZone1Fill := nextSupportZone1Fill

// not count its internal statements against the main-body compilation limit.
[majorChannelBias, majorTrendReady, majorChannelReady, majorChannelBaseNow, majorChannelLowerNow, majorChannelUpperNow, majorChannelWidth, majorFirstAnchorBar, majorFirstAnchorPrice, majorSecondAnchorBar, majorSecondAnchorPrice, majorBreakoutUpEvent, majorBreakoutDownEvent, _, _, majorChannelUpperFakeout, majorChannelLowerFakeout, majorChannelSignal, bullishMajorChannelTarget, bearishMajorChannelTarget] = f_calculate_major_layer(majorChannelPivotBars, channelValidationMaxBars, safeAtr, breakAtrBuffer, trendZoneAtrWidth, channelMaxAtrProjection, channelMaximumSlopeAtr, channelMinimumWidthConsistency, dowBias, breakBuffer, fakeoutWindow, retestWindow, channelForwardBars, bullishExceptionalBreakBar, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)

// قناة موحّدة: محرك القناة الرئيسية المؤكد يخدم الاتجاه والحدين العلوي والسفلي.
// أزيلت القناة الثانوية المكررة لتجاوز حد IL tokens دون حذف تحليل القنوات.
f_compact_trend_context() =>
    float lastImportantHigh = array.size(srHighPivotPrices) > 0 ? array.get(srHighPivotPrices, 0) : na
    float previousImportantHigh = array.size(srHighPivotPrices) > 1 ? array.get(srHighPivotPrices, 1) : na
    int lastImportantHighBar = array.size(srHighPivotBars) > 0 ? array.get(srHighPivotBars, 0) : na
    int previousImportantHighBar = array.size(srHighPivotBars) > 1 ? array.get(srHighPivotBars, 1) : na
    float lastImportantLow = array.size(srLowPivotPrices) > 0 ? array.get(srLowPivotPrices, 0) : na
    float previousImportantLow = array.size(srLowPivotPrices) > 1 ? array.get(srLowPivotPrices, 1) : na
    int lastImportantLowBar = array.size(srLowPivotBars) > 0 ? array.get(srLowPivotBars, 0) : na
    int previousImportantLowBar = array.size(srLowPivotBars) > 1 ? array.get(srLowPivotBars, 1) : na
    bool newImportantHighAnchor = barstate.isconfirmed and (f_int_changed(lastImportantHighBar[1], lastImportantHighBar) or f_int_changed(previousImportantHighBar[1], previousImportantHighBar))
    bool newImportantLowAnchor = barstate.isconfirmed and (f_int_changed(lastImportantLowBar[1], lastImportantLowBar) or f_int_changed(previousImportantLowBar[1], previousImportantLowBar))
    float compactHighSlope = not na(previousImportantHighBar) and not na(lastImportantHighBar) and lastImportantHighBar > previousImportantHighBar ? (lastImportantHigh - previousImportantHigh) / float(lastImportantHighBar - previousImportantHighBar) : na
    float compactLowSlope = not na(previousImportantLowBar) and not na(lastImportantLowBar) and lastImportantLowBar > previousImportantLowBar ? (lastImportantLow - previousImportantLow) / float(lastImportantLowBar - previousImportantLowBar) : na
    bool convergingImportantStructure = not na(compactHighSlope) and not na(compactLowSlope) and compactHighSlope < 0 and compactLowSlope > 0
    bool trendReady = majorTrendReady
    bool channelReady = majorChannelReady
    float channelLowerNow = majorChannelLowerNow
    float channelUpperNow = majorChannelUpperNow
    bool channelUpperFakeout = majorChannelUpperFakeout
    bool channelLowerFakeout = majorChannelLowerFakeout
    float channelFakeoutLevel = channelUpperFakeout ? channelUpperNow : channelLowerFakeout ? channelLowerNow : na
    [bullishTrendlineRetest, bearishTrendlineRetest, watchedBreakBoundaryNow, bullishTrendlineBreakEvent, bearishTrendlineBreakEvent] = f_trendline_retest_state(majorTrendReady, majorChannelBias, majorChannelBaseNow, majorFirstAnchorBar, majorFirstAnchorPrice, majorSecondAnchorBar, majorSecondAnchorPrice, breakBuffer, retestWindow, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio, bullishExceptionalBreakBar, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
    [lastImportantHigh, previousImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantLow, previousImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, previousImportantLowBar, newImportantHighAnchor, newImportantLowAnchor, compactHighSlope, compactLowSlope, convergingImportantStructure, trendReady, channelReady, channelLowerNow, channelUpperNow, channelUpperFakeout, channelLowerFakeout, channelFakeoutLevel, bullishTrendlineRetest, bearishTrendlineRetest, watchedBreakBoundaryNow, bullishTrendlineBreakEvent, bearishTrendlineBreakEvent]

[lastImportantHigh, previousImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantLow, previousImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, previousImportantLowBar, newImportantHighAnchor, newImportantLowAnchor, compactHighSlope, compactLowSlope, convergingImportantStructure, trendReady, channelReady, channelLowerNow, channelUpperNow, channelUpperFakeout, channelLowerFakeout, channelFakeoutLevel, bullishTrendlineRetest, bearishTrendlineRetest, watchedBreakBoundaryNow, bullishTrendlineBreakEvent, bearishTrendlineBreakEvent] = f_compact_trend_context()
var bool secondaryRisingInvalidated = false
var bool secondaryFallingInvalidated = false
if newImportantLowAnchor
    secondaryRisingInvalidated := false
if newImportantHighAnchor
    secondaryFallingInvalidated := false
bool secondaryRisingRawReady = not na(compactLowSlope) and compactLowSlope > 0
bool secondaryFallingRawReady = not na(compactHighSlope) and compactHighSlope < 0
float secondaryRisingTrendNow = secondaryRisingRawReady ? f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, bar_index) : na
float secondaryFallingTrendNow = secondaryFallingRawReady ? f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, bar_index) : na
bool secondaryFallingBreakUpRaw = f_level_break_event(true, secondaryFallingTrendNow, nz(secondaryFallingTrendNow[1], secondaryFallingTrendNow), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool secondaryRisingBreakDownRaw = f_level_break_event(false, secondaryRisingTrendNow, nz(secondaryRisingTrendNow[1], secondaryRisingTrendNow), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool bullishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent = secondaryFallingRawReady and not secondaryFallingInvalidated and not newImportantHighAnchor and secondaryFallingBreakUpRaw
bool bearishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent = secondaryRisingRawReady and not secondaryRisingInvalidated and not newImportantLowAnchor and secondaryRisingBreakDownRaw
if bullishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent
    secondaryFallingInvalidated := true
if bearishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent
    secondaryRisingInvalidated := true
bool secondaryRisingTrendReady = secondaryRisingRawReady and not secondaryRisingInvalidated
bool secondaryFallingTrendReady = secondaryFallingRawReady and not secondaryFallingInvalidated
int secondaryTrendSignal = secondaryRisingTrendReady and not secondaryFallingTrendReady ? 1 : secondaryFallingTrendReady and not secondaryRisingTrendReady ? -1 : 0
var line activeMajorTrendLine = na
var line activeMajorChannelLine = na
var line activeSecondaryRisingLine = na
var line activeSecondaryFallingLine = na
if barstate.islast
    bool confirmedMajorTrendReady = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorTrendReady : majorTrendReady[1]
    bool confirmedMajorChannelReady = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorChannelReady : majorChannelReady[1]
    int confirmedMajorBias = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorChannelBias : nz(majorChannelBias[1], 0)
    float confirmedMajorWidth = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorChannelWidth : majorChannelWidth[1]
    int majorVisualTransparency = trendLineTransparency
    // The simplified display limits labels, but a genuinely completed channel
    // keeps its light parallel boundary so the channel is not mistaken for a lone trend line.
    [nextMajorTrendLine, nextMajorParallelLine] = f_update_channel_visual(activeMajorTrendLine, activeMajorChannelLine, showTrendChannel, confirmedMajorTrendReady, confirmedMajorChannelReady, confirmedMajorBias, majorFirstAnchorBar, majorFirstAnchorPrice, majorSecondAnchorBar, majorSecondAnchorPrice, confirmedMajorWidth, bullColor, bearColor, majorVisualTransparency, channelLineTransparency, true)
    activeMajorTrendLine := nextMajorTrendLine
    activeMajorChannelLine := nextMajorParallelLine
    [nextSecondaryRisingLine, _] = f_update_channel_visual(activeSecondaryRisingLine, na, showTrendChannel, secondaryRisingTrendReady, false, 1, previousImportantLowBar, previousImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, na, bullColor, bearColor, math.min(90, trendLineTransparency + 18), 95, false)
    [nextSecondaryFallingLine, _] = f_update_channel_visual(activeSecondaryFallingLine, na, showTrendChannel, secondaryFallingTrendReady, false, -1, previousImportantHighBar, previousImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, na, bullColor, bearColor, math.min(90, trendLineTransparency + 18), 95, false)
    activeSecondaryRisingLine := nextSecondaryRisingLine
    activeSecondaryFallingLine := nextSecondaryFallingLine

// جودة شمعة الكسر لا تعتمد على الذيل وحده. الاعتماد البنيوي أدناه يحتاج
// إغلاقين، أو شمعة واحدة استثنائية بحجم مرتفع/قاب ثابت/اندفاع ATR.
f_swing_break_context() =>
    bool bullishBreakCandleQuality = closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bodyRatio >= 0.30 or vsaWideResult)
    bool bearishBreakCandleQuality = closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bodyRatio >= 0.30 or vsaWideResult)
    // A swing high and swing low confirmed from the same outside candle do not
    // reveal which extreme occurred first. Treat that pair as temporally ambiguous
    // until a later distinct pivot resolves the chronology.
    bool swingChronologyCompatible = na(lastSwingHighBar) or na(lastSwingLowBar) or lastSwingHighBar != lastSwingLowBar
    bool swingLevelsCompatible = (na(lastSwingHigh) or na(lastSwingLow) or lastSwingHigh > lastSwingLow + syminfo.mintick) and swingChronologyCompatible

    // A first ordinary close outside the swing is only a monitored attempt.
    // BOS/CHoCH requires two consecutive closes outside the same level, unless
    // the first bar is exceptional through high participation, a held gap, or ATR displacement.
    [bullishBreakAttemptRaw, bullishBreakRaw, confirmedSwingHighBreak, _, bullishBreakMode, _] = f_static_break_state(0, true, swingLevelsCompatible and not highWasBroken ? lastSwingHigh : na, lastSwingHigh, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
    [bearishBreakAttemptRaw, bearishBreakRaw, confirmedSwingLowBreak, _, bearishBreakMode, _] = f_static_break_state(1, false, swingLevelsCompatible and not lowWasBroken ? lastSwingLow : na, lastSwingLow, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
    bool bullishBreakAttempt = swingLevelsCompatible and not highWasBroken and bullishBreakAttemptRaw
    bool bearishBreakAttempt = swingLevelsCompatible and not lowWasBroken and bearishBreakAttemptRaw
    bool bullishBreak = swingLevelsCompatible and not highWasBroken and bullishBreakRaw
    bool bearishBreak = swingLevelsCompatible and not lowWasBroken and bearishBreakRaw
    bool bullishSingleExceptionalBreak = bullishBreak and bullishBreakMode == 1
    bool bearishSingleExceptionalBreak = bearishBreak and bearishBreakMode == 1
    [bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality, swingLevelsCompatible, confirmedSwingHighBreak, confirmedSwingLowBreak, bullishBreakMode, bearishBreakMode, bullishBreakAttempt, bearishBreakAttempt, bullishBreak, bearishBreak, bullishSingleExceptionalBreak, bearishSingleExceptionalBreak]

[bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality, swingLevelsCompatible, confirmedSwingHighBreak, confirmedSwingLowBreak, bullishBreakMode, bearishBreakMode, bullishBreakAttempt, bearishBreakAttempt, bullishBreak, bearishBreak, bullishSingleExceptionalBreak, bearishSingleExceptionalBreak] = f_swing_break_context()
// جودة الرفض موحدة لسحب السيولة، وهمي القناة، وفشل الكسر.
f_rejection_quality(bool bullishSide, float wickShare, bool bodyAlternative, bool participationOkay) =>
    (bullishSide ? closeLocation >= 0.55 : closeLocation <= 0.45) and (bodyAlternative ? bodyRatio >= 0.25 or wickShare >= 0.35 : wickShare >= 0.30) and (participationOkay or wickShare >= 0.55)

f_liquidity_sweep_state() =>
    float lowerWickShare = math.max(math.min(open, close) - low, 0.0) / candleRange
    float upperWickShare = math.max(high - math.max(open, close), 0.0) / candleRange
    float reclaimBuffer = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, safeAtr * 0.04)
    float maximumPenetration = safeAtr
    float lowerPenetration = not na(lastSwingLow) ? lastSwingLow - low : na
    float upperPenetration = not na(lastSwingHigh) ? high - lastSwingHigh : na
    bool participationOkay = not volumeAvailable or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher or statisticalVolumeHigh or relativeVolume >= 0.85
    bool bullishRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingLow) and not lowWasBroken and not lowWasSwept and lowerPenetration >= breakBuffer and lowerPenetration <= maximumPenetration and close >= lastSwingLow + reclaimBuffer
    bool bearishRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not highWasBroken and not highWasSwept and upperPenetration >= breakBuffer and upperPenetration <= maximumPenetration and close <= lastSwingHigh - reclaimBuffer
    bool bullishQualified = bullishRaw and not bearishRaw and f_rejection_quality(true, lowerWickShare, false, participationOkay)
    bool bearishQualified = bearishRaw and not bullishRaw and f_rejection_quality(false, upperWickShare, false, participationOkay)
    [lowerWickShare, upperWickShare, participationOkay, (bullishRaw and bearishRaw), bullishQualified, bearishQualified]

[sweepLowerWickShare, sweepUpperWickShare, sweepVolumeAcceptance, twoSidedLiquiditySweep, bullishSweep, bearishSweep] = f_liquidity_sweep_state()

// Confirmed break events are qualified per event inside the candidate engine.
var int structureBias = 0
var int lastStructureBreakBar = na
if structureBias == 0 and dowBias != 0
    structureBias := dowBias

int structureBreakGraceBars = math.max(2, pivotBars * 2 + 1)
bool recentStructureBreakBeforeCurrent = not na(lastStructureBreakBar) and bar_index - lastStructureBreakBar <= structureBreakGraceBars
int structureBiasBeforeBreak = not swingLevelsCompatible ? 0 : (not recentStructureBreakBeforeCurrent and structureBias != 0 and dowBias != 0 and structureBias != dowBias) ? dowBias : structureBias != 0 ? structureBias : dowBias
bool bullishChoch = bullishBreak and structureBiasBeforeBreak == -1
bool bearishChoch = bearishBreak and structureBiasBeforeBreak == 1
bool bullishBos = bullishBreak and not bullishChoch
bool bearishBos = bearishBreak and not bearishChoch

if bullishBreak
    highWasBroken := true
    structureBias := 1
    lastStructureBreakBar := bar_index
else if bearishBreak
    lowWasBroken := true
    structureBias := -1
    lastStructureBreakBar := bar_index

// A close-confirmed break bias and the latest Dow pivot sequence can disagree
// temporarily. Treat that state as a conflict rather than awarding both sides
// a structural point or presenting one of them as the sole market direction.
bool recentStructureBreak = not na(lastStructureBreakBar) and bar_index - lastStructureBreakBar <= structureBreakGraceBars
bool structureDirectionConflict = not recentStructureBreak and structureBias != 0 and dowBias != 0 and structureBias != dowBias
int resolvedStructureBias = not swingLevelsCompatible or structureDirectionConflict ? 0 : structureBias != 0 ? structureBias : dowBias

// Compact pattern layer keeps range/continuation breaks; measured reversal targets use structural levels.

// A zone now requires both a close-confirmed structure break and a displacement
// candle. Its base follows proximal/distal rules from the supplied references.
// Each valid active supply or demand zone is shown as one bounded transparent price area.
bool bullishZoneDisplacement = bullishBreak and bodyRatio >= zoneImpulseBodyRatio and closeLocation >= 0.65
bool bearishZoneDisplacement = bearishBreak and bodyRatio >= zoneImpulseBodyRatio and closeLocation <= 0.35
[demandOriginReady, demandFarthestOffset, demandProximalCandidate, demandDistalCandidate, demandBaseCandidateCount, _] = f_zone_origin(bullishZoneDisplacement, true, zoneLookback, zoneBaseBodyRatio)
[supplyOriginReady, supplyFarthestOffset, supplyProximalCandidate, supplyDistalCandidate, supplyBaseCandidateCount, _] = f_zone_origin(bearishZoneDisplacement, false, zoneLookback, zoneBaseBodyRatio)
var bool demandZoneActive = false
var bool supplyZoneActive = false
var bool demandZoneRetested = false
var bool supplyZoneRetested = false
var float demandZoneHigh = na
var float demandZoneLow = na
var float supplyZoneHigh = na
var float supplyZoneLow = na
var int demandZoneCreatedBar = na
var int supplyZoneCreatedBar = na
var int demandZoneOriginBar = na
var int supplyZoneOriginBar = na
var int demandZoneQuality = 0
var int supplyZoneQuality = 0
var int demandZoneQualityAxes = 3
var int supplyZoneQualityAxes = 3
var float demandZoneCreationAtr = na
var float supplyZoneCreationAtr = na
var float demandZoneCreationClose = na
var float supplyZoneCreationClose = na
var bool bullishBreakerActive = false
var bool bearishBreakerActive = false
var bool bullishBreakerRetested = false
var bool bearishBreakerRetested = false
var float bullishBreakerHigh = na
var float bullishBreakerLow = na
var float bearishBreakerHigh = na
var float bearishBreakerLow = na
var int bullishBreakerCreatedBar = na
var int bearishBreakerCreatedBar = na

bool validDemandCandidate = demandOriginReady and f_zone_geometry_valid(demandProximalCandidate, demandDistalCandidate, safeAtr)
bool validSupplyCandidate = supplyOriginReady and f_zone_geometry_valid(supplyDistalCandidate, supplyProximalCandidate, safeAtr)
int demandCandidateQualityAxes = volumeAvailable ? 4 : 3
int supplyCandidateQualityAxes = volumeAvailable ? 4 : 3
int demandCandidateQuality = (demandBaseCandidateCount <= 3 ? 1 : 0) + ((demandProximalCandidate - demandDistalCandidate) <= safeAtr * 1.50 ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= 1.0 or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher) ? 1 : 0) + (bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 1 : 0)
int supplyCandidateQuality = (supplyBaseCandidateCount <= 3 ? 1 : 0) + ((supplyDistalCandidate - supplyProximalCandidate) <= safeAtr * 1.50 ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= 1.0 or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher) ? 1 : 0) + (bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 1 : 0)
bool currentDemandStateReady = demandZoneActive and f_zone_state_valid(demandZoneHigh, demandZoneLow, demandZoneCreationAtr, demandZoneCreationClose)
bool currentSupplyStateReady = supplyZoneActive and f_zone_state_valid(supplyZoneHigh, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneCreationAtr, supplyZoneCreationClose)
bool currentDemandQualified = currentDemandStateReady and demandZoneQualityAxes > 0 and demandZoneQuality * 100 >= demandZoneQualityAxes * 60
bool currentSupplyQualified = currentSupplyStateReady and supplyZoneQualityAxes > 0 and supplyZoneQuality * 100 >= supplyZoneQualityAxes * 60
bool existingSupplyBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, supplyZoneHigh, nz(supplyZoneHigh[1], supplyZoneHigh), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool existingDemandBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, demandZoneLow, nz(demandZoneLow[1], demandZoneLow), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool existingSupplyAlreadyBroken = currentSupplyStateReady and existingSupplyBreakRaw
bool existingDemandAlreadyBroken = currentDemandStateReady and existingDemandBreakRaw
bool demandCandidateOpposesProtectedSupply = validDemandCandidate and currentSupplyQualified and not existingSupplyAlreadyBroken and f_zones_overlap(demandDistalCandidate, demandProximalCandidate, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneHigh, syminfo.mintick)
bool supplyCandidateOpposesProtectedDemand = validSupplyCandidate and currentDemandQualified and not existingDemandAlreadyBroken and f_zones_overlap(supplyProximalCandidate, supplyDistalCandidate, demandZoneLow, demandZoneHigh, syminfo.mintick)
int demandCandidateRelativeStrength = demandCandidateQuality * math.max(demandZoneQualityAxes, 1)
int currentDemandRelativeStrength = demandZoneQuality * demandCandidateQualityAxes
int supplyCandidateRelativeStrength = supplyCandidateQuality * math.max(supplyZoneQualityAxes, 1)
int currentSupplyRelativeStrength = supplyZoneQuality * supplyCandidateQualityAxes
bool demandCandidateCloser = validDemandCandidate and (not currentDemandStateReady or math.abs(close - demandProximalCandidate) + safeAtr * 0.10 < math.abs(close - demandZoneHigh))
bool supplyCandidateCloser = validSupplyCandidate and (not currentSupplyStateReady or math.abs(close - supplyProximalCandidate) + safeAtr * 0.10 < math.abs(close - supplyZoneLow))
bool demandCandidateImproves = not currentDemandStateReady or demandZoneRetested or demandCandidateRelativeStrength > currentDemandRelativeStrength or demandCandidateRelativeStrength == currentDemandRelativeStrength and demandCandidateCloser
bool supplyCandidateImproves = not currentSupplyStateReady or supplyZoneRetested or supplyCandidateRelativeStrength > currentSupplyRelativeStrength or supplyCandidateRelativeStrength == currentSupplyRelativeStrength and supplyCandidateCloser
bool demandCandidateQualified = demandCandidateQualityAxes > 0 and demandCandidateQuality * 100 >= demandCandidateQualityAxes * 60
bool supplyCandidateQualified = supplyCandidateQualityAxes > 0 and supplyCandidateQuality * 100 >= supplyCandidateQualityAxes * 60
bool acceptDemandCandidate = bullishZoneDisplacement and validDemandCandidate and demandCandidateQualified and demandCandidateImproves and not demandCandidateOpposesProtectedSupply
bool acceptSupplyCandidate = bearishZoneDisplacement and validSupplyCandidate and supplyCandidateQualified and supplyCandidateImproves and not supplyCandidateOpposesProtectedDemand

// A newer weak zone must never overwrite a stronger untouched zone. Replacement is allowed only for better normalized quality, a consumed zone, or equal quality materially closer to price.
if acceptDemandCandidate
    demandZoneActive := true
    demandZoneRetested := false
    demandZoneHigh := demandProximalCandidate
    demandZoneLow := demandDistalCandidate
    demandZoneCreatedBar := bar_index
    demandZoneOriginBar := bar_index - demandFarthestOffset
    demandZoneCreationAtr := safeAtr
    demandZoneCreationClose := close
    demandZoneQualityAxes := demandCandidateQualityAxes
    demandZoneQuality := demandCandidateQuality

if acceptSupplyCandidate
    supplyZoneActive := true
    supplyZoneRetested := false
    supplyZoneHigh := supplyDistalCandidate
    supplyZoneLow := supplyProximalCandidate
    supplyZoneCreatedBar := bar_index
    supplyZoneOriginBar := bar_index - supplyFarthestOffset
    supplyZoneCreationAtr := safeAtr
    supplyZoneCreationClose := close
    supplyZoneQualityAxes := supplyCandidateQualityAxes
    supplyZoneQuality := supplyCandidateQuality

bool demandZoneMalformed = demandZoneActive and not f_zone_state_valid(demandZoneHigh, demandZoneLow, demandZoneCreationAtr, demandZoneCreationClose)
bool supplyZoneMalformed = supplyZoneActive and not f_zone_state_valid(supplyZoneHigh, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneCreationAtr, supplyZoneCreationClose)
bool demandZoneBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, demandZoneLow, nz(demandZoneLow[1], demandZoneLow), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool supplyZoneBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, supplyZoneHigh, nz(supplyZoneHigh[1], supplyZoneHigh), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool demandZoneInvalidated = demandZoneActive and not demandZoneMalformed and demandZoneBreakRaw
bool supplyZoneInvalidated = supplyZoneActive and not supplyZoneMalformed and supplyZoneBreakRaw
int adaptiveZoneMaxBars = zoneMaxBars
bool demandZoneExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and not na(demandZoneCreatedBar) and bar_index - demandZoneCreatedBar > adaptiveZoneMaxBars
bool supplyZoneExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and not na(supplyZoneCreatedBar) and bar_index - supplyZoneCreatedBar > adaptiveZoneMaxBars
bool demandZoneQualified = demandZoneActive and demandZoneQualityAxes > 0 and demandZoneQuality * 100 >= demandZoneQualityAxes * 60
bool supplyZoneQualified = supplyZoneActive and supplyZoneQualityAxes > 0 and supplyZoneQuality * 100 >= supplyZoneQualityAxes * 60
bool initialSupplyDemandConflict = demandZoneActive and supplyZoneActive and demandZoneQualified and supplyZoneQualified and f_zones_overlap(demandZoneLow, demandZoneHigh, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneHigh, syminfo.mintick)
if initialSupplyDemandConflict
    int demandConflictStrength = demandZoneQuality * math.max(supplyZoneQualityAxes, 1)
    int supplyConflictStrength = supplyZoneQuality * math.max(demandZoneQualityAxes, 1)
    bool keepDemand = demandConflictStrength > supplyConflictStrength or demandConflictStrength == supplyConflictStrength and resolvedStructureBias == 1
    bool keepSupply = supplyConflictStrength > demandConflictStrength or demandConflictStrength == supplyConflictStrength and resolvedStructureBias == -1
    if not keepDemand
        demandZoneActive := false
        demandZoneRetested := false
        demandZoneHigh := na
        demandZoneLow := na
        demandZoneCreatedBar := na
        demandZoneOriginBar := na
        demandZoneQuality := 0
        demandZoneQualityAxes := 3
        demandZoneCreationAtr := na
        demandZoneCreationClose := na
    if not keepSupply
        supplyZoneActive := false
        supplyZoneRetested := false
        supplyZoneHigh := na
        supplyZoneLow := na
        supplyZoneCreatedBar := na
        supplyZoneOriginBar := na
        supplyZoneQuality := 0
        supplyZoneQualityAxes := 3
        supplyZoneCreationAtr := na
        supplyZoneCreationClose := na
// Re-evaluate after deterministic cleanup. Opposing zones can no longer deadlock both retest engines until expiry.
demandZoneQualified := demandZoneActive and demandZoneQualityAxes > 0 and demandZoneQuality * 100 >= demandZoneQualityAxes * 60
supplyZoneQualified := supplyZoneActive and supplyZoneQualityAxes > 0 and supplyZoneQuality * 100 >= supplyZoneQualityAxes * 60
bool supplyDemandConflictBefore = demandZoneActive and supplyZoneActive and demandZoneQualified and supplyZoneQualified and f_zones_overlap(demandZoneLow, demandZoneHigh, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneHigh, syminfo.mintick)
bool bullishDemandRetestRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and not supplyDemandConflictBefore and demandZoneActive and not demandZoneRetested and not demandZoneInvalidated and not demandZoneExpired and bar_index > demandZoneCreatedBar and low <= demandZoneHigh and high >= demandZoneLow and close > demandZoneHigh and close > open
bool bearishSupplyRetestRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and not supplyDemandConflictBefore and supplyZoneActive and not supplyZoneRetested and not supplyZoneInvalidated and not supplyZoneExpired and bar_index > supplyZoneCreatedBar and high >= supplyZoneLow and low <= supplyZoneHigh and close < supplyZoneLow and close < open
bool bullishDemandRetest = bullishDemandRetestRaw and demandZoneQualified
bool bearishSupplyRetest = bearishSupplyRetestRaw and supplyZoneQualified

if bullishDemandRetest
    demandZoneRetested := true
if bearishSupplyRetest
    supplyZoneRetested := true

// المنطقة الجيدة التي تُكسر بإغلاق تتحول إلى بريكر واحد خفيف بدل إنشاء محرك مناطق مكرر.
if supplyZoneInvalidated and supplyZoneQualified and not supplyDemandConflictBefore
    bullishBreakerActive := true
    bullishBreakerRetested := false
    bullishBreakerHigh := supplyZoneHigh
    bullishBreakerLow := supplyZoneLow
    bullishBreakerCreatedBar := bar_index
if demandZoneInvalidated and demandZoneQualified and not supplyDemandConflictBefore
    bearishBreakerActive := true
    bearishBreakerRetested := false
    bearishBreakerHigh := demandZoneHigh
    bearishBreakerLow := demandZoneLow
    bearishBreakerCreatedBar := bar_index

if demandZoneMalformed or demandZoneInvalidated or demandZoneExpired
    demandZoneActive := false
    demandZoneRetested := false
    demandZoneHigh := na
    demandZoneLow := na
    demandZoneCreatedBar := na
    demandZoneOriginBar := na
    demandZoneQuality := 0
    demandZoneQualityAxes := 3
    demandZoneCreationAtr := na
    demandZoneCreationClose := na
if supplyZoneMalformed or supplyZoneInvalidated or supplyZoneExpired
    supplyZoneActive := false
    supplyZoneRetested := false
    supplyZoneHigh := na
    supplyZoneLow := na
    supplyZoneCreatedBar := na
    supplyZoneOriginBar := na
    supplyZoneQuality := 0
    supplyZoneQualityAxes := 3
    supplyZoneCreationAtr := na
    supplyZoneCreationClose := na

// مناطق العرض والطلب نطاقات سعرية وليست خطوطًا منفردة؛ الصندوق يمتد يمينًا
// حتى الإبطال أو انتهاء الصلاحية، بينما تبقى حدوده مرجعًا للوقف والهدف.
bool bullishBreakerExpired = bullishBreakerActive and not na(bullishBreakerCreatedBar) and bar_index - bullishBreakerCreatedBar > adaptiveZoneMaxBars
bool bearishBreakerExpired = bearishBreakerActive and not na(bearishBreakerCreatedBar) and bar_index - bearishBreakerCreatedBar > adaptiveZoneMaxBars
bool breakerConflictBefore = bullishBreakerActive and bearishBreakerActive and f_zones_overlap(bullishBreakerLow, bullishBreakerHigh, bearishBreakerLow, bearishBreakerHigh, syminfo.mintick)
bool bullishBreakerBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, bullishBreakerLow, nz(bullishBreakerLow[1], bullishBreakerLow), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool bearishBreakerBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, bearishBreakerHigh, nz(bearishBreakerHigh[1], bearishBreakerHigh), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool bullishBreakerFailed = bullishBreakerActive and bullishBreakerBreakRaw
bool bearishBreakerFailed = bearishBreakerActive and bearishBreakerBreakRaw
bool bullishBreakerRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and not breakerConflictBefore and bullishBreakerActive and not bullishBreakerRetested and not bullishBreakerExpired and not bullishBreakerFailed and bar_index > bullishBreakerCreatedBar and low <= bullishBreakerHigh and high >= bullishBreakerLow and close > bullishBreakerHigh and close > open
bool bearishBreakerRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and not breakerConflictBefore and bearishBreakerActive and not bearishBreakerRetested and not bearishBreakerExpired and not bearishBreakerFailed and bar_index > bearishBreakerCreatedBar and high >= bearishBreakerLow and low <= bearishBreakerHigh and close < bearishBreakerLow and close < open
if bullishBreakerRetest
    bullishBreakerRetested := true
if bearishBreakerRetest
    bearishBreakerRetested := true
if bullishBreakerExpired or bullishBreakerFailed
    bullishBreakerActive := false
    bullishBreakerRetested := false
    bullishBreakerHigh := na
    bullishBreakerLow := na
    bullishBreakerCreatedBar := na
if bearishBreakerExpired or bearishBreakerFailed
    bearishBreakerActive := false
    bearishBreakerRetested := false
    bearishBreakerHigh := na
    bearishBreakerLow := na
    bearishBreakerCreatedBar := na

bool breakerAnalysisConflict = bullishBreakerActive and bearishBreakerActive and f_zones_overlap(bullishBreakerLow, bullishBreakerHigh, bearishBreakerLow, bearishBreakerHigh, syminfo.mintick)
bool bullishBreakerAnalysisReady = bullishBreakerActive and not breakerAnalysisConflict
bool bearishBreakerAnalysisReady = bearishBreakerActive and not breakerAnalysisConflict

// آخر منطقة صالحة فقط لكل جهة، بطول قصير وشفافية عالية حتى تبقى مرجعاً
// للدخول والوقف والهدف من دون إعادة المربعات الممتدة التي غطت الشارت سابقاً.
var line demandZoneTopVisual = na
var line demandZoneBottomVisual = na
var linefill demandZoneFillVisual = na
var line supplyZoneTopVisual = na
var line supplyZoneBottomVisual = na
var linefill supplyZoneFillVisual = na
float zoneVisualMaximumDistance = safeAtr * 6.0
bool supplyDemandAnalysisConflict = demandZoneActive and supplyZoneActive and demandZoneQualified and supplyZoneQualified and f_zones_overlap(demandZoneLow, demandZoneHigh, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneHigh, syminfo.mintick)
bool demandZoneAnalysisReady = demandZoneActive and demandZoneQualified and not supplyDemandAnalysisConflict
bool supplyZoneAnalysisReady = supplyZoneActive and supplyZoneQualified and not supplyDemandAnalysisConflict
bool rawDemandZoneVisualRelevant = demandZoneActive and demandZoneQualified and not supplyDemandAnalysisConflict and not na(demandZoneHigh) and not na(demandZoneLow) and close >= demandZoneLow - zoneVisualMaximumDistance and close <= demandZoneHigh + zoneVisualMaximumDistance
bool rawSupplyZoneVisualRelevant = supplyZoneActive and supplyZoneQualified and not supplyDemandAnalysisConflict and not na(supplyZoneHigh) and not na(supplyZoneLow) and close >= supplyZoneLow - zoneVisualMaximumDistance and close <= supplyZoneHigh + zoneVisualMaximumDistance
bool demandSupportVisualMerge = rawDemandZoneVisualRelevant and supportZoneDisplayVisible and f_zones_overlap(demandZoneLow, demandZoneHigh, supportZoneDisplayLow, supportZoneDisplayHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
bool supplyResistanceVisualMerge = rawSupplyZoneVisualRelevant and resistanceZoneDisplayVisible and f_zones_overlap(supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneHigh, resistanceZoneDisplayLow, resistanceZoneDisplayHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
float demandBaseDisplayLow = demandSupportVisualMerge ? math.min(demandZoneLow, supportZoneDisplayLow) : demandZoneLow
float demandBaseDisplayHigh = demandSupportVisualMerge ? math.max(demandZoneHigh, supportZoneDisplayHigh) : demandZoneHigh
float supplyBaseDisplayLow = supplyResistanceVisualMerge ? math.min(supplyZoneLow, resistanceZoneDisplayLow) : supplyZoneLow
float supplyBaseDisplayHigh = supplyResistanceVisualMerge ? math.max(supplyZoneHigh, resistanceZoneDisplayHigh) : supplyZoneHigh
bool demandResistanceVisualConflict = rawDemandZoneVisualRelevant and resistanceZoneDisplayVisible and not supplyResistanceVisualMerge and f_zones_overlap(demandBaseDisplayLow, demandBaseDisplayHigh, resistanceZoneDisplayLow, resistanceZoneDisplayHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
bool supplySupportVisualConflict = rawSupplyZoneVisualRelevant and supportZoneDisplayVisible and not demandSupportVisualMerge and f_zones_overlap(supplyBaseDisplayLow, supplyBaseDisplayHigh, supportZoneDisplayLow, supportZoneDisplayHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
float demandResistanceMergedLow = demandResistanceVisualConflict ? math.min(demandBaseDisplayLow, resistanceZoneDisplayLow) : na
float demandResistanceMergedHigh = demandResistanceVisualConflict ? math.max(demandBaseDisplayHigh, resistanceZoneDisplayHigh) : na
float supplySupportMergedLow = supplySupportVisualConflict ? math.min(supplyBaseDisplayLow, supportZoneDisplayLow) : na
float supplySupportMergedHigh = supplySupportVisualConflict ? math.max(supplyBaseDisplayHigh, supportZoneDisplayHigh) : na
bool demandConflictActsAsDemand = demandResistanceVisualConflict and (close > demandResistanceMergedHigh + breakBuffer or resolvedStructureBias == 1 and close >= (demandResistanceMergedLow + demandResistanceMergedHigh) * 0.50)
bool demandConflictActsAsResistance = demandResistanceVisualConflict and (close < demandResistanceMergedLow - breakBuffer or resolvedStructureBias == -1 and close < (demandResistanceMergedLow + demandResistanceMergedHigh) * 0.50)
bool demandConflictIsPivot = demandResistanceVisualConflict and not demandConflictActsAsDemand and not demandConflictActsAsResistance
bool supplyConflictActsAsSupply = supplySupportVisualConflict and (close < supplySupportMergedLow - breakBuffer or resolvedStructureBias == -1 and close <= (supplySupportMergedLow + supplySupportMergedHigh) * 0.50)
bool supplyConflictActsAsSupport = supplySupportVisualConflict and (close > supplySupportMergedHigh + breakBuffer or resolvedStructureBias == 1 and close > (supplySupportMergedLow + supplySupportMergedHigh) * 0.50)
bool supplyConflictIsPivot = supplySupportVisualConflict and not supplyConflictActsAsSupply and not supplyConflictActsAsSupport
bool demandZoneVisualRelevant = rawDemandZoneVisualRelevant and (not demandResistanceVisualConflict or demandConflictActsAsDemand or demandConflictIsPivot)
bool supplyZoneVisualRelevant = rawSupplyZoneVisualRelevant and (not supplySupportVisualConflict or supplyConflictActsAsSupply or supplyConflictIsPivot)
float demandZoneDisplayLow = demandResistanceVisualConflict and (demandConflictActsAsDemand or demandConflictIsPivot) ? demandResistanceMergedLow : demandBaseDisplayLow
float demandZoneDisplayHigh = demandResistanceVisualConflict and (demandConflictActsAsDemand or demandConflictIsPivot) ? demandResistanceMergedHigh : demandBaseDisplayHigh
float supplyZoneDisplayLow = supplySupportVisualConflict and (supplyConflictActsAsSupply or supplyConflictIsPivot) ? supplySupportMergedLow : supplyBaseDisplayLow
float supplyZoneDisplayHigh = supplySupportVisualConflict and (supplyConflictActsAsSupply or supplyConflictIsPivot) ? supplySupportMergedHigh : supplyBaseDisplayHigh
color demandZoneDisplayColor = demandConflictIsPivot ? #94A3B8 : bullColor
color supplyZoneDisplayColor = supplyConflictIsPivot ? #94A3B8 : bearColor
string demandZoneDisplayCaption = demandConflictIsPivot ? "منطقة محورية" : "منطقة طلب"
string supplyZoneDisplayCaption = supplyConflictIsPivot ? "منطقة محورية" : "منطقة عرض"
bool suppressResistanceZoneVisual = supplyResistanceVisualMerge or demandResistanceVisualConflict and (demandConflictActsAsDemand or demandConflictIsPivot)
bool suppressSupportZoneVisual = demandSupportVisualMerge or supplySupportVisualConflict and (supplyConflictActsAsSupply or supplyConflictIsPivot)
int zoneVisualForwardBars = chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 20 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 16 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 14 : 12
int demandZoneVisualFirstBar = math.max(bar_index - 4999, math.max(0, nz(demandZoneOriginBar, bar_index)))
int supplyZoneVisualFirstBar = math.max(bar_index - 4999, math.max(0, nz(supplyZoneOriginBar, bar_index)))
if barstate.islast
    if suppressResistanceZoneVisual
        if not na(resistanceZone1Fill)
            linefill.delete(resistanceZone1Fill)
            resistanceZone1Fill := na
        resistanceZone1TopLine := f_clear_line(resistanceZone1TopLine)
        resistanceZone1BottomLine := f_clear_line(resistanceZone1BottomLine)
    if suppressSupportZoneVisual
        if not na(supportZone1Fill)
            linefill.delete(supportZone1Fill)
            supportZone1Fill := na
        supportZone1TopLine := f_clear_line(supportZone1TopLine)
        supportZone1BottomLine := f_clear_line(supportZone1BottomLine)
    [nextDemandTop, nextDemandBottom, nextDemandFill] = f_update_zone_visual(demandZoneTopVisual, demandZoneBottomVisual, demandZoneFillVisual, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and demandZoneVisualRelevant, demandZoneVisualFirstBar, bar_index + zoneVisualForwardBars, demandZoneDisplayHigh, demandZoneDisplayLow, demandZoneDisplayColor)
    demandZoneTopVisual := nextDemandTop
    demandZoneBottomVisual := nextDemandBottom
    demandZoneFillVisual := nextDemandFill
    [nextSupplyTop, nextSupplyBottom, nextSupplyFill] = f_update_zone_visual(supplyZoneTopVisual, supplyZoneBottomVisual, supplyZoneFillVisual, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and supplyZoneVisualRelevant, supplyZoneVisualFirstBar, bar_index + zoneVisualForwardBars, supplyZoneDisplayHigh, supplyZoneDisplayLow, supplyZoneDisplayColor)
    supplyZoneTopVisual := nextSupplyTop
    supplyZoneBottomVisual := nextSupplyBottom
    supplyZoneFillVisual := nextSupplyFill

// An old valid zone remains a drawing/stop/target reference, but it must not
// contribute a permanent location vote when price has travelled far away.
float zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance = safeAtr * zoneEvidenceMaxDistanceAtr
bool demandZoneEvidenceRelevant = demandZoneAnalysisReady and (not demandResistanceVisualConflict or demandConflictActsAsDemand) and not na(demandZoneHigh) and close >= demandZoneLow - breakBuffer and (bullishDemandRetest or math.max(close - demandZoneHigh, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance)
bool supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant = supplyZoneAnalysisReady and (not supplySupportVisualConflict or supplyConflictActsAsSupply) and not na(supplyZoneLow) and close <= supplyZoneHigh + breakBuffer and (bearishSupplyRetest or math.max(supplyZoneLow - close, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance)
bool bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant = bullishBreakerAnalysisReady and close >= bullishBreakerLow - breakBuffer and math.max(close - bullishBreakerHigh, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance
bool bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant = bearishBreakerAnalysisReady and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + breakBuffer and math.max(bearishBreakerLow - close, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance
bool fallbackResistanceVisualReady = stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and fallbackResistanceOutsideZones and not f_level_inside_sr_zones(fallbackResistanceLevel, supplyZoneLow, supplyZoneHigh, bearishBreakerLow, bearishBreakerHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance) and not f_level_inside_sr_zones(fallbackResistanceLevel, demandZoneDisplayLow, demandZoneDisplayHigh, na, na, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
bool fallbackSupportVisualReady = stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and fallbackSupportOutsideZones and not f_level_inside_sr_zones(fallbackSupportLevel, demandZoneLow, demandZoneHigh, bullishBreakerLow, bullishBreakerHigh, srZoneExclusionTolerance) and not f_level_inside_sr_zones(fallbackSupportLevel, supplyZoneDisplayLow, supplyZoneDisplayHigh, na, na, srZoneExclusionTolerance)
if barstate.islast
    strongResistanceVisualLine := f_update_sr_line(strongResistanceVisualLine, fallbackResistanceStartBar, fallbackResistanceVisualReady ? fallbackResistanceLevel : na, color.white, line.style_solid, 1, 55)
    strongSupportVisualLine := f_update_sr_line(strongSupportVisualLine, fallbackSupportStartBar, fallbackSupportVisualReady ? fallbackSupportLevel : na, #F59E0B, line.style_solid, 2, 55)
bool conflictingZoneLocation = demandConflictIsPivot or supplyConflictIsPivot or (demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and (supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and not bullishDemandRetest and not bearishSupplyRetest and not bullishBreakerRetest and not bearishBreakerRetest
bool bullishZoneTrigger = bullishBreakerRetest or bullishDemandRetest
bool bearishZoneTrigger = bearishBreakerRetest or bearishSupplyRetest
int zoneSignal = bullishZoneTrigger and not bearishZoneTrigger ? 1 : bearishZoneTrigger and not bullishZoneTrigger ? -1 : conflictingZoneLocation or bullishZoneTrigger and bearishZoneTrigger ? 0 : (demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : (supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? -1 : 0
float effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel = channelUpperFakeout or channelLowerFakeout ? channelFakeoutLevel : majorChannelUpperFakeout ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerFakeout ? majorChannelLowerNow : na
bool qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout = (majorChannelUpperFakeout or channelUpperFakeout) and f_rejection_quality(false, sweepUpperWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)
