    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 5, stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and fallbackSupportVisualReady, fallbackSupportLevel, not na(higherTimeframeSupport) and math.abs(fallbackSupportLevel - higherTimeframeSupport) <= syminfo.mintick ? "دعم HTF" : "دعم مفرد", #F59E0B, safeAtr)

float nearestMaSupportForStop = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, safeAtr * 0.10, sma24, sma50, sma200, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float nearestMaResistanceForStop = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, safeAtr * 0.10, sma24, sma50, sma200, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)

float triggerPrice = nz(close[1], close)
float maResistanceTrigger = nearestMaResistanceForStop[1]
float maSupportTrigger = nearestMaSupportForStop[1]
float fibResistanceTrigger = f_nearest_price_level(true, triggerPrice, 0.0, fibRetracement382[1], fibRetracement500[1], fibRetracement618[1], fibRetracement786[1], na, na, na, na, na, na)
float fibSupportTrigger = f_nearest_price_level(false, triggerPrice, 0.0, fibRetracement382[1], fibRetracement500[1], fibRetracement618[1], fibRetracement786[1], na, na, na, na, na, na)
float bullishTechnicalResistance = na(maResistanceTrigger) ? fibResistanceTrigger : na(fibResistanceTrigger) ? maResistanceTrigger : math.min(maResistanceTrigger, fibResistanceTrigger)
float bearishTechnicalSupport = na(maSupportTrigger) ? fibSupportTrigger : na(fibSupportTrigger) ? maSupportTrigger : math.max(maSupportTrigger, fibSupportTrigger)
[_, bullishTechnicalBreak, bullishTechnicalBreakLevel, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(16, true, bullishTechnicalResistance, bullishTechnicalResistance, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
[_, bearishTechnicalBreak, bearishTechnicalBreakLevel, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(17, false, bearishTechnicalSupport, bearishTechnicalSupport, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
float technicalBounceTolerance = math.max(retestTolerance, safeAtr * 0.12)
bool bullishTechnicalBounce = f_true_level_bounce(true, bearishTechnicalSupport, technicalBounceTolerance, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio)
bool bearishTechnicalBounce = f_true_level_bounce(false, bullishTechnicalResistance, technicalBounceTolerance, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio)
int bullishTechnicalEvent = bullishTechnicalBreak ? 1 : bullishTechnicalBounce ? 2 : 0
int bearishTechnicalEvent = bearishTechnicalBreak ? 1 : bearishTechnicalBounce ? 2 : 0
float bullishTechnicalLevel = bullishTechnicalBreak ? bullishTechnicalBreakLevel : bearishTechnicalSupport
float bearishTechnicalLevel = bearishTechnicalBreak ? bearishTechnicalBreakLevel : bullishTechnicalResistance

SetupInputContext setupInputContext = SetupInputContext.new(firstAnalysisSessionBar, chartSeconds, safeAtr, sma24, movingAverageBias, resolvedStructureBias, higherWeightedScore, vsaSignal, closeLocation, bodyRatio, retestWindow, retestTolerance, breakBuffer, enableEarlyOpportunityEngine, requireHigherTimeframeAlignment, useAdaptiveEntryQuality, preventExtendedEntries, volumePass, bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality, bullishTechnicalEvent, bearishTechnicalEvent, bullishTechnicalLevel, bearishTechnicalLevel)
[longCandidateActive, longActionableRaw] = f_update_setup_candidate_state(longCandidateState, 1, setupInputContext)
[shortCandidateActive, shortActionableRaw] = f_update_setup_candidate_state(shortCandidateState, -1, setupInputContext)
bool actionableLongSetup = longActionableRaw
bool actionableShortSetup = shortActionableRaw
[dealingRangeEvidenceReady, dealingRangeSignal, _] = f_dealing_range_context(usePremiumDiscountContext, (longCandidateActive and not longCandidateState.directBreak) or (shortCandidateActive and not shortCandidateState.directBreak), swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar, close)

// =============================================================================
// التوافق مقسم إلى مجموعات مستقلة قدر الإمكان، ويستخدم مقاما ديناميكيا حسب
// الأدوات المتاحة والمفعلة بدلا من عد الحدث السعري نفسه عدة مرات.
f_calculate_evidence_aggregates() =>
    bool swingStructureReady = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and swingLevelsCompatible
    // أوزان المدارس: الهيكل/HTF 30%، الموقع والمستويات 30%، المشاركة/المزاد 20%، الزناد والتوقيت 20%.
    int structureEvidenceMax = (swingStructureReady ? 2 : 0) + (higherTimeframesReady ? 1 : 0)
    int bullishStructureEvidence = (swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 2 : 0) + (higherTimeframesReady and higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishStructureEvidence = (swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? 2 : 0) + (higherTimeframesReady and higherWeightedScore < 0 ? 1 : 0)

    // المشاركة مدرستان فرعيتان مستقلتان قدر الإمكان: الجهد/النتيجة ثم OBV.
    bool bullDirectVolumeEvidence = useVolumeFilter and longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.directBreak and volumeAvailable
    bool bearDirectVolumeEvidence = useVolumeFilter and shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.directBreak and volumeAvailable
    bool vsaEvidenceEnabled = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable
    int participationEvidenceMax = volumeAvailable ? 2 : 0
    int bullishParticipationEvidence = volumeAvailable ? (((bullDirectVolumeEvidence and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation >= 0.55) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == 1) ? 1 : 0) + (obvSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0)) : 0
    int bearishParticipationEvidence = volumeAvailable ? (((bearDirectVolumeEvidence and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation <= 0.45) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == -1) ? 1 : 0) + (obvSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0)) : 0

    float evidenceVwapValue = useVwapFilter ? entryVwapValue : na
    bool vwapEvidenceReady = not na(evidenceVwapValue)
    bool zoneEvidenceReady = demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant
    bool horizontalSrEvidenceReady = horizontalSrSignal != 0
    bool trendLocationEvidenceReady = majorTrendReady or secondaryRisingTrendReady or secondaryFallingTrendReady
    bool bullishTrendLocation = majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == 1 or secondaryRisingTrendReady
    bool bearishTrendLocation = majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == -1 or secondaryFallingTrendReady
    int locationEvidenceMax = math.min(3, (trendLocationEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0))
    int bullishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, (bullishTrendLocation ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close > evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0))
    int bearishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, (bearishTrendLocation ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close < evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0))

    // Recovery confirmations are compressed into one bounded vote:
    // swing sequence, level break, rising participation, SMA24/50, RSI50 and OBV.
    int bullishRecoveryConfirmations = (lastLowTag == "HL" ? 1 : 0) + (bullishBreak or strongResistanceBreak ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= 1.0 and relativeVolume > nz(relativeVolume[1], relativeVolume) and close > open ? 1 : 0) + (movingAverageBias == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (rsiValue > 50 ? 1 : 0) + (obvSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishRecoveryConfirmations = (lastHighTag == "LH" ? 1 : 0) + (bearishBreak or strongSupportBreak ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= 1.0 and relativeVolume > nz(relativeVolume[1], relativeVolume) and close < open ? 1 : 0) + (movingAverageBias == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (rsiValue < 50 ? 1 : 0) + (obvSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0)
    bool bullishRecoveryCluster = precedingDownContext and bullishRecoveryConfirmations >= 4
    bool bearishRecoveryCluster = precedingUpContext and bearishRecoveryConfirmations >= 4

    int barsSinceBullishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bullishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline)
    int barsSinceBearishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bearishInsideBarBreakout or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance)
    bool recentBullishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBullishPriceAction) and barsSinceBullishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    bool recentBearishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBearishPriceAction) and barsSinceBearishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    // المرشح النشط هو زناد الخطة الأساسي. الأدلة السلوكية الأخرى تؤكد جودته
    // ولا تجعل BOS الصالح يبدأ من صفر في مجموعة الزناد.
    int triggerEvidenceMax = math.min(2, 1 + (useCandleConfirmation ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence ? 1 : 0))
    bool longOriginAuction = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION
    bool shortOriginAuction = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION
    bool longOriginCandle = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE
    bool shortOriginCandle = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE
    bool longOriginPriceAction = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN
    bool shortOriginPriceAction = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN
    bool longOriginDivergence = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_DIVERGENCE
    bool shortOriginDivergence = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_DIVERGENCE
    int bullishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (longCandidateActive ? 1 : 0) + (recentBullishAuctionTrigger and not longOriginAuction ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBullishCandle and not longOriginCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBullishPriceAction and not longOriginPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBullishDivergence and not longOriginDivergence ? 1 : 0) + (bullishRecoveryCluster ? 1 : 0))
    int bearishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (shortCandidateActive ? 1 : 0) + (recentBearishAuctionTrigger and not shortOriginAuction ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBearishCandle and not shortOriginCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBearishPriceAction and not shortOriginPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBearishDivergence and not shortOriginDivergence ? 1 : 0) + (bearishRecoveryCluster ? 1 : 0))

    int evidenceMaximum = math.max(1, structureEvidenceMax + participationEvidenceMax + locationEvidenceMax + triggerEvidenceMax)
    int bullishEvidence = bullishStructureEvidence + bullishParticipationEvidence + bullishLocationEvidence + bullishTriggerEvidence
    int bearishEvidence = bearishStructureEvidence + bearishParticipationEvidence + bearishLocationEvidence + bearishTriggerEvidence
    int bullishEvidenceAxes = (bullishStructureEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bullishParticipationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bullishLocationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bullishTriggerEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishEvidenceAxes = (bearishStructureEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bearishParticipationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bearishLocationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bearishTriggerEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    [evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, 100.0 * float(bullishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum), 100.0 * float(bearishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum), bullishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidenceAxes]

[evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent, bullishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidenceAxes] = f_calculate_evidence_aggregates()
// حالة الخطة ثابتة بين المباشر وBar Replay. الاختراق والوقف الفني لا يثبتان إلا بالإغلاق،
// بينما تبقى الأهداف السعرية محسوبة من مدى الشمعة.
var int planDirection = 0
var bool planActive = false
var int planStartBar = na
var float planEntry = na
var float planStop = na
var float planTarget1 = na
var float planTarget2 = na
var float planTarget3 = na
var int planScore = 0
var int planScoreMax = 1
var bool target1Done = false
var bool target2Done = false
var bool target3Done = false
var int target1HitBar = na
var int target2HitBar = na
var int target3HitBar = na
var float planHighSinceEntry = na
var float planLowSinceEntry = na
var int planQualityTier = 0
var bool planHtfOpposed = false
var int planEntryKind = 0
var int planEntrySubKind = 0
var int lastPlanExitBar = na
var int planOutcomeBar = na
var int planOutcomeKind = 0
var float planOutcomePrice = na

var line entryLine = na
var line stopLine = na
var line target1Line = na
var line target2Line = na
var line target3Line = na
var label entryLevelLabel = na
var label stopLevelLabel = na
var label target1LevelLabel = na
var label target2LevelLabel = na
var label target3LevelLabel = na
var label activePlanEntryMarker = na
var label activePlanOutcomeMarker = na
// قيّم الخطة القائمة أولا. بهذه الأولوية لا تستطيع إشارة جديدة أن تمسح وقفا
// أو هدفا حدث على الشمعة نفسها. الأهداف السعرية تُسجل أولا، والوقف الفني لا يعتمد إلا بإغلاق الشمعة.
if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    planHighSinceEntry := na(planHighSinceEntry) ? high : math.max(planHighSinceEntry, high)
    planLowSinceEntry := na(planLowSinceEntry) ? low : math.min(planLowSinceEntry, low)

bool planStopTouchedNow = planActive and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? low <= planStop : high >= planStop)
bool planStopClosedNow = planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? close <= planStop : close >= planStop)
bool planStopTriggeredNow = stopUsesTouch ? planStopTouchedNow : planStopClosedNow

if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    bool rawTarget1Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget1 : low <= planTarget1
    bool rawTarget2Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget2 : low <= planTarget2
    bool rawTarget3Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget3 : low <= planTarget3
    bool newTarget1Touched = rawTarget1Touched and not target1Done
    bool newTarget2Touched = rawTarget2Touched and not target2Done
    bool newTarget3Touched = rawTarget3Touched and not target3Done
    bool anyNewTargetTouched = newTarget1Touched or newTarget2Touched or newTarget3Touched
    // A close-based stop occurs only at the completed bar close, so any target
    // touched inside that bar is chronologically earlier and must be recorded.
    // Ambiguity exists only when both stop and target are touch-based in one OHLC bar.
    bool touchOutcomeAmbiguous = stopUsesTouch and planStopTouchedNow and anyNewTargetTouched
    bool target1Touched = not touchOutcomeAmbiguous and newTarget1Touched
    bool target2Touched = not touchOutcomeAmbiguous and newTarget2Touched
    bool target3Touched = not touchOutcomeAmbiguous and newTarget3Touched

    if touchOutcomeAmbiguous
        planActive := false
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 5
        planOutcomePrice := close
    else if target3Touched and not target3Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        if not target2Done
            target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        target3Done := true
        target3HitBar := bar_index
        planActive := false
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 3
        planOutcomePrice := planTarget3
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 58, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 58, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 42, 2)

    else
        if target2Touched and not target2Done
            if not target1Done
                target1HitBar := bar_index
            target2HitBar := bar_index
            target1Done := true
            target2Done := true
            f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 58, 1)
            f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 50, 1)
        else if target1Touched and not target1Done
            target1Done := true
            target1HitBar := bar_index
            f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 50, 1)

        if planActive and planStopTriggeredNow
            planActive := false
            lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
            planOutcomeBar := bar_index
            planOutcomeKind := 1
            planOutcomePrice := stopUsesTouch ? planStop : close
            f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
            f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 12, 2)
            if not target1Done
                f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
            if not target2Done
                f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
            if not target3Done
                f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)

// Only after the confirmed close do we calculate the next bar's protected
// stop. This prevents the current high from raising a stop that is then tested
// against the current low when their true intrabar order is unknown.
if planActive and usePostTargetAtrTrail and target1Done and barstate.isconfirmed and postTargetTrailReady
    float rawTrailCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? planHighSinceEntry - postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple : planLowSinceEntry + postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple
    float breakEvenProtectedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.max(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry) : math.min(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry)
    float priceValidCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.min(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float tickRoundedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? f_floor_to_tick(priceValidCandidate) : f_ceil_to_tick(priceValidCandidate)
    if (planDirection == 1 ? tickRoundedCandidate >= planEntry : tickRoundedCandidate <= planEntry) and (planDirection == 1 ? tickRoundedCandidate > planStop + syminfo.mintick : tickRoundedCandidate < planStop - syminfo.mintick)
        planStop := tickRoundedCandidate
        stopLine := f_replace_plan_line(stopLine, true, planStop, #F23645, planStopLineTransparency, line.style_solid)

bool rawExceptionalLongBreakPass = f_exceptional_direct_break_pass(true, longCandidateActive, longCandidateState.directBreak, longCandidateState.qualified, volumePass, bodyRatio, closeLocation, bullishEvidenceAxes, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, higherWeightedScore, longCandidateState.reversalEligible)
bool rawExceptionalShortBreakPass = f_exceptional_direct_break_pass(false, shortCandidateActive, shortCandidateState.directBreak, shortCandidateState.qualified, volumePass, bodyRatio, closeLocation, bearishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidence, higherWeightedScore, shortCandidateState.reversalEligible)
bool longBreakWyckoffBlocked = longCandidateState.directBreak and bullishSingleExceptionalBreak and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffPhase > 0 and wyckoffPhase < 5
bool shortBreakWyckoffBlocked = shortCandidateState.directBreak and bearishSingleExceptionalBreak and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffPhase > 0 and wyckoffPhase < 5
bool confirmedLongBreakPass = not longCandidateState.directBreak or longCandidateState.qualified and not longBreakWyckoffBlocked and (na(longCandidateState.ref) or close >= longCandidateState.ref - retestTolerance)
bool confirmedShortBreakPass = not shortCandidateState.directBreak or shortCandidateState.qualified and not shortBreakWyckoffBlocked and (na(shortCandidateState.ref) or close <= shortCandidateState.ref + retestTolerance)
bool exceptionalLongBreakPass = rawExceptionalLongBreakPass and not longBreakWyckoffBlocked and bullishEvidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
bool exceptionalShortBreakPass = rawExceptionalShortBreakPass and not shortBreakWyckoffBlocked and bearishEvidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
// Shared confirmation gates are top-level series because they are used by both
// the decision function and the later geometry/display functions. Keeping them
// local would make Pine report CE10272 outside the function scope.
float confirmedReversalEvidenceMinimum = minimumPlanEvidencePercent
bool bullishConfirmedReversalPass = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.reversalEligible and (resolvedStructureBias == 1 or longCandidateState.directBreak and movingAverageBias != -1 and close > sma24) and bullishEvidencePercent >= confirmedReversalEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
bool bearishConfirmedReversalPass = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.reversalEligible and (resolvedStructureBias == -1 or shortCandidateState.directBreak and movingAverageBias != 1 and close < sma24) and bearishEvidencePercent >= confirmedReversalEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
bool adxReady = not na(adxValue) and not na(plusDi) and not na(minusDi)
float adaptiveAdxStrongLevel = math.max(20.0, math.min(28.0, nz(ta.sma(adxValue, 50), 23.0) + 2.0))

// Compact market-regime layer: structure/location lead while ADX and averages
// only classify trend strength and the unsafe middle of a range.
f_market_regime_context() =>
    int htfBias = higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 1 : higherWeightedScore < 0 ? -1 : 0
    int regimeScore = resolvedStructureBias * 2 + movingAverageBias + majorChannelSignal + htfBias + (close > sma24 ? 1 : close < sma24 ? -1 : 0)
    bool rangeReady = sidewaysRangeReady and not na(sidewaysRangeHigh) and not na(sidewaysRangeLow) and sidewaysRangeHigh > sidewaysRangeLow
    float rangePosition = rangeReady and close >= sidewaysRangeLow and close <= sidewaysRangeHigh ? (close - sidewaysRangeLow) / math.max(sidewaysRangeHigh - sidewaysRangeLow, syminfo.mintick) : na
    bool rangeMiddleRegime = not na(rangePosition) and rangePosition >= 0.38 and rangePosition <= 0.62 and (not adxReady or adxValue < 20.0) and math.abs(sma24 - sma50) <= safeAtr * 0.60
    bool strongBullTrendRegime = not rangeMiddleRegime and regimeScore >= 5 and (not adxReady or adxValue >= 18.0 and plusDi >= minusDi - 1.0)
    bool strongBearTrendRegime = not rangeMiddleRegime and regimeScore <= -5 and (not adxReady or adxValue >= 18.0 and minusDi >= plusDi - 1.0)
    int marketRegimeCode = strongBullTrendRegime ? 2 : strongBearTrendRegime ? -2 : regimeScore >= 3 ? 1 : regimeScore <= -3 ? -1 : 0
    [marketRegimeCode, strongBullTrendRegime, strongBearTrendRegime, rangeMiddleRegime]

f_market_regime_caption(int regimeCode, bool rangeMiddle) =>
    rangeMiddle ? "عرضي • منتصف النطاق" : regimeCode == 2 ? "اتجاه صاعد قوي" : regimeCode == -2 ? "اتجاه هابط قوي" : regimeCode == 1 ? "ميل صاعد" : regimeCode == -1 ? "ميل هابط" : "انتقالي/عرضي"

[marketRegimeCode, strongBullTrendRegime, strongBearTrendRegime, rangeMiddleRegime] = f_market_regime_context()
// Structure and location remain hard safety gates; monitoring can precede a
// confirmed reversal, but execution cannot fight a strong opposite regime.
// A directional helper keeps long/short rules exactly symmetric and compiles
// one decision path instead of duplicating the same gates twice.
f_side_decision(bool isLong) =>
    int dir = isLong ? 1 : -1
    bool candidateActive = isLong ? longCandidateActive : shortCandidateActive
    int eventKind = isLong ? longCandidateState.eventKind : shortCandidateState.eventKind
    int stopKind = isLong ? longCandidateState.stopKind : shortCandidateState.stopKind
    bool directBreak = isLong ? longCandidateState.directBreak : shortCandidateState.directBreak
    bool qualified = isLong ? longCandidateState.qualified : shortCandidateState.qualified
    bool transitionEligible = isLong ? longCandidateState.transitionEligible : shortCandidateState.transitionEligible
    bool reversalEligible = isLong ? longCandidateState.reversalEligible : shortCandidateState.reversalEligible
    bool momentumExtensionUsed = isLong ? longCandidateState.momentumExtensionUsed : shortCandidateState.momentumExtensionUsed
    bool usesGuard = isLong ? longCandidateState.usesGuard : shortCandidateState.usesGuard
    float guardLevel = isLong ? longCandidateState.guardLevel : shortCandidateState.guardLevel
    float evidencePercent = isLong ? bullishEvidencePercent : bearishEvidencePercent
    int evidenceAxes = isLong ? bullishEvidenceAxes : bearishEvidenceAxes
    int opposingAxes = isLong ? bearishEvidenceAxes : bullishEvidenceAxes
    int evidence = isLong ? bullishEvidence : bearishEvidence
    int opposingEvidence = isLong ? bearishEvidence : bullishEvidence
    bool reversalPass = isLong ? bullishConfirmedReversalPass : bearishConfirmedReversalPass
    bool exceptionalBreakPass = isLong ? exceptionalLongBreakPass : exceptionalShortBreakPass
    bool confirmedBreakPass = isLong ? confirmedLongBreakPass : confirmedShortBreakPass
    bool actionableSetup = isLong ? actionableLongSetup : actionableShortSetup
    bool divergence = isLong ? recentBullishDivergence : recentBearishDivergence
    int sideObv = obvSignal * dir
    int sideVsa = vsaSignal * dir
    float sideClose = isLong ? closeLocation : 1.0 - closeLocation
    float sideDi = isLong ? plusDi : minusDi
    float opposingDi = isLong ? minusDi : plusDi

    int volumeBias = obvSignal != 0 ? obvSignal : volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= 1.0 ? (close > open ? 1 : close < open ? -1 : 0) : 0
    int higherBias = higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 1 : higherWeightedScore < 0 ? -1 : 0
    int principalTrendVote = majorTrendReady ? majorChannelBias : 0
    int sharedScore = resolvedStructureBias * 4 + horizontalSrSignal * 4 + principalTrendVote * 2 + majorChannelSignal + secondaryTrendSignal + higherBias * 2 + movingAverageBias * 2 + wyckoffContextSignal * 2 + volumeBias
    int contextScore = sharedScore + (candidateActive and not directBreak ? dealingRangeSignal : 0)
    int contextBias = contextScore >= 5 ? 1 : contextScore <= -5 ? -1 : 0
    bool strongOpposite = isLong ? strongBearTrendRegime : strongBullTrendRegime
    bool majorOpposed = majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == -dir
    bool immediateDmiBlock = adxReady and adxValue >= adaptiveAdxStrongLevel and opposingDi >= sideDi + 6.0
    bool breakEvent = eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK
    bool retestEvent = eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST
    bool wyckoffEvent = stopKind % 10 == 6 or reversalEligible
    bool technicalLocationSupport = horizontalSrSignal == dir or zoneSignal == dir or dealingRangeSignal == dir or wyckoffEvent
    bool htfOpposed = requireHigherTimeframeAlignment and higherTimeframesReady and higherWeightedScore * dir < 0
    bool htfStrongOpposed = htfOpposed and higherWeightedScore * dir <= -2
    bool htfCounterTrendOverride = reversalPass or htfStrongOpposed and resolvedStructureBias == dir and evidenceAxes >= 3 and evidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 4.0 and (retestEvent and technicalLocationSupport or exceptionalBreakPass)
    bool htfPlanPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or not higherTimeframesReady or higherWeightedScore * dir >= -1 or htfCounterTrendOverride
    bool primarySrPass = horizontalSrSignal != -dir or usesGuard and not na(guardLevel) and (isLong ? close >= guardLevel - retestTolerance : close <= guardLevel + retestTolerance)
    bool localCounterWaveTrigger = retestEvent and technicalLocationSupport or breakEvent and (stopKind % 10 == 2 or horizontalSrSignal == dir or resolvedStructureBias == dir)
    bool majorCounterWaveOverride = majorOpposed and not strongOpposite and resolvedStructureBias == dir and evidenceAxes >= 3 and evidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 2.0 and localCounterWaveTrigger
    bool contextPass = (not majorOpposed or majorCounterWaveOverride or reversalPass) and not structureDirectionConflict and (contextBias != -dir or majorCounterWaveOverride) and (not strongOpposite or reversalPass)
    bool watchContextPass = contextPass or reversalEligible and not strongOpposite
    bool rangeRetestPass = retestEvent and technicalLocationSupport
    bool regimeWatchPass = (not strongOpposite or reversalEligible) and (not rangeMiddleRegime or breakEvent or rangeRetestPass)
    bool directionalCandle = isLong ? close > open : close < open
    bool watchCandle = retestEvent ? sideClose >= 0.50 and sideVsa != -1 : breakEvent ? bodyRatio >= 0.22 and sideClose >= 0.53 : directionalCandle and sideClose >= 0.53
    bool watchVolume = not volumeAvailable or (retestEvent ? sideObv != -1 and sideVsa != -1 : breakEvent ? sideObv != -1 or standardVolumeEffort : sideVsa != -1 and (sideObv != -1 or divergence or wyckoffEvent))
    bool watchEvent = candidateActive and not momentumExtensionUsed and (qualified or transitionEligible or reversalEligible) and confirmedBreakPass
    bool axisMajority = evidenceAxes >= 2 and evidenceAxes > opposingAxes
    bool formationOpportunity = watchEvent and primarySrPass and watchContextPass and regimeWatchPass and not immediateDmiBlock and axisMajority and evidencePercent >= formationEvidencePercent
    bool monitoringOpportunity = formationOpportunity and watchCandle and watchVolume and evidencePercent >= monitoringEvidencePercent and evidence > opposingEvidence

    // V98.3/V22.3: preserve early monitoring, but require a true plan-level evidence floor.
    // Strong retests at a validated technical location get a small -1 point allowance;
    // unconfirmed early events need +1 point unless a confirmed reversal already exists.
    bool strongRetestLocation = retestEvent and technicalLocationSupport
    float effectivePlanEvidenceFloor = math.max(monitoringEvidencePercent, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + (eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY and not reversalPass ? 1.0 : strongRetestLocation ? -1.0 : 0.0))
    bool planEvidencePass = evidencePercent >= effectivePlanEvidenceFloor

    // Execution-candle quality is event-aware. The candidate is not deleted when this
    // fails; it stays under monitoring so a later valid close/retest can activate it.
    bool breakContinuationCandle = breakEvent and directBreak and qualified and directionalCandle and bodyRatio >= 0.16 and sideClose >= 0.52 and sideVsa != -1 and (technicalLocationSupport or resolvedStructureBias == dir or majorChannelSignal == dir or secondaryTrendSignal == dir)
    bool entryCandle = retestEvent ? directionalCandle and sideClose >= (wyckoffEvent ? 0.52 : 0.54) : breakEvent ? exceptionalBreakPass or bodyRatio >= 0.26 and sideClose >= 0.55 or breakContinuationCandle : directionalCandle and sideClose >= 0.55
    bool entryVolume = not volumeAvailable or (retestEvent ? sideObv != -1 and sideVsa != -1 and (relativeVolume <= 1.35 or sideObv == 1 or standardVolumeEffort) : breakEvent ? exceptionalBreakPass or sideObv != -1 and (relativeVolume >= 0.90 or standardVolumeEffort or sideVsa == 1) : sideVsa != -1 and (sideObv == 1 or divergence or wyckoffEvent))
    bool entryDmi = not immediateDmiBlock and (not adxReady or (breakEvent ? adxValue >= 13.0 and sideDi >= opposingDi - 1.5 or exceptionalBreakPass : retestEvent ? sideDi >= opposingDi - 3.0 or reversalPass : wyckoffEvent or sideDi >= opposingDi - 1.5))
    bool wyckoffTransitionPass = wyckoffEvent and not strongOpposite and resolvedStructureBias != -dir
    bool trendPass = majorCounterWaveOverride or contextBias != -dir and movingAverageBias != -dir or retestEvent and contextBias != -dir and movingAverageBias != -dir or reversalPass or wyckoffTransitionPass
    bool decisionEdge = axisMajority and evidence > opposingEvidence and evidencePercent >= monitoringEvidencePercent and (resolvedStructureBias != -dir or reversalPass or wyckoffTransitionPass) and (higherWeightedScore * dir >= -1 or htfCounterTrendOverride or wyckoffTransitionPass)

    // A raw break in the middle of a sideways range is no longer enough by itself
    // for execution. It must be exceptional, aligned with validated horizontal S/R,
    // or backed by structure plus three evidence axes. Retests keep their location gate.
    bool rangeBreakPlanPass = breakEvent and (exceptionalBreakPass or horizontalSrSignal == dir or resolvedStructureBias == dir and evidenceAxes >= 3)
    bool rangePlanPass = not rangeMiddleRegime or rangeBreakPlanPass or rangeRetestPass
    bool planReady = actionableSetup and not momentumExtensionUsed and not immediateDmiBlock and swingLevelsCompatible and primarySrPass and contextPass and htfPlanPass and rangePlanPass and confirmedBreakPass and decisionEdge
    bool balancedSecondaryConfirmation = entryVolume and entryDmi or not directBreak and evidenceAxes >= 3 and evidenceAxes > opposingAxes and evidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 2.0 and (entryVolume or entryDmi)
    bool reversalExecutionReady = eventKind != SETUP_EVENT_EARLY or reversalPass
    bool excellentPlan = planReady and planEvidencePass and entryCandle and trendPass and balancedSecondaryConfirmation and reversalExecutionReady
    bool reversalFormation = (eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY or reversalEligible) and not reversalPass and not excellentPlan
    bool rangeBreakBlocked = rangeMiddleRegime and breakEvent and not rangePlanPass
    int blockCode = not candidateActive ? 0 : momentumExtensionUsed ? 8 : immediateDmiBlock ? 13 : reversalFormation ? 12 : htfStrongOpposed and not htfPlanPass ? 9 : rangeBreakBlocked ? 7 : breakEvent and not entryCandle ? 10 : breakEvent and not entryVolume ? 11 : not watchEvent or not watchCandle ? 2 : evidencePercent < monitoringEvidencePercent or not axisMajority ? 1 : not watchContextPass or not primarySrPass ? 3 : not watchVolume ? 4 : excellentPlan ? 6 : 5
    [formationOpportunity, monitoringOpportunity, excellentPlan, blockCode]

[longFormationOpportunity, longTechnicalOpportunity, excellentLongPlan, longDecisionBlockCode] = f_side_decision(true)
[shortFormationOpportunity, shortTechnicalOpportunity, excellentShortTechnical, shortDecisionBlockCode] = f_side_decision(false)
bool excellentShortPlan = shortTradesAllowed and excellentShortTechnical
bool simultaneousExcellentConflict = excellentLongPlan and excellentShortPlan
int requestedPlanDirection = excellentLongPlan and (not simultaneousExcellentConflict or bullishEvidence > bearishEvidence) ? 1 : excellentShortPlan and (not simultaneousExcellentConflict or bearishEvidence > bullishEvidence) ? -1 : 0
bool requestedPlanHtfOpposed = requestedPlanDirection != 0 and higherTimeframesReady and higherWeightedScore * requestedPlanDirection < 0
bool requestedPlanMajorOpposed = requestedPlanDirection != 0 and majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == -requestedPlanDirection
bool strongOpportunity = requestedPlanDirection == 1 ? bullishEvidenceAxes >= 3 and bullishEvidenceAxes >= bearishEvidenceAxes + 2 and bullishEvidencePercent >= 68.0 and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 3 : requestedPlanDirection == -1 ? bearishEvidenceAxes >= 3 and bearishEvidenceAxes >= bullishEvidenceAxes + 2 and bearishEvidencePercent >= 68.0 and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 3 : false
int requestedPlanQualityTier = requestedPlanDirection == 0 ? 0 : strongOpportunity ? 3 : 2
int longTechnicalOpportunityKind = longCandidateState.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY or longCandidateState.reversalEligible ? SETUP_EVENT_EARLY : longCandidateState.eventKind
int shortTechnicalOpportunityKind = shortCandidateState.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY or shortCandidateState.reversalEligible ? SETUP_EVENT_EARLY : shortCandidateState.eventKind

// الخطة المنتهية بالوقف لا تبقى كخطوط أو أهداف تاريخية؛ يحتفظ الشارت
// بعلامة الوقف الصغيرة فقط. الخطط المنجزة أو الملتبسة تبقى باهتة للمراجعة.
bool planEndedThisBar = not planActive and not na(planOutcomeBar) and planOutcomeBar == bar_index
if planEndedThisBar
    // Active plans are never cleared here. Cleanup runs only after planActive
    // becomes false on the confirmed outcome bar; the plan is then reduced to one marker.
    entryLine := f_clear_line(entryLine)
    stopLine := f_clear_line(stopLine)
    target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
    target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
    target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
    entryLevelLabel := f_clear_label(entryLevelLabel)
    stopLevelLabel := f_clear_label(stopLevelLabel)
    target1LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target1LevelLabel)
    target2LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target2LevelLabel)
    target3LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target3LevelLabel)
    activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
    planHighSinceEntry := na
    planLowSinceEntry := na
bool planCooldownComplete = na(lastPlanExitBar) or bar_index - lastPlanExitBar > planReentryCooldownBars
bool newPlanCreatedEvent = false

// Stop/target geometry is isolated from the global program. The function is
// called on every calculation, while candidateValid preserves the original
// entry gate. This removes the largest remaining main-body block.
f_candidate_plan_geometry(int requestedDirection, int requestedQualityTier, bool htfOpposedQuality) =>
    bool isLong = requestedDirection == 1
    float setupReferenceLevel = isLong ? longCandidateState.ref : shortCandidateState.ref
    int setupEventBar = isLong ? longCandidateState.eventBar : shortCandidateState.eventBar
    float genericSwingRange = f_setup_wave_range(isLong, setupEventBar, math.max(retestWindow * 4, pivotBars * 12), lastSwingHigh, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLow, lastSwingLowBar)
    float setupRawRange = isLong ? longCandidateState.rng : shortCandidateState.rng
    float setupWaveOrigin = isLong ? longCandidateState.waveOrigin : shortCandidateState.waveOrigin
    float setupNRange = isLong ? longCandidateState.nRange : shortCandidateState.nRange
    float baseContextualStopAnchor = isLong ? longCandidateState.stop : shortCandidateState.stop
    int setupStopKind = isLong ? longCandidateState.stopKind : shortCandidateState.stopKind
    int setupEventKind = isLong ? longCandidateState.eventKind : shortCandidateState.eventKind
    bool setupUsesGuard = isLong ? longCandidateState.usesGuard : shortCandidateState.usesGuard
    float setupGuardLevel = isLong ? longCandidateState.guardLevel : shortCandidateState.guardLevel
    bool setupMomentumExtensionUsed = isLong ? longCandidateState.momentumExtensionUsed : shortCandidateState.momentumExtensionUsed
    int candidateStopMode = int(math.floor(float(setupStopKind) / 10.0))
    bool protectedContextStop = candidateStopMode == 2
    float setupSpecificStopAnchor = candidateStopMode == 1 ? baseContextualStopAnchor : na
    float nearestFibStopLevel = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 2.0), fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float majorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : majorChannelBias == 1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na) : (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelBias == -1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na)
    float stopCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, isLong ? highVolumeSupportLow : highVolumeResistanceHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleLow : lastKeyCandleHigh, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float protectedSwingStop = isLong ? lastSwingLow : lastSwingHigh
    [minimumStopAtrMultiple, maximumStopAtrMultiple] = f_stop_atr_limits(chartSeconds)
    float stopMaximumDistance = safeAtr * (maximumStopAtrMultiple + stopAtrBuffer)
    // ترتيب الوقف صارم: 1) قمم/قيعان ومستويات أفقية وHTF، 2) ترند/قناة،
    // 3) SMA، 4) تصحيح فيبوناتشي، 5) عرض/طلب، ثم الشمعة كحل أخير.
    float primaryClusterStop = isLong ? f_nearest_price_level(false, close, safeAtr * 0.10, nearestSupportZoneLow, nextSupportZoneLow, strongResistanceRoleReversed and close >= brokenResistanceLow - breakBuffer ? brokenResistanceLow : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : f_nearest_price_level(true, close, safeAtr * 0.10, nearestResistanceZoneHigh, nextResistanceZoneHigh, strongSupportRoleReversed and close <= brokenSupportHigh + breakBuffer ? brokenSupportHigh : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float historicalPivotStop = f_nearest_stored_pivot(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, stopMaximumDistance, isLong ? srLowPivotPrices : srHighPivotPrices, isLong ? srLowPivotBars : srHighPivotBars, isLong ? srLowPivotTimes : srHighPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    float primaryPivotStop = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, protectedSwingStop, historicalPivotStop, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float higherTimeframeStop = isLong ? higherTimeframeSupport : higherTimeframeResistance
    float structuralStopRaw = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, primaryClusterStop, primaryPivotStop, higherTimeframeStop, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float structuralStopAnchor = not na(structuralStopRaw) and math.abs(structuralStopRaw - close) <= stopMaximumDistance ? structuralStopRaw : na
    float trendStopRaw = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, majorChannelStopLevel, isLong ? (secondaryRisingTrendReady ? secondaryRisingTrendNow : na) : (secondaryFallingTrendReady ? secondaryFallingTrendNow : na), na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float trendStopAnchor = not na(trendStopRaw) and math.abs(trendStopRaw - close) <= stopMaximumDistance ? trendStopRaw : na
    float maStopRaw = isLong ? nearestMaSupportForStop : nearestMaResistanceForStop
    float maStopAnchor = not na(maStopRaw) and math.abs(maStopRaw - close) <= stopMaximumDistance ? maStopRaw : na
    // A lower-priority level must be skipped when it lies outside the permitted
    // stop envelope; otherwise a distant Fibonacci level can block a valid zone.
    float fibStopAnchor = not na(nearestFibStopLevel) and math.abs(nearestFibStopLevel - close) <= stopMaximumDistance ? nearestFibStopLevel : na
    float zoneStopRaw = isLong ? (demandZoneAnalysisReady ? demandZoneLow : na) : (supplyZoneAnalysisReady ? supplyZoneHigh : na)
    float zoneStopAnchor = not na(zoneStopRaw) and math.abs(zoneStopRaw - close) <= stopMaximumDistance ? zoneStopRaw : na
    float fallbackStopAnchor = not na(baseContextualStopAnchor) ? baseContextualStopAnchor : stopCandleLevel
    float priorityStopAnchor = not na(structuralStopAnchor) ? structuralStopAnchor : not na(trendStopAnchor) ? trendStopAnchor : not na(maStopAnchor) ? maStopAnchor : not na(fibStopAnchor) ? fibStopAnchor : not na(zoneStopAnchor) ? zoneStopAnchor : fallbackStopAnchor
    // إبطال النموذج أو شمعة الاختبار لا يجوز تضييقه بمستوى أعلى أولوية؛ يؤخذ الأبعد فقط.
    float thesisInvalidationAnchor = not na(setupSpecificStopAnchor) ? setupSpecificStopAnchor : baseContextualStopAnchor
    float hierarchyStopAnchor = not na(thesisInvalidationAnchor) and not na(priorityStopAnchor) ? (isLong ? math.min(thesisInvalidationAnchor, priorityStopAnchor) : math.max(thesisInvalidationAnchor, priorityStopAnchor)) : not na(thesisInvalidationAnchor) ? thesisInvalidationAnchor : priorityStopAnchor
    float contextualStopAnchor = protectedContextStop and not na(baseContextualStopAnchor) and not na(hierarchyStopAnchor) ? (isLong ? math.min(hierarchyStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor) : math.max(hierarchyStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor)) : hierarchyStopAnchor
    bool contextualAnchorValid = not na(contextualStopAnchor) and (isLong ? contextualStopAnchor < close - syminfo.mintick : contextualStopAnchor > close + syminfo.mintick)
    bool priorityAnchorValid = not na(priorityStopAnchor) and (isLong ? priorityStopAnchor < close - syminfo.mintick : priorityStopAnchor > close + syminfo.mintick)
    float validInvalidationAnchor = contextualAnchorValid ? contextualStopAnchor : priorityAnchorValid ? priorityStopAnchor : na
    float closeInvalidationBufferAtr = stopUsesTouch ? stopAtrBuffer : math.min(stopAtrBuffer, setupEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST or setupStopKind % 10 == 6 ? 0.15 : 0.10)
    float technicalInvalidationPrice = isLong ? validInvalidationAnchor - safeAtr * closeInvalidationBufferAtr : validInvalidationAnchor + safeAtr * closeInvalidationBufferAtr
    float currentPriceTick = f_market_tick(close)
    float minimumStopDistance = safeAtr * minimumStopAtrMultiple
    float validStopUnrounded = isLong ? math.min(technicalInvalidationPrice, close - math.max(currentPriceTick, minimumStopDistance)) : math.max(technicalInvalidationPrice, close + math.max(currentPriceTick, minimumStopDistance))
    float validStop = isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(validStopUnrounded) : f_ceil_to_tick(validStopUnrounded)
    float technicalRisk = math.max(math.abs(close - validStop), currentPriceTick)
    float confirmedWaveRange = not na(setupRawRange) and setupRawRange > currentPriceTick ? setupRawRange : genericSwingRange
    float waveRiskFloor = not na(confirmedWaveRange) ? math.min(safeAtr * maximumStopAtrMultiple, confirmedWaveRange * 0.15) : 0.0
    float targetSizingRisk = math.max(technicalRisk, math.max(safeAtr * minimumStopAtrMultiple, waveRiskFloor))
    float risk = technicalRisk
    float swingRange = not na(confirmedWaveRange) ? math.max(confirmedWaveRange, targetSizingRisk) : targetSizingRisk * 2.0

    // Risk already scales with the chart's structure and ATR. Do not shrink
    // reward below 1R on fast charts. Larger intervals may widen the ladder.
    float rewardHorizonFactor = math.max(targetHorizonFactor, 1.0)
    // The timeframe factor expands fallback R/Fibonacci objectives only.
    // Real structural levels keep their natural price and use the unscaled R ladder.
    float structuralRr1 = math.max(rr1, minimumTarget1RiskMultiple)
    float orderedRr1 = math.max(rr1 * rewardHorizonFactor, structuralRr1)
    float orderedRr2 = math.max(rr2 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr1 + 0.60)
    float orderedRr3 = math.max(rr3 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr2 + 0.75)
    float minimumTarget1Distance = targetSizingRisk * orderedRr1
    float minimumTarget2Distance = targetSizingRisk * orderedRr2
    float minimumTarget3Distance = targetSizingRisk * orderedRr3
    float minimumTargetGap = targetSizingRisk * 0.40
    // الغلاف الأقصى لا يجوز أن يصبح أصغر من الحد الأدنى الذي اختاره المستخدم.
    float maximumTarget1Distance = math.max(math.max(targetSizingRisk * 2.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget1Distance), swingRange * 0.40)
    float maximumTarget2Distance = math.max(math.max(targetSizingRisk * 4.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget2Distance + minimumTargetGap), swingRange * 0.65)
    float maximumTarget3Distance = math.max(math.max(targetSizingRisk * 6.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget3Distance + minimumTargetGap), swingRange)
    float candidateTarget1 = na
    float candidateTarget2 = na
    float candidateTarget3 = na
    // امتدادات 23.6/38.2/50/61.8/100% تُحسب دائما كطبقة لاحقة؛
    // حتى وضع R لا يلغيها من البحث إذا غابت المستويات الأعلى أولوية.
    float brokenLevel = nz(setupReferenceLevel, isLong ? nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : nz(lastSwingLow, close))
    [fibTarget1, fibTarget2, fibTarget3, fibObjective1, fibObjective2, fibObjective3] = f_build_fib_extension_targets(isLong, brokenLevel, swingRange, close, minimumTarget1Distance, minimumTarget2Distance, minimumTarget3Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget2Distance, maximumTarget3Distance, minimumTargetGap)
    candidateTarget1 := fibTarget1
    candidateTarget2 := fibTarget2
    candidateTarget3 := fibTarget3

    float nWaveTarget = not na(setupWaveOrigin) and not na(setupNRange) ? (isLong ? setupWaveOrigin + setupNRange : setupWaveOrigin - setupNRange) : na
    float vWaveTarget = not na(setupWaveOrigin) and not na(setupReferenceLevel) and (isLong ? setupReferenceLevel > setupWaveOrigin : setupReferenceLevel < setupWaveOrigin) ? (isLong ? setupReferenceLevel + (setupReferenceLevel - setupWaveOrigin) : setupReferenceLevel - (setupWaveOrigin - setupReferenceLevel)) : na
    float measuredTarget = not na(nWaveTarget) ? nWaveTarget : (isLong ? close + swingRange : close - swingRange)

    // محرك الأهداف: القمم/القيعان التاريخية والعوائق البنيوية أولاً، ثم
    // أولوية صارمة للأهداف: 1) قمم/قيعان ومناطق أفقية، 2) ترند/قناة،
    // 3) SMA50/200 كعوائق ديناميكية، 4) مناطق العرض/الطلب ووايكوف،
    // 5) عرض/طلب ووايكوف، 6) امتداد فيبوناتشي وهدف الموجة N/القناة عند غياب السابق.
    float minimumStructuralTargetDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float majorChannelBoundaryTarget = majorChannelReady ? (isLong ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerNow) : majorTrendReady ? majorChannelBaseNow : na
    float majorChannelMeasuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMajorChannelTarget : bearishMajorChannelTarget
    float horizontalObstacleLevel = isLong ? nearestResistanceZoneLow : nearestSupportZoneHigh
    float secondaryHorizontalObstacleLevel = isLong ? nextResistanceZoneLow : nextSupportZoneHigh
    float opposingZoneBoundary = isLong ? f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick, supplyZoneAnalysisReady ? supplyZoneLow : na, bearishBreakerAnalysisReady ? bearishBreakerLow : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick, demandZoneAnalysisReady ? demandZoneHigh : na, bullishBreakerAnalysisReady ? bullishBreakerHigh : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float targetWyckoffLevel = wyckoffRangeReady ? (isLong ? wyckoffRangeResistance : wyckoffRangeSupport) : na
    float historicalPivotObstacle = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, isLong ? srHighPivotTimes : srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    bool insidePrimaryOpposingSrZone = isLong ? (not na(nearestResistanceZoneLow) and close >= nearestResistanceZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= nearestResistanceZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick) : (not na(nearestSupportZoneLow) and close >= nearestSupportZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= nearestSupportZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick)
    bool insideSecondaryOpposingSrZone = isLong ? (not na(nextResistanceZoneLow) and close >= nextResistanceZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= nextResistanceZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick) : (not na(nextSupportZoneLow) and close >= nextSupportZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= nextSupportZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick)
    bool insideOpposingHardZone = (isLong ? supplyZoneAnalysisReady and close >= supplyZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= supplyZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick : demandZoneAnalysisReady and close >= demandZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= demandZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick) or (isLong ? bearishBreakerAnalysisReady and close >= bearishBreakerLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + syminfo.mintick : bullishBreakerAnalysisReady and close >= bullishBreakerLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= bullishBreakerHigh + syminfo.mintick) or insidePrimaryOpposingSrZone or insideSecondaryOpposingSrZone
    float nearestStructuralObstacle = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, historicalPivotObstacle, isLong ? higherTimeframeResistance : higherTimeframeSupport, horizontalObstacleLevel, secondaryHorizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, na, na, na, na)
    float nearestTrendObstacle = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float nearestHardObstacle = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, nearestStructuralObstacle, nearestTrendObstacle, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float hardObstacleDistanceSizingR = not na(nearestHardObstacle) ? math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) / targetSizingRisk : na
    float eventEvidencePercent = isLong ? bullishEvidencePercent : bearishEvidencePercent
    int eventEvidenceAxes = isLong ? bullishEvidenceAxes : bearishEvidenceAxes
    int opposingEventAxes = isLong ? bearishEvidenceAxes : bullishEvidenceAxes
    bool limitedRoomCandidate = not insideOpposingHardZone and not na(hardObstacleDistanceSizingR) and hardObstacleDistanceSizingR >= minimumObstacleRoomRiskMultiple and hardObstacleDistanceSizingR < orderedRr1
    bool limitedRoomApproved = limitedRoomCandidate and eventEvidenceAxes >= 2 and eventEvidenceAxes > opposingEventAxes and eventEvidencePercent >= 58.0 and (setupEventKind != SETUP_EVENT_BREAK or (isLong ? confirmedLongBreakPass : confirmedShortBreakPass) and eventEvidencePercent >= 61.0)
    float requiredTarget1R = limitedRoomApproved ? math.max(minimumObstacleRoomRiskMultiple, hardObstacleDistanceSizingR) : orderedRr1
    bool roomToObstacleValid = not insideOpposingHardZone and (na(hardObstacleDistanceSizingR) or hardObstacleDistanceSizingR >= orderedRr1 or limitedRoomApproved)
    float effectiveMinimumTarget1Distance = targetSizingRisk * requiredTarget1R
    float htfTargetLevel = isLong ? higherTimeframeResistance : higherTimeframeSupport
    // Profit targets prioritize real structure. Fast MAs, generic retracement and key-candle prices
    // remain contextual/stop evidence but no longer outrank wave/channel objectives as profit targets.
    array<float> targetLevels = f_target_level_array(horizontalObstacleLevel, secondaryHorizontalObstacleLevel, htfTargetLevel, historicalPivotObstacle, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, fibObjective1, majorChannelMeasuredTarget, measuredTarget, vWaveTarget)
    float rankedTarget1 = f_ranked_array_level(isLong, close, effectiveMinimumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, targetLevels)
    bool hardObstacleMeetsTargetDistance = not na(nearestHardObstacle) and math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) + syminfo.mintick * 0.5 >= effectiveMinimumTarget1Distance
    bool hardObstacleIsCloserOrEqual = not na(nearestHardObstacle) and (isLong ? nearestHardObstacle <= nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1) + syminfo.mintick : nearestHardObstacle >= nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1) - syminfo.mintick)
    bool firstTargetUsesHardObstacle = hardObstacleMeetsTargetDistance and hardObstacleIsCloserOrEqual
    candidateTarget1 := firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? nearestHardObstacle : nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1)
    float target2MinimumDistance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * orderedRr2, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float historicalPivotTarget2 = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, target2MinimumDistance, maximumTarget2Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, isLong ? srHighPivotTimes : srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    array.set(targetLevels, 3, historicalPivotTarget2)
    array.set(targetLevels, 7, fibObjective2)
    float rankedTarget2 = f_ranked_array_level(isLong, close, target2MinimumDistance, maximumTarget2Distance, targetLevels)
    float fallbackTarget2Distance = math.max(target2MinimumDistance, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    candidateTarget2 := nz(rankedTarget2, isLong ? close + fallbackTarget2Distance : close - fallbackTarget2Distance)
    float target3MinimumDistance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * orderedRr3, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float historicalPivotTarget3 = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, target3MinimumDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, isLong ? srHighPivotTimes : srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    array.set(targetLevels, 3, historicalPivotTarget3)
    array.set(targetLevels, 7, fibObjective3)
    float rankedTarget3 = f_ranked_array_level(isLong, close, target3MinimumDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, targetLevels)
    float fallbackTarget3Distance = math.max(target3MinimumDistance, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    candidateTarget3 := nz(rankedTarget3, isLong ? close + fallbackTarget3Distance : close - fallbackTarget3Distance)
    // Targets round toward the entry side: a long objective must not be
    // pushed above resistance, and a short objective must not be pushed below support.
    candidateTarget1 := isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget1) : f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget1)
    candidateTarget2 := isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget2) : f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget2)
    candidateTarget3 := isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget3) : f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget3)
    // This is stop distance as a percentage of instrument price, not portfolio
    // capital at risk. Position sizing remains external to the indicator.
    float planPriceDistancePercent = close > 0 ? risk / close * 100.0 : 1000.0
    float planRiskAtrMultiple = safeAtr > 0 ? risk / safeAtr : 1000.0
    float effectivePriceDistancePercentLimit = math.min(maxPlanPriceDistancePercent, chartSeconds <= 60 ? 3.0 : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 4.0 : chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 5.5 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 7.0 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 9.0 : chartSeconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 12.0 : 20.0)
    bool priceDistancePercentValid = planPriceDistancePercent <= effectivePriceDistancePercentLimit
    bool riskAtrValid = planRiskAtrMultiple <= maximumStopAtrMultiple + 0.0001
    bool riskDistanceValid = priceDistancePercentValid and riskAtrValid
    bool stopPriceValid = validStop >= syminfo.mintick and (isLong ? validStop < close : validStop > close)
    bool guardStopValid = not setupUsesGuard or na(setupGuardLevel) or (isLong ? validStop < setupGuardLevel - currentPriceTick * 0.25 : validStop > setupGuardLevel + currentPriceTick * 0.25)
    bool planNumbersReady = not na(close) and not na(validStop) and not na(risk) and not na(candidateTarget1) and not na(candidateTarget2) and not na(candidateTarget3)
    bool targetPricesPositive = planNumbersReady and candidateTarget1 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget2 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget3 >= syminfo.mintick
    bool targetOrderValid = planNumbersReady and (isLong ? candidateTarget1 > close and candidateTarget2 > candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 > candidateTarget2 : candidateTarget1 < close and candidateTarget2 < candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 < candidateTarget2)
    float target1RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) / risk : na
    float target2RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) / risk : na
    float target3RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget3 - close) / risk : na
    float targetRoundingToleranceR = planNumbersReady and risk > 0 ? currentPriceTick / risk + 0.0001 : 0.0001
    bool standardTargetSequenceValid = planNumbersReady and target1RiskMultiple + targetRoundingToleranceR >= requiredTarget1R and target2RiskMultiple + targetRoundingToleranceR >= orderedRr2 and target3RiskMultiple + targetRoundingToleranceR >= orderedRr3 and target2RiskMultiple >= target1RiskMultiple + 0.39 and target3RiskMultiple >= target2RiskMultiple + 0.39
    bool targetRewardValid = standardTargetSequenceValid
    bool excellentFinalEventPass = setupEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST or setupEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK and (isLong ? confirmedLongBreakPass : confirmedShortBreakPass) or setupEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY and (isLong ? bullishConfirmedReversalPass : bearishConfirmedReversalPass)
    bool excellentFinalGeometryPass = standardTargetSequenceValid and riskAtrValid and not setupMomentumExtensionUsed
    bool excellentFinalProfilePass = requestedQualityTier >= 2 and excellentFinalEventPass
    bool candidateValid = requestedDirection != 0 and planNumbersReady and riskDistanceValid and roomToObstacleValid and stopPriceValid and guardStopValid and targetPricesPositive and targetOrderValid and targetRewardValid and excellentFinalGeometryPass and excellentFinalProfilePass
    int score = isLong ? bullishEvidence : bearishEvidence
    bool strongGeometry = target1RiskMultiple >= 1.40 and target2RiskMultiple >= 2.10 and target3RiskMultiple >= 2.90 and planRiskAtrMultiple <= maximumStopAtrMultiple * 0.78
    int finalQualityTier = htfOpposedQuality or limitedRoomApproved ? 1 : requestedQualityTier == 3 and strongGeometry ? 3 : 2
    [candidateValid, requestedDirection, validStop, candidateTarget1, candidateTarget2, candidateTarget3, score, finalQualityTier, setupEventKind, setupStopKind % 10]

[candidatePlanValid, candidatePlanDirection, candidatePlanStop, candidatePlanTarget1, candidatePlanTarget2, candidatePlanTarget3, candidatePlanScore, candidatePlanQualityTier, candidatePlanEventKind, candidatePlanEventSubKind] = f_candidate_plan_geometry(requestedPlanDirection, requestedPlanQualityTier, requestedPlanHtfOpposed or requestedPlanMajorOpposed)

if (barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and planCooldownComplete)
    if candidatePlanValid
        bool isLong = candidatePlanDirection == 1
        planDirection := candidatePlanDirection
        planActive := true
        planStartBar := bar_index
        planEntry := close
        planStop := candidatePlanStop
        planTarget1 := candidatePlanTarget1
        planTarget2 := candidatePlanTarget2
        planTarget3 := candidatePlanTarget3
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planScore := candidatePlanScore
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planQualityTier := candidatePlanQualityTier
        planHtfOpposed := requestedPlanHtfOpposed
        planEntryKind := candidatePlanEventKind
        planEntrySubKind := candidatePlanEventSubKind
        planOutcomeBar := na
        planOutcomeKind := 0
        planOutcomePrice := na
        newPlanCreatedEvent := true
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na
        if isLong
            f_clear_setup_candidate(longCandidateState)
        else
            f_clear_setup_candidate(shortCandidateState)

        // هذه هي شمعة القرار الفعلية: لا تظهر العلامة إلا بعد إغلاق الشمعة
        // واكتمال الإعداد والأدلة وإدارة المخاطرة، لا لمجرد اقتراب السعر.

        activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
        activePlanOutcomeMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanOutcomeMarker)
        color confirmedEntryColor = isLong ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor
        entryLine := f_replace_plan_line(entryLine, true, planEntry, confirmedEntryColor, planEntryLineTransparency, line.style_solid)
        stopLine := f_replace_plan_line(stopLine, true, planStop, #F23645, planStopLineTransparency, line.style_solid)
        target1Line := f_replace_plan_line(target1Line, true, planTarget1, #22C55E, planTarget1LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target2Line := f_replace_plan_line(target2Line, true, planTarget2, #22C55E, planTarget2LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target3Line := f_replace_plan_line(target3Line, true, planTarget3, #22C55E, planTarget3LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        entryLevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(entryLevelLabel, true, planEntry, confirmedEntryColor)
        stopLevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(stopLevelLabel, true, planStop, #F23645)
        target1LevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(target1LevelLabel, true, planTarget1, #22C55E)
        target2LevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(target2LevelLabel, true, planTarget2, #22C55E)
        target3LevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(target3LevelLabel, true, planTarget3, #22C55E)
    else
        planDirection := 0
        planActive := false
        planStartBar := na
        planEntry := na
        planStop := na
        planTarget1 := na
        planTarget2 := na
        planTarget3 := na
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planScore := 0
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planQualityTier := 0
        planHtfOpposed := false
        planEntryKind := 0
        planEntrySubKind := 0
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na

// عزل تحديث الرسومات في دالة يقلل حجم النطاق الرئيسي. الدالة لا تعيد
// إسناد مراجع عامة؛ هي تعدل خصائص line/label فقط، وهي أنواع مرجعية.
f_update_plan_level_label(label id, int x, float level, float offset, string caption, color textColor) =>
    label.set_xy(id, x, level + offset)
    label.set_text(id, caption + f_price(level))
    label.set_color(id, color.new(color.black, 100))
    label.set_textcolor(id, textColor)

f_entry_type_caption(bool isLong, int kind) =>
    kind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK ? (isLong ? "اختراق مؤكد" : "كسر مؤكد") : kind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST ? "إعادة اختبار" : "انعكاس مؤكد"

if planActive
    color activeEntryVisualColor = planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor
    if na(entryLine)
        entryLine := f_new_plan_line(planEntry, activeEntryVisualColor, planEntryLineTransparency, line.style_solid)
    if na(stopLine)
        stopLine := f_new_plan_line(planStop, #F23645, planStopLineTransparency, line.style_solid)
    if na(target1Line)
        target1Line := f_new_plan_line(planTarget1, #22C55E, planTarget1LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
    if na(target2Line)
        target2Line := f_new_plan_line(planTarget2, #22C55E, planTarget2LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
    if na(target3Line)
        target3Line := f_new_plan_line(planTarget3, #22C55E, planTarget3LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
    if na(entryLevelLabel)
        entryLevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planEntry, activeEntryVisualColor)
    if na(stopLevelLabel)
        stopLevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planStop, #F23645)
    if na(target1LevelLabel)
        target1LevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planTarget1, #22C55E)
    if na(target2LevelLabel)
        target2LevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planTarget2, #22C55E)
    if na(target3LevelLabel)
        target3LevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planTarget3, #22C55E)

f_refresh_plan_display() =>
    if planActive and not na(entryLevelLabel)
        int x = bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float offset = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, safeAtr * 0.008)
        int qualityPercent = planScoreMax > 0 ? int(math.round(float(planScore) * 100.0 / float(planScoreMax))) : 60
        string qualityCaption = (planQualityTier == 3 ? "قوية " : planQualityTier == 1 ? "محافظة " : "جيدة ") + str.tostring(qualityPercent) + "%"
        string typeCaption = planEntrySubKind == 9 ? (planEntryKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK ? (planDirection == 1 ? "اختراق مستوى فني" : "كسر مستوى فني") : "ارتداد مستوى فني") : f_entry_type_caption(planDirection == 1, planEntryKind)
        string entryCaption = (planDirection == 1 ? "دخول شراء | " : "دخول بيع | ") + typeCaption + " | " + qualityCaption + (planHtfOpposed ? " | HTF معارض " : " ")
        f_update_plan_level_label(entryLevelLabel, x, planEntry, offset, entryCaption, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor)
        f_update_plan_level_label(stopLevelLabel, x + 2, planStop, offset, stopUsesTouch ? "وقف لمس " : "وقف إغلاق ", #F23645)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target1LevelLabel, x + 4, planTarget1, offset, "هدف 1" + (target1Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target2LevelLabel, x + 6, planTarget2, offset, "هدف 2" + (target2Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target3LevelLabel, x + 8, planTarget3, offset, "هدف 3" + (target3Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
    true
f_refresh_plan_display()

// =============================================================================
// Compact display — analysis and trade-management calculations remain unchanged
// =============================================================================
// =============================================================================
// Monitoring cards use legacy candle-low/high placement; executable entries appear only after valid stop/targets.
// Full trade lines remain gated by valid stop/targets.
// A new card is identified by direction, event type, subtype, event time and reference level.
int preferredTechnicalOpportunity = longTechnicalOpportunity and (not shortTechnicalOpportunity or bullishEvidencePercent >= bearishEvidencePercent + 2.0) ? 1 : shortTechnicalOpportunity and (not longTechnicalOpportunity or bearishEvidencePercent >= bullishEvidencePercent + 2.0) ? -1 : 0

// One episode = one formation point and at most one upgraded confirmation signal.
// Labels use bar_index + price coordinates, so they remain attached to the candle
// when the chart is panned or zoomed.
f_add_opportunity_stage_visual(array<label> ids, bool isLong, bool confirmedStage, int kind, float evidencePercent, int cap) =>
    color pointColor = isLong ? #2196F3 : #F59E0B
    color signalColor = isLong ? confirmedLongEntryColor : sellOpportunityColor
    float gap = math.max(safeAtr * (confirmedStage ? 1.05 : 0.40), syminfo.mintick * (confirmedStage ? 14.0 : 8.0))
    float y = isLong ? low - gap : high + gap
    string side = isLong ? "شراء" : shortTradesAllowed ? "بيع" : "هبوط"
    int ownAxes = isLong ? bullishEvidenceAxes : bearishEvidenceAxes
    int opposingAxes = isLong ? bearishEvidenceAxes : bullishEvidenceAxes
    string axesText = str.tostring(ownAxes) + ">" + str.tostring(opposingAxes)
    string signalText = confirmedStage ? side + " " + axesText + " • " + str.tostring(int(math.round(evidencePercent))) + "%" : kind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY ? "↺" : "⬤"
    string formationTip = kind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY ? (isLong ? "تكوّن فرصة انعكاس صاعد" : shortTradesAllowed ? "تكوّن فرصة انعكاس هابط" : "تكوّن انعكاس هابط") : (isLong ? "بداية تكوّن فرصة شراء" : shortTradesAllowed ? "بداية تكوّن فرصة بيع" : "بداية تكوّن هبوط فني")
    label id = label.new(bar_index, y, signalText, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = confirmedStage ? (isLong ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down) : label.style_none, color = confirmedStage ? color.new(signalColor, 24) : color.new(pointColor, 100), textcolor = confirmedStage ? color.white : pointColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_center, tooltip = confirmedStage ? side + " مؤكد " + str.tostring(int(math.round(evidencePercent))) + "%\nمحاور المدارس " + axesText + "\n" + f_entry_type_caption(isLong, kind) : formationTip, force_overlay = true)
    array.push(ids, id)
    while array.size(ids) > cap
        label.delete(array.shift(ids))
    id

int preferredFormationDirection = longFormationOpportunity and (not shortFormationOpportunity or bullishEvidencePercent >= bearishEvidencePercent + 1.0) ? 1 : shortFormationOpportunity and (not longFormationOpportunity or bearishEvidencePercent >= bullishEvidencePercent + 1.5) ? -1 : 0
bool longFormationNow = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and preferredFormationDirection == 1
bool shortFormationNow = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and preferredFormationDirection == -1

f_update_opportunity_episode(array<label> ids, bool isLong, bool formationNow, bool technicalNow, int dominantTechnicalDirection, int requestedDirection, int kind, float evidencePercent, int evidenceAxes, bool episodeActive, bool confirmationShown, int missBars, int startBar, float startEvidence, int startAxes, int cap) =>
    bool nextActive = episodeActive
    bool nextShown = confirmationShown
    int nextMissBars = missBars
    int nextStartBar = startBar
    float nextStartEvidence = startEvidence
    int nextStartAxes = startAxes
    if barstate.isconfirmed
        if planActive or (isLong ? bullishEvidenceAxes <= bearishEvidenceAxes : bearishEvidenceAxes <= bullishEvidenceAxes)
            // The monitoring card is replaced by the full plan, or removed immediately when its school-axis majority is lost.
            // No active opportunity is lost; its executable geometry remains visible.
            f_clear_label_array(ids)
            nextActive := false
            nextShown := false
            nextMissBars := 0
            nextStartBar := na
            nextStartEvidence := na
            nextStartAxes := 0
        else
            if formationNow
                nextMissBars := 0
                if not nextActive
                    f_clear_label_array(ids)
                    nextActive := true
                    nextShown := false
                    nextStartBar := bar_index
                    nextStartEvidence := evidencePercent
                    nextStartAxes := evidenceAxes
                    f_add_opportunity_stage_visual(ids, isLong, false, kind, evidencePercent, cap)
            else
                nextMissBars := math.min(2, nextMissBars + 1)
                if nextMissBars >= 2
                    f_clear_label_array(ids)
                    nextActive := false
                    nextShown := false
                    nextStartBar := na
                    nextStartEvidence := na
                    nextStartAxes := 0
            bool monitoringThresholdCrossed = nz(nextStartEvidence, evidencePercent) < monitoringEvidencePercent and evidencePercent >= monitoringEvidencePercent
            int direction = isLong ? 1 : -1
            bool confirmationPersistence = technicalNow and (technicalNow[1] or requestedDirection == direction or evidenceAxes >= 3 and evidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent)
            bool confirmationUpgrade = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and nextActive and not nextShown and confirmationPersistence and dominantTechnicalDirection == direction and not na(nextStartBar) and bar_index > nextStartBar and (monitoringThresholdCrossed or evidencePercent >= nz(nextStartEvidence, evidencePercent) + confirmationUpgradePercent or evidenceAxes > nextStartAxes or requestedDirection == direction)
            if confirmationUpgrade
                f_add_opportunity_stage_visual(ids, isLong, true, kind, evidencePercent, cap)
                nextShown := true
    [nextActive, nextShown, nextMissBars, nextStartBar, nextStartEvidence, nextStartAxes]

var array<label> longOpportunityStageVisuals = array.new<label>()
var array<label> shortOpportunityStageVisuals = array.new<label>()
var bool longFormationEpisodeActive = false
var bool shortFormationEpisodeActive = false
var bool longConfirmationShown = false
var bool shortConfirmationShown = false
var int longFormationMissBars = 0
var int shortFormationMissBars = 0
var int longFormationStartBar = na
var int shortFormationStartBar = na
var float longFormationStartEvidence = na
var float shortFormationStartEvidence = na
var int longFormationStartAxes = 0
var int shortFormationStartAxes = 0
int opportunityStageVisualCap = 2

[nextLongEpisodeActive, nextLongConfirmationShown, nextLongMissBars, nextLongStartBar, nextLongStartEvidence, nextLongStartAxes] = f_update_opportunity_episode(longOpportunityStageVisuals, true, longFormationNow, longTechnicalOpportunity, preferredTechnicalOpportunity, requestedPlanDirection, longTechnicalOpportunityKind, bullishEvidencePercent, bullishEvidenceAxes, longFormationEpisodeActive, longConfirmationShown, longFormationMissBars, longFormationStartBar, longFormationStartEvidence, longFormationStartAxes, opportunityStageVisualCap)
longFormationEpisodeActive := nextLongEpisodeActive
longConfirmationShown := nextLongConfirmationShown
longFormationMissBars := nextLongMissBars
longFormationStartBar := nextLongStartBar
longFormationStartEvidence := nextLongStartEvidence
longFormationStartAxes := nextLongStartAxes
[nextShortEpisodeActive, nextShortConfirmationShown, nextShortMissBars, nextShortStartBar, nextShortStartEvidence, nextShortStartAxes] = f_update_opportunity_episode(shortOpportunityStageVisuals, false, shortFormationNow, shortTechnicalOpportunity, preferredTechnicalOpportunity, requestedPlanDirection, shortTechnicalOpportunityKind, bearishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidenceAxes, shortFormationEpisodeActive, shortConfirmationShown, shortFormationMissBars, shortFormationStartBar, shortFormationStartEvidence, shortFormationStartAxes, opportunityStageVisualCap)
shortFormationEpisodeActive := nextShortEpisodeActive
shortConfirmationShown := nextShortConfirmationShown
shortFormationMissBars := nextShortMissBars
shortFormationStartBar := nextShortStartBar
shortFormationStartEvidence := nextShortStartEvidence
shortFormationStartAxes := nextShortStartAxes

f_rtl(string value) =>
    "‏" + str.replace_all(value, "\n", "\n‏")

f_gate_caption(int code, bool longSide) =>
    code == 0 ? "لا حدث فني" : code == 1 ? "تكوّن؛ ينتظر زيادة الأدلة" : code == 2 ? "فرصة قائمة؛ تنتظر إغلاق تأكيد" : code == 3 ? "تعارض هيكل/SR؛ تحت المراقبة" : code == 4 ? "الفرصة تنتظر مشاركة/حجم أفضل" : code == 5 ? "مراقبة؛ شروط الدخول لم تكتمل" : code == 6 ? (longSide ? "شراء مكتمل فنيًا" : "بيع مكتمل فنيًا") : code == 7 ? "كسر وسط نطاق؛ ينتظر حافة/Retest" : code == 8 ? "لا مطاردة؛ ينتظر تصحيح + إعادة تأكيد" : code == 9 ? "HTF معارض؛ يلزم تأكيد أقوى" : code == 10 ? "الكسر قائم؛ ينتظر شمعة أقوى" : code == 11 ? "الكسر قائم؛ المشاركة ضعيفة" : code == 12 ? "↺ تكوّن انعكاس؛ ينتظر التأكيد/Retest" : code == 13 ? "DMI معاكس قوي؛ يمنع الخطة" : "شرط جودة لم يكتمل"

f_refresh_analysis_summary(table summaryTable) =>
    if barstate.islast
        int d = bullishEvidencePercent >= monitoringEvidencePercent and bullishEvidencePercent > bearishEvidencePercent ? 1 : bearishEvidencePercent >= monitoringEvidencePercent and bearishEvidencePercent > bullishEvidencePercent ? -1 : resolvedStructureBias
        int p = int(math.round(d == 1 ? bullishEvidencePercent : d == -1 ? bearishEvidencePercent : math.max(bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent)))
        int fav = d == 1 ? bullishEvidenceAxes : bearishEvidenceAxes
        int opp = d == 1 ? bearishEvidenceAxes : bullishEvidenceAxes
        color dc = d == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : d == -1 ? sellOpportunityColor : color.silver
        string opinion = f_market_regime_caption(marketRegimeCode, rangeMiddleRegime) + " | " + (d == 1 ? "شراء " : d == -1 ? (shortTradesAllowed ? "بيع " : "هبوط ") : "محايد ") + str.tostring(p) + "%"
        string schools = d == 0 ? "لا أغلبية" : "المحاور " + str.tostring(fav) + ">" + str.tostring(opp)
        string htf = higherTimeframe1 + ":" + f_htf_state_caption(higherTrendRaw1) + " • " + higherTimeframe2 + ":" + f_htf_state_caption(higherTrendRaw2) + " • " + higherTimeframe3 + ":" + f_htf_state_caption(higherTrendRaw3)
        int diagnosticDirection = preferredTechnicalOpportunity != 0 ? preferredTechnicalOpportunity : bullishEvidencePercent >= bearishEvidencePercent ? 1 : -1
        int diagnosticCode = diagnosticDirection == 1 ? longDecisionBlockCode : shortDecisionBlockCode
        string planText = planActive ? ((planDirection == 1 ? "خطة شراء فعالة" : "خطة بيع فعالة") + (planHtfOpposed ? " | HTF معارض" : "") + (majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == -planDirection ? " | عكس الرئيسي" : "")) : requestedPlanDirection != 0 and not candidatePlanValid ? (requestedPlanHtfOpposed ? "مؤهل بحذر؛ HTF معارض والهندسة منعته" : "الدخول مؤهل؛ الهندسة منعته") : f_gate_caption(diagnosticCode, diagnosticDirection == 1)
        for r = 0 to 3
            string title = r == 0 ? "الرأي" : r == 1 ? "المدارس" : r == 2 ? "الفواصل" : "الفرصة"
            string value = r == 0 ? opinion : r == 1 ? schools : r == 2 ? htf : planText
            color vc = r == 0 ? dc : r == 3 and diagnosticCode >= 3 and diagnosticCode <= 4 ? warningColor : color.white
            table.cell(summaryTable, 0, r, f_rtl(value), width = 22, text_color = vc, bgcolor = color.new(color.black, 86), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right)
            table.cell(summaryTable, 1, r, f_rtl(title), width = 10, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 70), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right)
    true

var table analysisSummaryTable = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 4, bgcolor = color.new(color.black, 84), frame_color = color.new(color.gray, 45), frame_width = 1, border_color = color.new(color.gray, 76), border_width = 1)
f_refresh_analysis_summary(analysisSummaryTable)

f_refresh_plan_event_markers(label currentEntryMarker, label currentOutcomeMarker) =>
    label resultEntryMarker = currentEntryMarker
    label resultOutcomeMarker = currentOutcomeMarker
    if chartSignalDisplayAllowed
        if newPlanCreatedEvent
            int markerPercent = planScoreMax > 0 ? int(math.round(float(planScore) * 100.0 / float(planScoreMax))) : 60
            string markerAxes = str.tostring(planDirection == 1 ? bullishEvidenceAxes : bearishEvidenceAxes) + ">" + str.tostring(planDirection == 1 ? bearishEvidenceAxes : bullishEvidenceAxes)
            resultEntryMarker := f_replace_chart_marker(resultEntryMarker, bar_index, planEntry, (planDirection == 1 ? "دخول شراء " : "دخول بيع ") + markerAxes + " | Q" + str.tostring(markerPercent) + "/100", planDirection == 1, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, safeAtr, 1.78)
        if planEndedThisBar and not na(planOutcomePrice)
            bool outcomeBelow = planOutcomeKind == 3 ? planDirection == -1 : planDirection == 1
            string outcomeText = planOutcomeKind == 1 ? "وقف" : planOutcomeKind == 3 ? "هدف 3" : "ملتبسة"
            color outcomeColor = planOutcomeKind == 3 ? #22C55E : planOutcomeKind == 1 ? #F23645 : #F59E0B
            resultOutcomeMarker := f_replace_chart_marker(resultOutcomeMarker, bar_index, planOutcomePrice, outcomeText, outcomeBelow, outcomeColor, safeAtr, 1.40)
    [resultEntryMarker, resultOutcomeMarker]

[nextEntryMarker, nextOutcomeMarker] = f_refresh_plan_event_markers(activePlanEntryMarker, activePlanOutcomeMarker)
activePlanEntryMarker := nextEntryMarker
activePlanOutcomeMarker := nextOutcomeMarker
